### [1] [Approaching Landauer's limit in a single superconducting vortex memory bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37753)
*Nofar Fridman,Itai Keren,Avia Noah,Yishay Zur,Martin E. Huber,Nadav Katz,Roland Willa,Hadar Steinberg,Yonathan Anahory*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用纳米级双阱钉扎势中的超导涡旋来操纵存储比特所需的能量，并通过实验与数值分析表明该比特擦除所需的功与朗道尔极限（k_B T ln 2）处于同一数量级，为高效低温存储器件提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算行业能源消耗日益增长，亟需开发更低能耗的存储器件。作者希望探索利用超导涡旋在双阱势中的运动来实现高效比特操纵，并定量评估其能量代价是否接近朗道尔极限。

Method: 实验上，对多种双阱系统施加正弦驱动力，分析驱动力幅值与频率的影响；数值上，模拟比特擦除过程并计算所需功，与朗道尔极限（k_B T ln 2，4.2 K下）进行对比。实验采用稳态正弦驱动（与朗道尔擦除协议不兼容），故借助数值分析评估擦除功。

Result: 弱驱动下表现出特征性的随机共振行为；强低频驱动下可实现绝热式操纵。数值分析表明，比特操纵（擦除）所需功与朗道尔极限处于同一数量级。

Conclusion: 这些发现为构建高效存储元件提供了可能性，此类元件可与低温量子电路集成，或用作拓扑量子计算的量子比特，从而降低存储器件能耗，缓解计算行业日益增长的全球能源需求。

Abstract: This work investigates the energy required to manipulate a memory bit using a superconducting vortex confined in a nanoscale double-well pinning potential. In the experiments, various double-well systems subjected to sinusoidal forces is explored, and the effects of both driving force and frequency is analyzed. Results reveal characteristic stochastic resonance behavior for weak drives and adiabatic manipulation at strong, low-frequency drives. Although our experimental method requires a steady sinusoidal drive incompatible with Landauer's erasure protocol, our numerical analysis demonstrates that the work required for such bit manipulation (erasure) is within an order of magnitude of Landauer's limit ($k_B T \ln 2$) at 4.2 K. These findings open possibilities for creating highly efficient memory elements that could be integrated with cryogenic quantum circuits or function as topological quantum computing qubits; thereby contributing to memory devices with lower energy consumption and mitigating the escalating global energy demand in the computing industry.

</details>


### [2] [Finite-step statistical mechanics of scale-invariant learning: spectral equations of state and curvature transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38012)
*Alexander Itin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络随机梯度动力学中看似热力学的关系，证明这些关系源于尺度对称性而非热平衡，并给出超越谐波近似的精确曲率输运恒等式来量化偏离理想气体描述的非理想涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在限制性噪声假设下，平稳学习可用理想气体描述，但在梯度噪声偏离这些假设的神经网络中，类似Maxwell关系的熵标度关系仍近似成立。作者希望厘清：哪些热力学式样的关系来自尺度对称性而非平衡态，以及如何为带记忆的离散随机梯度动力学量化偏离理想气体描述的程度。

Method: 利用轨迹的精确重标度解释熵关系（无需假设热平衡）；在局域谐波近似下，通过自调节权值范数导出谱状态方程，其维里系数由曲率谱的矩确定，且耦合参数组可维持稳态概率流；核心结果是超出谐波近似的精确曲率输运恒等式，并结合有限步损失平衡思想，将谐波涨落关系的修正分解为可测量的曲率变化、有限步余项、梯度-记忆关联项和有限窗口漂移项；进一步用位移加权曲率变化作为时间之箭的诊断量，其均值与二阶矩给出正反向状态对之间相对熵的下界；并计算了高斯输入下整流教师-学生网络激活边界对曲率的贡献。

Result: 在球模型、教师-学生网络以及CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100上的卷积网络实验中，输运描述得到支持，同时识别出谐波与三次余项近似的适用界限。结果将基于对称性的关系与平衡态假设区分开来，并提供了学习动力学中非理想涨落的定量诊断手段。

Conclusion: 本文将尺度对称性导致的类热力学关系与平衡态假设分离，提出精确曲率输运恒等式作为核心工具，能够将非理想涨落的修正分解为可测量贡献项，并通过位移加权曲率变化诊断时间之箭。实验验证了输运描述的有效性并界定了谐波及三次余项近似的适用范围。

Abstract: Stationary learning admits an ideal-gas description under restrictive noise assumptions. In neural networks whose gradient noise departs from these assumptions, however, an entropy-scaling relation resembling a Maxwell relation remains approximately valid. We ask which thermodynamic-looking relations follow from scale symmetry rather than equilibrium, and how departures from the ideal-gas description can be quantified, for discrete stochastic gradient dynamics with memory. An exact rescaling of trajectories explains the entropy relation without assuming thermal equilibrium. Within a local harmonic approximation, self-adjusting weight norms yield a spectral equation of state whose virial coefficients are set by moments of the curvature spectrum, and coupled parameter groups can sustain stationary probability currents. Our central result beyond this approximation is an exact curvature-transport identity. Together with a finite-step loss balance, it decomposes corrections to the harmonic fluctuation relation into measurable curvature changes, finite-step remainders, gradient-memory correlations and finite-window drift terms. A related displacement-weighted curvature change provides a diagnostic of the arrow of time; its mean and second moment give a lower bound on the relative entropy between forward and reversed state pairs. We also calculate the activation-boundary contribution to curvature for rectified teacher-student networks with Gaussian inputs. Tests in sphere models, teacher-student networks and convolutional networks on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 support the transport description and identify limits of the harmonic and cubic-remainder approximations. These results separate symmetry-based relations from equilibrium assumptions and provide quantitative diagnostics of nonideal fluctuations in learning dynamics.

</details>


### [3] [Far Tails of the Positional Distribution in random walk: A Saddle-Point Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38492)
*Chen Zhou,YuDa Cheng,Yangji Li,Jing Xu,Wanli Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用鞍点法研究连续时间随机游走在普通与平衡初始条件下位置分布的稀有事件，发现远尾行为对等待时间分布在最小值的展开形式敏感：一般幂级数展开导致近似指数衰减，而在最小值处有截断时则出现意外的高斯衰减；最可能更新次数及相关率函数对初始条件不敏感，但位置分布的远尾仍依赖初始条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有事件与大偏差理论在随机游走研究中备受关注，但连续时间随机游走位置分布远尾行为与等待时间分布局部结构（尤其是最小值附近展开形式）及初始条件之间的关系尚未被充分厘清。

Method: 采用鞍点方法（大偏差方法），分别考虑普通初始条件与平衡初始条件，分析等待时间分布在最小值附近的一般幂级数展开与在最小值处截断两种情况，推导位置分布的远尾渐近行为，并计算最可能更新次数与相应率函数。

Result: 当等待时间分布在最小值附近为一般幂级数展开时，位置分布远尾呈近似指数衰减；当等待时间分布在最小值处有截断时，位置分布远尾呈意外的高斯衰减，这与更新计数分布的远尾行为形成对比。此外，达到给定位置所需的最可能更新次数及相关率函数对初始条件不敏感，而位置分布的远尾仍保留对初始条件的依赖。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了等待时间分布最小值附近的细节（幂级数展开与截断）及初始条件对连续时间随机游走位置分布稀有事件统计的显著影响，为随机游走的稀有事件统计与大偏差理论提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Rare events and large-deviation theory have attracted considerable interest in the study of random walks. Here, we investigate the rare events of the positional distribution of continuous-time random walks under ordinary and equilibrium initial conditions using the saddle point method. We show that the far-tail behavior is sensitive to the expansion of the waiting-time distribution near its minimum value. For a general power-series expansion near the minimum waiting time, the positional distribution exhibits an approximately exponential decay in its far tails. In contrast, when the waiting-time distribution has a cutoff at its minimum value, the positional distribution exhibits an unexpected Gaussian decay, in contrast to the far-tail behavior of the renewal-count distribution. We further find that the most likely number of renewals required to reach a given position, as well as the associated rate functions, is insensitive to the initial conditions, whereas the far tails of the positional distribution retain a dependence on the initial conditions. Our results provide new insights into the rare-event statistics and large deviations of random walks.

</details>


### [4] [Swimming versus Screening: How Activity Reshapes the Ionic Cloud of a Charged Particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38594)
*Tanmoy Chakraborty,Tomer Markovich,Ram M. Adar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究活性运动如何改变带电游动体在电解质中的库仑相互作用，发现持续运动会改变稳态离子电荷密度分布，产生活性诱导的欠屏蔽效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性物质领域的一个核心问题是活性如何改变粒子间的相互作用。本文针对库仑相互作用这一基本问题，探究带电游动体在电解质中时，其持续运动如何改变传统的静电屏蔽图像。

Method: 考虑浸没在电解质中的带电游动体，分析其持续运动对稳态离子电荷密度分布的影响。在低活性极限下，游动体对离子近似准静态，恢复传统 Debye-Hückel 屏蔽（指数衰减，屏蔽长度为 $λ_D$）。在较高活性下，分析游动体的弹道-扩散动力学所对应的不同空间区域，推导弹道区、中间区和远场区的电荷密度衰减形式。

Result: 低活性下恢复标准 Debye-Hückel 屏蔽。高活性下出现不同的空间区域：先出现幂律衰减，中间距离出现拉伸指数衰减，足够远处恢复指数屏蔽，但屏蔽长度增大为 $λ>λ_D$，即活性诱导的欠屏蔽（underscreening）。活性运动同时改变了静电屏蔽的空间形式和屏蔽范围。

Conclusion: 活性运动不仅改变静电屏蔽的空间形式，还增大其屏蔽范围，产生欠屏蔽效应。这一结果推动了对由正、负带电游动体组成的活性电解质的研究。

Abstract: A major question in active matter is how interactions between particles are modified by activity. Here, we address this question for Coulomb interactions by considering a charged swimmer immersed in an electrolyte. A static charge is screened by an ionic cloud that decays over the Debye length $λ_D$. We show that the persistent motion of the swimmer can qualitatively modify the steady-state ionic charge-density profile. At low activity, the swimmer appears effectively quasistatic to the ions, and conventional Debye-Hückel screening is recovered. At higher activity, the ballistic-to-diffusive dynamics of the swimmer are reflected in distinct spatial regimes. A power-law decay is followed by a stretched-exponential decay at intermediate distances. At sufficiently large distances, exponential screening is restored, but with an increased screening length, $λ>λ_D$, revealing activity-induced underscreening. Thus, active motion modifies both the spatial form and the range of electrostatic screening. Our results motivate the study of active electrolytes composed of positively and negatively charged swimmers.

</details>


### [5] [Information-Theoretic Reliability--Safety Bounds and Finite-Alignment Dynamics in AI Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38651)
*Miao-Miao Yi,Zhen-Yu Cai,Y -M. Du,C. P. Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度建立了AI可靠性与安全性之间的权衡界限，并用玻尔兹曼机模型推导出精确关系；进一步研究知识蒸馏中教师响应如何重整化学生模型参数，并用重整化群方法分析多代蒸馏的定点结构与临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI的可靠性与安全性及其在知识蒸馏中的传递与演化日益重要，但两者之间的内在权衡关系及其在多代蒸馏下的动力学规律缺乏理论刻画，需要建立统一的定量框架。

Method: 作者首先证明一个与模型形式无关的通用权衡不等式，其边界由隐蔽层区分良性/危险输入的判别能力 Kh 单一参数决定；随后构建规范玻尔兹曼机模型描述AI响应行为，推导可靠性与安全性的两条精确关系式；在知识蒸馏中，将教师响应对学生模型参数的影响视为重整化，并采用重整化群方法研究多代蒸馏，推导两种代表性代际蒸馏协议的精确递推关系与流方程（任意师生对齐强度下）。

Result: 权衡曲线由危险判别容量 K 决定，整体答案倾向控制曲线上的工作点，理论关系与现有实验数据吻合良好；多代蒸馏的重整化群流揭示了定点结构、长期动力学与临界行为，并给出能力损失与稳定传递之间临界转变的可检验预测。

Conclusion: 可靠性与安全性之间存在由判别容量单一参数决定的普适权衡，知识蒸馏可视为对权衡参数的重整化，且多代蒸馏展现出临界相变行为，为AI安全传递提供了理论预测工具。

Abstract: The reliability and safety of artificial intelligence (AI), together with their transfer and evolution through knowledge distillation, have attracted growing attention. We establish an information-theoretic bound on the reliability--safety trade-off and characterize the dynamics of this trade-off under knowledge distillation. We first prove a universal trade-off inequality that is independent of the specific model form. Its boundary is determined solely by a single parameter Kh, which quantifies the hidden layer's ability to distinguish benign from hazardous inputs. We then develop a canonical Boltzmann-machine model of AI response behavior and derive two exact relations between reliability and safety. The trade-off curve is determined by the hazard discrimination capacity K, while the overall answer tendency controls the operating point along the curve. These relations agree well with existing experimental data. In knowledge distillation, we find that teacher responses induce a renormalization of the student model's trade-off parameters. We further use a renormalization-group approach to study multigenerational distillation, deriving exact recursion relations and the associated renormalization-group-like flows for two representative direct intergenerational distillation protocols at arbitrary teacher--student alignment strength. These flows reveal fixed-point structures, long-term dynamics, and critical behavior, yielding testable predictions for the critical transition between capability loss and stable transmission.

</details>


### [6] [Finite-time minimum-work protocols for underdamped traps driven by active forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38653)
*Kangcan Yao,Ruicheng Bao,Zhonghuai Hou*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为欠阻尼活性粒子设计了最小功（即最大功提取）协议，发现最小平均功及其涨落与质量无关，但惯性会改变协议本身；并进一步应用于欠阻尼活性 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 粒子（AOUP），给出显式功提取判据，揭示提取功对转动噪声与初始条件的依赖，且惯性会强烈影响最优协议的可实现性，呈现过度阻尼到欠阻尼之间的转变型变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性系统能提供非平衡能量，原则上可用控制协议加以收集，但在微观热力学中，惯性与活性如何联合影响最优功提取协议、最大可提取功及其涨落、以及协议的可实现性仍不清楚，这正是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 以一个位于平动谐波势阱中的欠阻尼活性粒子为模型，设计最小功（工作提取最大化的最优可提取功）协议；随后将该结果应用于欠阻尼活性 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 粒子（AOUP），构造显式的功提取判据，并分析提取功对旋转噪声和初始条件的依赖；同时比较欠阻尼与过阻尼最优协议在实现难度上的差异。

Result: 最小平均功及其涨落均与质量无关，尽管惯性会改变最优协议的具体形式；在 AOUP 情形下给出了显式功提取判据，并揭示了提取功对转动噪声和初始条件的依赖规律；惯性强烈影响最优协议的可实现性，并导致欠阻尼与过阻尼最优协议之间出现转变型（transition-like）的变化。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了微观尺度下的功提取机制，并为设计可靠的能量收集微观器件与机器人提供指导。

Abstract: Active systems provide non-equilibrium energy that can, in principle, be harvested using control protocols, making the optimization and reliability of work extraction a fundamental problem in microscopic thermodynamics. Yet how inertia and activity jointly modify the optimal work extraction protocol, the maximum extractable work its fluctuations, and implementability of protocols remains unclear. Here, we design minimum-work protocols, which maximize work extraction whenever extraction is possible, for an underdamped active particle in a translated harmonic trap. Remarkably, both the minimum mean work and its fluctuations are independent of mass, although inertia modifies the protocol. The results are then applied to an underdamped active Ornstein-Uhlenbeck particle (AOUP), designing an explicit work-extraction criterion and revealing extracted work's dependence on rotational noise and initial conditions. We further show that inertia strongly affects the difficulty of implementing the optimal protocol, producing a transition-like change between underdamped and overdamped optimal protocols. These findings uncover work extraction mechanisms at the microscale and guide the design of reliable energy-harvesting microscopic devices and robots.

</details>


### [7] [Thermodynamic cost of locally maintaining a steady state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38925)
*Sarang Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现严格局域的遵守细致平衡的高斯过程可以具有任意长程条件相关性（长程条件互信息 CMI）的独特稳态，但这类过程若由局域二次哈密顿量耦合到快速弛豫平衡热库实现，则必然产生熵。作者将熵产生率下界用稳态 CMI 联系起来，并证明每点每弛豫时间的熵产生满足 σ̂ ≥ c ξ²（ξ 为马尔可夫长度），同时刻画了耦合范围与熵产生的权衡，并与离散态跳过程形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域哈密顿量的吉布斯态具有短程条件相关性，直觉上要维持长程条件相关性的稳态，要么需要非局域运动，要么需要熵产生（即打破细致平衡）。本文旨在厘清这一直觉在何种意义上成立、何种意义下会被违反，并定量刻画长程 CMI 与熵产生之间的代价关系。

Method: 作者构造了严格局域、服从细致平衡且具有唯一稳态的高斯过程，证明其稳态可具有任意长程 CMI。进一步考虑这类过程由局域二次哈密顿量局域耦合到快速弛豫的平衡热库实现的情况，利用信息论和热力学工具，将稳态 CMI 与熵产生率联系起来，给出每格点每弛豫时间的熵产生下界 σ̂ ≥ c ξ²（大 ξ 极限），其中 ξ 为 CMI 指数衰减对应的马尔可夫长度，c 为依赖实现方式的常数；同时导出耦合范围与熵产生的权衡。最后与离散状态空间跳过程对比，指出在不可约性假设下，长程 CMI 稳态只可能在违背细致平衡时出现，并提出快速混合局域跳过程无法稳定长程 CMI 稳态的猜想及支持证据。

Result: 1) 存在严格局域、满足细致平衡且唯一稳态具有任意长程 CMI 的高斯过程，说明"长程 CMI 需要非局域移动或熵产生"的直觉对高斯情形不成立；2) 但这些过程若以局域二次哈密顿量局域耦合到快速弛豫平衡热库实现，仍必须消耗熵，且熵产生下界为 σ̂ ≥ c ξ²（大 ξ），表明长程 CMI 以二次方熵产生代价购买；3) 给出了耦合范围与熵产生之间的权衡界；4) 对于离散状态空间的跳过程，在不可约条件下长程 CMI 稳态必须违背细致平衡，并猜想快速混合的局域跳过程无法稳定长程 CMI 稳态。

Conclusion: 长程条件相关性并不必然要求非局域动力学或熵产生——严格局域且细致平衡的高斯过程即可实现。然而，当这类高斯过程通过局域二次哈密顿量局域耦合到快速弛豫平衡热库实现时，长程 CMI 必须付出熵产生的代价，其下界以马尔可夫长度的平方增长（σ̂ ≥ c ξ²）。这与离散态跳过程形成鲜明对比：后者在不可约性条件下，长程 CMI 稳态必然违背细致平衡。作者推测快速混合局域跳过程无法稳定长程 CMI 稳态，并给出了支持证据。

Abstract: The Gibbs states of local Hamiltonians have short-range conditional correlations. To maintain a steady state with long-range conditional correlations, one might expect that a stochastic process must ``pay'' either in nonlocal moves or entropy production. Surprisingly, we find strictly local Gaussian processes that obey detailed balance and have unique steady states with arbitrarily long-range conditional correlations. We show that such processes must still produce entropy, if implemented using a local quadratic Hamiltonian coupled locally to equilibrium, rapidly relaxing baths. We lower-bound this entropy production in terms of the steady state's conditional mutual information (CMI); if the CMI decays exponentially with distance, the associated length-scale is called the Markov length $ξ$. The entropy production per site per relaxation time obeys $\hatσ\ge c ξ^2$ for large $ξ$, and with $c$ an implementation-dependent constant. We also bound the tradeoff between coupling range and entropy production. We contrast these Gaussian processes with jump processes on discrete state spaces, for which, under certain irreducibility assumptions, steady states with long-range CMI are only possible if detailed balance is violated. We conjecture (and present evidence) that rapidly mixing local jump processes cannot stabilize steady states with long-range CMI.

</details>

Abstract: Rotary molecular motors commonly couple components with different rotational symmetries, but how this gearing controls energy transduction remains unclear. Here we show that symmetry can produce a fundamental tradeoff in coupled stochastic barrierless rotary motors: the gear ratio that maximizes output power under tight coupling is also the ratio that requires the strongest coupling to remain locked. Consequently, weakening the coupling can invert the effect of gearing, transforming a power maximum into a local minimum. At strong driving, both the power-optimal and most slip-susceptible ratios approach symmetry match. Numerical solutions of the Fokker-Planck dynamics for rotors with finite energy barriers reproduce these predictions and reveal how the behavior changes when driving strength is fixed per rotation or per subunit. Motivated by the bacterial flagellar motor, these results identify coupling strength as a key determinant of whether matched rotational symmetries enhance or suppress energy transduction, providing a general design principle for coupled molecular motors.

Abstract: We apply the Constructive Cavity Method (CCM) to the Ising perceptron. The process starts from positive soft channels admitting an auxiliary representation $$ U(x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}} e^{tx}ρ(\mathrm{d}t),\qquad ρ\ge0, $$ with an auxiliary measure having a finite exponential quadratic moment. This rewrites the perceptron as a dense, bipartite Gaussian model with an Ising layer and a continuous auxiliary layer. The CCM blanket geometry then gives two constructive variance profiles, $Q_\ell$ on the Ising side and $Γ_\ell$ on the auxiliary side. The bipartite shell scale is $$ Q_{\ell+1}Γ_{\ell+1}-Q_\ellΓ_\ell, $$ and Gaussian core comparison removes the core contribution $ΔQ_\ellΔΓ_\ell$ under blanket refinement. The scalar cavity functional has three terms, $$ \mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{perc}}(ν)=\mathbb{E}\left[\log\langle2\cosh x\rangle_ν+α\log\langle U(s)\rangle_ν-\log\langle e^y\rangle_ν\right], $$ where $x$ is the new-spin field, $s$ is the new-constraint field, and $y$ is the cavity correction. CCM identifies the constructive cavity variables with the Aizenman--Sims--Starr random overlap structure (ROSt) variables. Parisi's recursive averaging then gives $$ \mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{perc}}^{\mathrm{CCM}}(ζ,Q,Γ)=X_0(0)+αY_0(0)-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{R}ζ_\ell\left(Q_{\ell+1}Γ_{\ell+1}-Q_\ellΓ_\ell\right). $$ The replica-symmetric specialization gives Gardner's formula for the generalized Ising perceptron. Specializing the constraint terminal to the hard channel gives the Krauth--Mézard functional for the binary perceptron.

</details>

Abstract: The renormalization-group functions $β$, $η$ and $ν$ of the three-dimensional $O(N)$ model in fixed dimension (massive zero-momentum scheme) were known to eighth order in the coupling. We extend them to tenth order, and $β$ to eleventh, for general $N$, which requires the two- and four-point functions to ten loops (1,593,254 graphs at the last order), and re-evaluate all lower orders. Through the Callan-Symanzik equation every coefficient follows from per-order sums that are linear in finite, zero-momentum $\bar R$-subtracted parametric integrals, one per graph, which we sample on GPUs by tropical Monte Carlo with densities adapted to the forest terms. The computation reproduces the known sums from two to seven loops within three standard deviations and agrees at ninth and tenth order, at lower precision, with an independent route through an intermediate scheme. The new coefficients have relative uncertainties of a few $10^{-5}$ or better, and the eighth-order coefficients of $η$ and $ν$ improve by factors of 120 and 4. The time needed to integrate an order to fixed relative precision grows by only 2.4-2.9 per loop from seven loops on, while the construction of the integrands still grows factorially and, at tenth order, costs more than the integration. Resummed, the tenth-order series give $ν=0.62997(51)$, $η=0.0333(18)$ and $ω=0.803(14)$ for $N=1$, against $0.62997097(12)$, $0.036297612(48)$ and $0.82968(23)$ from the conformal bootstrap; at $N=3$ the deviations grow, so that the uncertainty is now dominated by the resummation rather than by the coefficients. Every result is generated automatically from, or checked against, a public ledger of independent, exactly pooled runs, hosted on GitHub, to which anyone is welcome to contribute computing time.

</details>

Abstract: Helium scattering measures the coherent intermediate scattering function of an adlayer, the characteristic function of the separation between adparticles at two times. Published dephasing rates of H and D on Pt(111) are analysed with its memory equation and a nearest-neighbour Ising lattice gas. The measured rate belongs to the adlayer. The hop rate of a single adsorbate is extracted from it by dividing out an interaction factor, without a model of the barrier. This factor lies between $0.97$ and $1.17$ at $0.1$~ML, so that the published single-adsorbate analyses hold there. At $0.4$~ML and $80$~K it lies between $1.2$ and $4.7$, depending on how the occupied neighbours modify a tunnelling jump. The memory function is interpreted as a friction, the collective friction, which the adlayer exerts on its own density fluctuations. The lateral coupling, fixed by the coverage series, is the free energy of a neighbouring pair, with a vibrational pair entropy. The small deformation of the measured curves from the Chudley--Elliott form, the tilt, is proposed as an observable of the coupling. The tilts at $0.15$ and $0.2$~ML are consistent with it, although they are hard to extract from the published curves. The memory equation provides a transition-state theory for the adlayer, with the collective friction in the role of the friction of the substrate and without divergence at the crossover. Combined with the standard quantum transition-state theory of a single adsorbate, the extracted hop rates give the friction of the substrate, $124$ and $75$~ps$^{-1}$ for H and D, independent of the coverage.

Abstract: For Gaussian white noise the Itô and Stratonovich prescriptions differ by $O(dt)$ per time step, which accumulates into the familiar spurious drift $\tfrac12 D\,II'$. We show that for renewal shot noise with state-dependent gain, $\dot x=-C(x)-I(x)ξ[t]$ with $ξ[t]$ a train of Dirac spikes of finite random amplitude, the two prescriptions differ by a finite amount at every event, and that where $I$ vanishes the difference can become qualitative. The Marcus kick, the jump-process counterpart of the Stratonovich prescription, is the flow map of $dx/ds=I(x)$: no kick can cross a zero $x^\ast$ of $I$, for any jump and waiting-time statistics. The Itô kick is only the tangent map of that flow. For $I(x)=1+βx$ it crosses $x^\ast=-1/β$ if and only if $ξβ>1$ and collapses the whole line onto $x^\ast$ at $ξβ=1$; below threshold it is itself a Marcus kick with a remapped jump law. Bounded jump laws thus have a threshold, while unbounded ones leak across the barrier for \emph{any} parameters. The discrepancy cannot be absorbed into a renormalised drift beyond lowest order, and only the Marcus kick is generated by a single operator, which makes the master equation of Bianucci, Bologna and Mannella (2026) intrinsically Marcus. Numerically, the standard Heun kick is a second-order truncation of the Marcus map that preserves the barrier only because its order is even; it shortens every jump, biasing second moments in proportion to the squared jump amplitude and the jump kurtosis, and depleting the region near the barrier. We give the exact-flow integrator that replaces it.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 在格点场论的统计采样中，直接对精细格点进行蒙特卡洛采样计算代价高昂。作者希望利用基于分数的扩散模型作为生成式采样器，在S^2目标流形（海森堡自旋的取值空间）上高效生成配置，并通过多尺度/粗粒化方案进一步提升采样精度与效率，同时解决在球面流形上训练扩散模型的若干技术难题。

Imposing traversal directions increases the shortest-path exponent, and imposing time order increases it further; these constraints therefore change the scaling exponent, not only the amplitude. For every path, the standard deviation scales with the same exponent as the mean, so it is scale-invariant, unlike the visited-site mass; the rescaled length distributions collapse onto a finite-width scaling function. This provides a theoretical explanation for the persistence of clonal memory and for experimentally observed clonal cofluctuations between variables with zero covariance in the single-particle stationary state.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The one-dimensional Ising chain with inverse-square interactions is a paradigmatic long-range system: it exhibits the unusual Thouless transition, with a discontinuity in the spontaneous magnetization, yet its full field-dependent thermodynamics remains unknown in closed form. We develop the high-temperature expansion of its free energy while retaining the dependence on a uniform magnetic field at every order. We prove that, after a natural rescaling of the coupling, each coefficient is a rational polynomial in the squared hyperbolic tangent of the reduced field. Remarkably, at each order $n$ the field-dependent free-energy coefficient of a finite, periodically repeated ring is an exact polynomial of degree at most $n$ in $L^{-2}$. The infinite-chain coefficient is its constant term and can therefore be reconstructed from $n+1$ finite rings without numerical extrapolation. Using this reconstruction, we determine the full field-dependent expansion through order 26: 26 polynomials containing 377 rational coefficient entries. We further derive exact formulas, valid at every order, for the coefficients of the two highest powers of the field variable using matching determinants and a cycle-Laplacian reduction. These results reveal an unexpected algebraic structure in the microscopic thermodynamics of the inverse-square chain and provide high-order information about its magnetic response.

Abstract: We introduce two variants of the Blume--Emery--Griffiths model with long-range, mean-field-like interactions and quenched disorder. Although the two variants are described by different Hamiltonians, they yield the same free energy. Moreover, both Hamiltonians lead to the same average energy and hence to the same microcanonical entropy. The canonical and microcanonical phase boundaries differ markedly, and so does the multicritical behaviour of the two ensembles. In the microcanonical ensemble, tricritical points appear for any fraction of active spins, whereas in the canonical ensemble they emerge only when this fraction is sufficiently large. At lower fractions, canonical critical endpoints arise instead. We also report the formation of gaps in the entropy as a function of magnetization, another manifestation of ensemble inequivalence.

A focus of the work is on the evolution of the average internal energy of the quantum subsystem that may be subject to external autonomous driving that does work on the system, and on entropy production in the system. The nonequilibrium statistical thermodynamics the quantum subsystem coupled to reservoirs, as well as that for the isolated subsystem and reservoirs, are discussed. When the subsystem is coupled to large quantum reservoirs, the effects of the quantum subsystem on the reservoirs can be captured through boundary conditions on the reservoir equations, and these boundary conditions are derived. The formulation is illustrated by considering the heat transfer through a system comprising a quantum system coupled to two heat reservoirs with different temperatures.

### [20] [Thermodynamic Crossovers in Supercritical Matter: One, No One, and One Hundred Thousand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39986)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Beyond a critical point, two thermodynamic phases become continuously connected, without being separated by any thermodynamic singularity. The paradigmatic example is the liquid-gas transition, where several criteria have been proposed to distinguish liquid-like and gas-like regimes within the supercritical region. Recently, a pair of thermodynamic crossover lines, $L^\pm$, was introduced to address a proposed incompatibility of previous definitions with the universal critical behavior of the Ising model. These lines divide the supercritical region into three regimes, meet only at the critical point, and obey the expected critical scaling laws. Here, we demonstrate that these requirements do not uniquely determine the crossover boundaries. Instead, infinitely many pairs of lines satisfy the same conditions, with different choices of thermodynamic paths selecting different crossover boundaries. We further show that the Lee-Yang formalism, despite providing an intrinsic characterization of critical singularities, does not resolve this ambiguity in defining real-axis crossover boundaries. Restricting to regular probing paths that respect the $Z_2$ symmetry of the critical point recovers the Widom and $L^\pm$ prescriptions asymptotically. This restriction, however, is a choice of protocol rather than a property of the system, and even then two inequivalent prescriptions survive. Our results demonstrate that universal critical behavior alone does not select privileged supercritical thermodynamic crossover boundaries. While such lines may identify physically meaningful changes in thermodynamic, structural, or transport properties, their precise locations and the widths of the regions they delimit remain dependent on the operational criteria used to define them.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截断弱相互作用是多体哈密顿量的常见简化，但该近似的有效性在距离和时间上未必一致。在混沌系统中，弱长程耦合可在远离源头处种下小扰动，随后局域动力学将其指数放大，从而可能在长距离上改变传播行为。作者旨在系统研究这一机制。

Result: 指数衰减尾巴下：当尾巴衰减足够浅时，长距离前沿保持弹道传播，其速度由种子空间衰减与局域混沌增长之间的竞争决定；当指数衰减足够快时，在可及的时空窗口内恢复由本征蝴蝶速度 $v_b$ 定义的最近邻前沿。幂律尾巴下：对所有研究的指数，到达时间与距离成对数关系，对应指数扩张的空间前沿，且幂律指数控制该对数的系数。

Conclusion: 两类行为均可由"播种-放大"图像解释：长程耦合在整个链上产生弱扰动，其增长主要由局域最近邻动力学支配。尾巴的整体强度主要决定长程前沿何时可见，而空间衰减形式决定传播规律。因此，弱作用尾巴可在不显著改变常规自旋动力学可观测量（如自扩散、局域退相关）的同时，质变地改变长距离退相关传播。

Abstract: Truncating weak interactions is a natural simplification of a many-body Hamiltonian, but the validity of this approximation need not be uniform in distance and time. In a chaotic system, a weak long-range coupling can seed a small disturbance far from its source, after which the local dynamics can amplify that disturbance exponentially. We study this mechanism in an infinite-temperature classical Heisenberg spin chain with a dominant nearest-neighbor exchange and a weak interaction tail. Using a classical decorrelator, we compare exponentially and power-law decaying tails over several decades in interaction strength. For exponentially decaying interactions, the long-distance front remains ballistic when the tail is sufficiently shallow, with a velocity controlled by the competition between the spatial decay of the seed and the local chaotic growth. For sufficiently rapid exponential decay, the nearest-neighbor front, which is defined by an intrinsic butterfly velocity $v_b$, is recovered within the accessible space-time window. For power-law interactions, the arrival time is logarithmic in distance for all exponents studied, corresponding to an exponentially expanding spatial front; the power-law exponent controls the coefficient of this logarithm. We interpret both behaviors using a seeding-and-amplification picture in which long-range couplings generate weak disturbances throughout the chain whose growth is governed primarily by the local nearest-neighbor dynamics. In this picture, the overall strength of the tail primarily controls when the long-range front becomes visible, while its spatial decay controls the propagation law. Thus a weak interaction tail can leave conventional spin-dynamical observables essentially unchanged while qualitatively altering the long-distance propagation of decorrelation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 任何护送方案因连续流无法跨阱转移概率而存在固有多方差下限，引入与驱动速率成比例的瞬移可以提升该下限，并满足推导出的零耗散条件，以最少跳跃次数即可显著改善自由能模拟的效率与精度。

Abstract: Escorted free energy simulations reduce dissipation with a flow field that follows the equilibrium distribution. A continuous flow, however, cannot transfer probability between metastable basins. We show that any escort then faces a variance floor set by the change in basin weights, and lift it by adding teleportation: jumps at a rate proportional to the driving rate. We derive the corresponding nonequilibrium identity, the zero-dissipation condition, and the minimal number of jumps it requires, and illustrate the gain on an exactly solvable model and a tilted double well.

</details>

*Shashwat Chakraborty,Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部纠错虽能在噪声下保护经典信息，但当事务（sites）以独立随机时间演化时，同步更新下的保护未必成立；尤其当保护依赖相互竞争的局部规则的集体作用时，稳定性证明尤为困难。

Method: 作者发展了一套时空重整化框架，揭示独立作用的局部更新如何集体稳定记忆；给出稳定界面几何与缺陷传播抑制导致大尺度可靠侵蚀的充分条件。

Abstract: Local error correction can preserve classical information against noise, but protection under synchronized updates need not survive when sites evolve at independent random times. Establishing stability is particularly challenging when protection relies on the collective action of competing local rules. Here we develop a spacetime renormalization framework that reveals how independently acting local updates can collectively stabilize memory. We identify sufficient conditions under which the geometry of stable interfaces and suppression of defect propagation produce reliable erosion at large scales. Applying this framework, we prove stability of the asynchronous \textsf{R} squeezing code and introduce robust processes in two and three dimensions. In these systems, both encoded phases persist under sufficiently weak noise, even when the noise favors one phase. Our results provide a criterion for designing autonomous memories sustained by competing local stochastic dynamics.

</details>

Abstract: Using numerically exact finite-temperature quantum Monte Carlo, we demonstrate that a $B_{1g}$ phonon coupled to the doped $t$-$t'$ repulsive Hubbard model produces genuine $d$-wave superconductivity at a substantial transition temperature, in excess of either interaction alone. The observed trends suggest that stronger Hubbard repulsion may cooperate with the phonon to further raise the pairing scale. The enhancement persists at stronger repulsion within the accessible window, limited by the fermion sign problem, and follows a common trend across phonon frequencies $Ω$ when organized by a retardation scale $2πT/Ω$. We also find a well-defined superconducting dome in doping, echoing a characteristic feature of correlated superconductors. Our results identify the $B_{1g}$ phonon as a plausible microscopic ingredient for robust $d$-wave superconductivity in the Hubbard model and suggest a concrete, physically motivated route toward materials with higher superconducting transition temperatures.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电离辐射引起的声子爆发会在超导量子处理器中产生关联错误，但Majorana量子比特中由非平衡声子脉冲引起的激发、弛豫及逻辑相干损失的具体微观机制尚不清楚，需要建立从声子谱到错误类型的定量联系以指导器件设计。

Abstract: Phonon bursts from ionizing radiation cause correlated errors in superconducting quantum processors. We study prescribed nonequilibrium phonon pulses in a Majorana qubit formed by two $2\,μ\mathrm{m}$ proximitized nanowires. Combining the lattice Lutchyn--Oreg model, microscopic electron--phonon coupling, and parity-resolved many-body dynamics, we track excitation, relaxation, and logical coherence. Without quasiparticle escape, recombination restores the initial Majorana occupations in about 130 ns, while phonons exchanged during excitation and recovery can retain logical-state information, leaving permanent coherence loss. For mirror-symmetric wires, the fractional coherence loss per wire is 0.75--1.0 times the excitation probability averaged over the two logical states, even at zero Majorana splitting; breaking mirror symmetry can suppress dephasing at small splitting. Factorized Boltzmann kinetics neglects quasiparticle correlations and overestimates residual parity flips. At equal deposited energy density, phonon spectra yield different excitation probabilities and coherence losses. Comparisons with Kitaev chains matched to the wire's gap and Majorana decay length reveal that low electron density suppresses excitation by energetic phonons, whereas hard-wall boundaries in a high-density control increase excitation efficiency by a factor of $5\times10^4$ relative to the wire for pulses centered at 1 meV. Magnetic-field and chemical-potential tuning yield different phonon sensitivities at the same bulk gap through changes in spin and electron--hole structure. Including hybrid states' semiconductor weights reduces near-threshold excitation rates fourfold when the tunnel coupling equals the parent superconducting gap. Our framework connects phonon spectra to leakage, poisoning, and coherence loss to guide Majorana-device design.

</details>

Result: 1）N₂流量为1.5 sccm时薄膜性能最优：Tc最高（4.93 K）、RRR最大、ρₙ最低、近Tc处面外上临界场斜率最小；2）背景真空度显著影响超导性能，真空度下降导致Tc降低；3）上临界场呈现明显各向异性；4）面内上临界场数据表明，样品质量优化会使薄膜偏离二维超导行为，为超导维度性提供了除膜厚之外的新视角。

Abstract: TiN thin films have received great attention in the field of superconducting quantum computing due to their low loss characteristics derived from stable chemical and thermal properties, as well as excellent electrical conductivity. In this study, TiN thin films with a thickness of 35 nm were epitaxially grown on MgO substrates using the DC reactive magnetron sputtering technique. The crystal structure, film thickness, and surface morphology of the thin films were characterized. The films exhibit a very flat surface with a root mean square roughness of only a few tenths of a nanometer. The influences of nitrogen ($N_2$) flow rate on the surface morphology, superconducting transition temperature ($T_c$), residual resistivity ratio (RRR), normal-state resistivity ($ρ_n$), upper critical field, and superconducting dimensionality were investigated systematically. The experimental results show that the TiN film deposited with a $N_2$ flow rate of 1.5 sccm exhibits optimal performance, including the highest $T_c$ (4.93 K), the largest RRR, the lowest $ρ_n$, and the lowest slope of the out-of-film upper critical field near Tc. Moreover, it is found that the background vacuum inside the growth chamber has a significant impact on the superconducting properties of TiN films, and the degradation of vacuum degree can lead to the decrease of $T_c$. The upper critical field reveals a significant anisotropic feature. Importantly, the in-film upper critical field data demonstrates that the optimization of sample quality leads to deviation from two-dimensional superconducting behavior, which provides a new perspective on the influencing factors of superconducting dimensionality beyond the thickness of films. The present results provide useful references for a comprehensive understanding of the relationship between growth parameters and the superconducting properties of thin films.

Since P is equal to I2R it is independent of current polarity, the observed asymmetry is not associated with polarity-dependent Joule heating but with the interplay of thermal relaxation, sweep history, and thermal boundary conditions. These results highlight the need to distinguish thermal switching and hysteresis from intrinsic nonreciprocal superconductivity when identifying superconducting diode effects in transport measurements.

Result: 滑移提升与隐藏反幺正对称性各自独立地迫使BdG陈数为零；对角配对的实部非零恰好打破反幺正阻碍，这是手性相的必要而非充分条件。实部配对截面给出三个解析能隙闭合族，并由Pfaffian判定陈数宇称。闭克莱因瓶上手性态形成ΔC=2手性壁，展开后出现两支同向传播的马约拉纳分支；其有限尺寸近零对在允许的局部接缝微扰下被一阶提升；截圆柱对照组具有相同低能通道数，表明胶合只是重连已有的无能隙内容。粒子-空穴对称性交换覆盖(deck)本征块，故后者不能被视为独立的BdG商汉密尔顿量。

Abstract: We study a spinless $π$-flux lattice superconductor whose normal-state momentum-space fundamental domain is a Klein bottle. Within this model, a glide lift and a hidden antiunitary symmetry independently force the Bogoliubov--de Gennes (BdG) Chern number to vanish. A diagonal pairing breaks the antiunitary obstruction exactly when its real part is nonzero; this is necessary, not sufficient, for a chiral phase. We derive explicit Pfaffians determining Chern parity and identify three analytic families of gap closings in a real-pairing section. For a chiral state on a closed real-space Klein bottle, the prescribed pairing and gluing produce a $ΔC=2$ chirality wall. A translation-invariant unfolding resolves two co-propagating Majorana branches. Their finite-size levels depend on circumference and holonomy; the near-zero pair is lifted at first order by allowed local seam perturbations. A cut-cylinder control has the same low-energy channel count, so gluing reconnects existing gapless content. We also distinguish literal gluing from deck projection: particle--hole symmetry exchanges the deck eigenblocks, precluding their interpretation as independent BdG quotient Hamiltonians. The contribution is a model-specific account of pairing compatibility and closed-surface seam spectra. The energy cost is evaluated for a fixed pairing background; neither a protected zero mode nor a self-consistent wall solution is claimed.

</details>

Exact diagonalization reveals pair binding over $0.7\lesssimν\lesssim0.77$, consistent with experiment, along with finite superfluid stiffness, chiral $p$-wave pairing correlations, and adiabatic connectivity to an attractive model. At weak repulsion, Kohn-Luttinger theory supports a Fermi-surface instability with the same pairing symmetry. The superconducting state has a Bogoliubov-de Gennes Chern number $C_{\mathrm{BdG}}=-1$, opposite to the normal-state Chern number at $ν=1$. Our results reveal interplay among superconductivity, fractional topology, and charge order.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限连通系统的精确空腔解只能通过数值迭代得到，计算量大；作者希望借助全连通平均场极限，提供能解析刻画临界行为的闭合方程，从而大幅降低计算成本并揭示修正项的物理来源。

Result: 非均匀展开便于与 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick 模型直接比较，但因 AT 线在零温极限发散而在低温下失效；均匀展开则能较准确地描述系统的临界行为，并对零温极限也适用。

Conclusion: 该解析展开方法为有限连通平均场自旋玻璃的临界行为提供了高效、闭合的解析描述，其中均匀展开在包括低温与零温在内的区域均表现良好。

Abstract: We compute a high-connectivity analytical expansion for Ising spin glasses on sparse random graphs. We expand the cavity equations around the fully-connected mean-field limit, focusing on the critical behavior of the system: our approach allows us to compute the de Almeida-Thouless line of mean-field finitely-connected systems in the temperature $T$--magnetic field $H$ plane, by means of closed analytical equations. The exact cavity solution of such systems can only be obtained by solving numerically iterative equations, so our approach brings a notable computational advantage. On the theoretical side, we are able to identify three sources of corrections to the fully-connected mean-field solution: i) the non-Gaussianity of the cavity fields, ii) the statistical correlation between the cavity fields and their fluctuations, and iii) the corrections to the cavity filter functions. These corrections are treated using two different expansions: a non-uniform expansion, where we expand all three contributions around the mean-field solution, and a uniform expansion, where we only expand the statistics but leave the cavity filters untouched. The former allows for a direct comparison with the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model but fails at low temperatures, as a consequence of the de Almeida-Thouless line diverging in the zero-temperature limit. On the other hand, the uniform expansion describes with good accuracy the critical behavior of the system. We also extend these results to the $T = 0$ limit.

</details>

Abstract: Cascade of delocalization transitions provides a route to multi-step control of delocalization, yet achieving a programmable periodic cascade remains a challenge. Here, we demonstrate a tunable periodic cascade of delocalization transitions in a non-Hermitian interpolating Aubry-André-Fibonacci (IAAF) model by introducing an imaginary phase shift into the quasiperiodic potential. Such a periodic cascade of delocalization transitions arises from the competition between kinetic-energy-facilitated delocalization and gain-loss-regulated localization. In numerical static localization characterizations, successive regular stripe patterns for localized and extended states emerge in phase diagrams. We also derive an analytical expression for the cascade period and find that it relies solely on the imaginary phase shift. This provides a direct way of continuously tuning the cascade period via a single control parameter. Complementary static and dynamical diagnostics consistently confirm this periodic cascade of delocalization transitions in the non-Hermitian IAAF system. Our findings identify a novel class of delocalization transitions with tunable cascading periods, opening a route toward experimentally programmable control of delocalization.

Conclusion: 扩展后的生成泛函DMFT能有效描述相关模式块存储网络中的检索动力学，准确刻画可重入的检索区域、吸引盆地几何与分隔鞍点，并在不动点假设下与广义自洽信噪比理论一致，为相关模式记忆的动力学行为提供了理论框架。

Abstract: In associative memories storing correlated patterns, retrieval of individual memories can compete with symmetric mixed states. A dynamical description is needed to determine how the initial state selects between these outcomes. We extend the generating-functional dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) for independent patterns to independent blocks of correlated binary patterns of fixed finite size. The resulting effective single-neuron process has a self-consistent cross-talk noise covariance and self-feedback coefficients that depend on the block-covariance eigenvalues. For hierarchically correlated patterns with nonmonotonic transfer, increasing the loading can permit retrieval from an imperfect initial state before further loading prevents it. DMFT reproduces the retrieval trajectories and basin geometry found in direct simulations. Near the lower boundary of this re-entrant region, direct networks reveal stationary saddles separating coexisting mixed and target-retrieval states. Parameter sensitivities of DMFT trajectories predict nearby shifts of this boundary. At the tested upper boundary, retrieval remains accessible from initial states with larger target overlap, indicating a change in basin access. Under fixed-point convergence assumptions, the theory recovers the equations of generalized self-consistent signal-to-noise analysis.

Abstract: Physical aging drives glasses toward equilibrium and changes their thermodynamic and mechanical properties, but the accompanying microscopic structural evolution remains poorly understood. Here, using our recently developed low-background setup combining fast scanning calorimetry with \emph{in-situ} X-ray total scattering, we investigate microscopic structural changes during physical aging in Pd$_{42.5}$Cu$_{30}$Ni$_{7.5}$P$_{20}$ metallic glass. The measurements span an unprecedented six orders of magnitude in time and a temperature range extending from above the glass transition temperature to more than 150\,K below it. We observe dominant structural changes near the first and second peaks of the structure factor. Near and above the glass transition temperature, changes in the first sharp diffraction peak closely follow enthalpy relaxation and are associated predominantly with increasing degrees of medium-range ordering. At lower temperatures, the enthalpy evolution can no longer be described by this contribution alone, revealing an additional, faster relaxation process that correlates with changes near the second diffraction peak and thus short-range structural rearrangements, including increased Pd--Pd correlations. These results establish a direct connection between thermodynamic relaxation and structural evolution, providing strong evidence that primary and secondary aging processes are associated with distinct structural length scales.

Abstract: The singularity theorems and the past incompleteness of inflationary spacetimes suggest that the beginning of the Universe lies beyond the domain of semiclassical gravity. We show that the creation of the Universe can nevertheless be understood within Lorentzian gravity by allowing gravitational junctions, so that the full spacetime need not be a smooth manifold. We construct rotationally invariant cosmic creation gates joining any finite number $n\geq2$ of locally de Sitter regions in $D\geq3$ spacetime dimensions at a timelike perfect-fluid junction with $P=wρ$. For $w>-(D-3)/(D-2)$, they are geodesically complete in both temporal directions. The total spatial volume approaches finite, positive limits in the infinite past and future on the global-time foliation adapted to the junction fluid. We also construct exact deformed cosmic creation gates through nonlinear homogeneous backreaction that retain the initial volume while the future volume grows without bound. In these solutions, the junction fluid always expands, cooling for $w>0$ and heating for allowed $w<0$. Keeping the bulk regions locally de Sitter, we determine the linearized perturbations: in three dimensions, the generic inhomogeneous sector contains one hybrid sound mode and $n-1$ relative transverse modes; in four and higher dimensions, only the homogeneous hybrid sound mode survives. A finite-volume beginning requires fine-tuned homogeneous and inhomogeneous initial data, while causal response generically leads to unbounded future spatial volume. We also discuss a broader creation criterion allowing bounded past-volume oscillations. The gravitational equations determine conditioned admissible histories. We discuss implications for cosmology.

</details>

Abstract: In the extended phase space of Donnelly and Freidel, the edge modes of a Schwarzschild black hole are dynamical reference frames whose phase space is the cotangent bundle of $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$. Gluing the two exterior regions at the bifurcation point leaves a single canonical pair. Within the extended phase space, this pair can be described either by Kuchař's mass and difference of asymptotic times or by the area and the relative rapidity of the two frames, which are two gauge choices of the same system. The latter is the pair of Carlip and Teitelboim, and the relative rapidity is the time shift of the crossed product construction. We quantize the frames in this gauge and construct the gravitational entangling product of the two exteriors. Requiring the frames to be causal restricts them to the semigroup $\mathrm{SL}^+(2,\mathbb{R})$, whose Plancherel measure yields the Bekenstein--Hawking entropy as the entanglement entropy between the two exteriors, together with a logarithmic correction, both independent of the ultraviolet cutoff.

Abstract: We study the circuit complexity of coupled curvature and entropy perturbations in two-field inflation. We start from the quadratic action and derive the full Hamiltonian. Abstract: Recently, it has been observed that in certain schemes of the bosonic string effective action, the non-derivative tachyon appears only in the tachyon potential, while all other couplings involve covariant derivatives of the tachyon and massless fields. Within these schemes, a minimal basis can be found whose coupling constants are constrained by T-duality upon circle reduction. Using this constraint, one obtains sets of couplings at each order of \(α'\) that are invariant under Buscher rules supplemented by higher-derivative corrections for all base-space fields except the tachyon. In this paper, we observe that consistency of these T-dual sets with S-matrix elements requires an appropriate field redefinition, such that each set becomes invariant under Buscher rules supplemented by higher-derivative corrections for all base-space fields except the metric, dilaton, and tachyon. This separates the couplings into a massless sector and a tachyon sector. We determine the six-derivative couplings in the massless sector, with all coupling constants fixed, and find two T-dual couplings in the tachyon sector with arbitrary coupling constants. We show that the leading-order action and the massless-sector couplings exactly reproduce the expansion of the sphere-level S-matrix element of two tachyons and two massless vertex operators at six-momentum order, and that the coupling constant of the four-tachyon coupling in the tachyon sector is fixed by the expansion of the four-tachyon S-matrix element at six-momentum order.

Abstract: We argue that the scattering amplitude of a single minus helicity gluon with arbitrarily many positive helicity gluons is topological. By reinterpreting wall-crossing and the Weinberg soft theorem as skein relations and Reidemeister moves of the subknots, we construct a state-counting description consisting of adding the `corners' of their HOMFLY-PT polynomials. Abstract: Fractional field theory is a promising framework wherein to quantize the gravitational interaction but the presence of nonlocal operators can make its handling difficult. With the purpose of easing this problem, we recast the theory as a local dual in two ways. First, we generalize the Caffarelli--Silvestre extension theorem to self-adjoint fractional field theories with multi-scale kinetic terms and nonlocal interactions, taking the example of a scalar field. The latter is reinterpreted as a nonlocal boundary degree of freedom holographically dual to a local theory of two scalars living in a higher-dimensional bulk. However, inclusion of a local non-linear potential is not straightforward. We therefore propose an alternative picture where the nonlocal theory is not extended to a higher-dimensional space but is localized at the price of introducing a continuum of masses. This \emph{spectral localization} allows for a natural implementation of the fakeon prescription and the related classicization procedure, already employed in local theories with higher-derivative and complex ghosts. Applications to fractional quantum gravity and its comparison with quadratic quantum gravity are discussed.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义对称性对时空的切向结构敏感。以往对QED等高形式对称性的讨论多在自旋或无特殊切向结构的流形上进行，而真实的求和往往依赖于流形是否自旋。作者希望澄清：当流形非自旋（仅定向）时，光子作为spin^c联络以及路径积分对spin^c结构的求和，如何修改磁1-form对称性及其反常结构，并揭示其中纯引力的、由切向结构决定的效应。

Abstract: Generalized symmetries are sensitive to the tangential structure of spacetime. We study this interplay for massless QED on oriented non-spin four-manifolds, where the photon becomes a spin$^c$ connection and the path integral sums over spin$^c$ structures. The shifted flux quantization extends the magnetic 1-form symmetry into a $w_2$-twisted 1-form symmetry: its $2π$ rotation acts as a gravitational sign, fixed by the normal bundle of the surface the operator wraps. The obstruction to treating it as an ordinary magnetic 1-form symmetry is an order-two mixed magnetic-gravitational anomaly, which is classified by reduced oriented bordism and detected on the Wu manifold. In a fixed spin$^c$ sector, this anomaly is removable by a counterterm, so it is a property of the sum over spin$^c$ structures. We discuss its consequences for gapped, Coulomb, and Higgs phases. The chiral anomaly is governed by the index of the spin$^c$ Dirac operator, so rational chiral defects carry gravitational as well as electromagnetic responses. A spin$^c$ Hall topological field theory cancels the electromagnetic part but leaves a framing anomaly. At the level of anomaly inflow, this is canceled for every rational angle by stacking a neutral, generally noninvertible Witten-Reshetikhin-Turaev layer. Abstract: We introduce virasoro_blocks, software that computes Virasoro conformal blocks at any central charge in a form suitable for the numerical bootstrap. In this software we use optimal interpolation to reduce the number of poles while keeping high accuracy. Abstract: We formulate a Schwinger--Keldysh worldline effective field theory for quantum black holes that treats dissipation, fluctuations, and decoherence within one real-time influence functional. Their spectra obey positivity constraints, while thermal Kubo--Martin--Schwinger symmetry relates them through the fluctuation--dissipation relation. A Hubbard--Stratonovich representation turns the noise kernel into a Gaussian source driving the resolved metric and worldline. Abstract: Newton's law of universal gravitation describes the attraction between two masses through an inverse-distance potential. In Einstein's theory gravity gravitates: each mass reshapes the spacetime geometry through which the other acts. More than a century later, extracting the two-body interaction from this coupled geometry remains a fundamental analytical challenge. Even without motion or radiation, the static sector of the interaction harbours an infinite hierarchy of field couplings: the purest laboratory for gravitational non-linearity. Here we determine the static interaction from ninth to twelfth order in Newton's constant for arbitrary mass ratios, and derive a closed expression for the first backreaction beyond the Schwarzschild test-body limit to all orders in the gravitational coupling. We achieve both advances by formulating the problem around the stationary interaction energy. Together, these results bridge the gap between an infinitesimal probe and comparable masses, exposing hidden analytical simplicity within Einstein's theory and providing essential building blocks for modelling orbital dynamics and gravitational-wave generation.

The ultraviolet conditions for their finiteness are established, and the nonlocal causal structure is analyzed in terms of a spatial convolution of the ordinary Pauli-Jordan distribution. An illustrative interacting extension and numerical profiles of the kernels and amplitudes are also presented.

Relative equivariant localization can obtain supersymmetric observables in odd dimensions from fixed-point data on an auxiliary even-dimensional filling, without an explicit solution. We formulate the Euclidean action and Killing-spinor equations in terms of field strengths, and construct a bilinear system adapted to the change in spinor reality conditions from symplectic-Majorana to Majorana. On the non-null gauged active spinor branch, independent, oppositely R-charged spinors are paired by Majorana conjugation on the same complex background. One algebraic gauging correction and two algebraic bilinear projections suffice to extend the equal-charge sector to independent Cartan charges, and the underlying efficient reorganization also applies in the Lorentzian theory. Massive 3-form self-duality fixes the quartic anomaly class and determines the higher-form fixed-point datum from Abelian moment maps. Localization at the single fixed point of the filling recovers the general supersymmetric AdS$_7$ black-hole action, without extending the solution. We prove gauge-covariant Killing-spinor invariance under the combined supersymmetric Killing flow and R-symmetry action, deriving the linear chemical-potential constraint. Extremizing the constrained entropy functional and requiring real positive stationary entropy yields the nonlinear charge relation within the Euclidean analysis, equivalent to Lorentzian horizon extremality for the known spherical black-hole family.

Nevertheless, in the presence of a scalar field, this symmetry group is not large enough in five dimensions to guarantee solution uniqueness, and the additional U(1) symmetry remains a conjecture in \cite{Sadeghian:2014}. Our results extend to the superconformal and topologically twisted indices on $S^2\times S^1$, whose large-$N$ and Cardy limit is determined by the same saddle. The flavour vectors are not computed from a compactification but fixed by matching, so our results are an existence proof. Matching the measured mass-squared differences selects the seesaw-dominated case with normal ordering. Swampland criteria constrain the instanton action and the reheating temperature. Abstract: The factorization over the ideal in the $d$-dimensional Vasiliev theory requires an accurate description of the higher-spin algebra $\mathcal{A}$ and its twisted adjoint module $\mathcal{A}^{tw}$ as certain functional spaces. It is shown that the conditions of $\mathcal{A}$ to be closed under action of the Moyal star product, and of $\mathcal{A}^{tw}$ to be a star-product $\mathcal{A}$-module along with some auxiliary conditions lead to properly defined free-level factorization procedure. The conditions on admissible functions have simple algebraic form; in the case of $\mathcal{A}$ the analogous functional space has been considered by Soloviev \cite{Soloviev:2013iba}. The relation of the obtained functional classes to the locality problem in the Vasiliev theory is discussed. In particular it is observed that certain non-local expressions diverge due to properties of $\mathcal{A}^{tw}$ while the spin-local expressions are well-defined.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究引力路径积分中 S^3 超曲面上的度规涨落对宇宙波函数的影响，特别是探索边界条件 (通过协变边界作用量实现) 如何约束涨落场的边界行为，以及这些边界选择如何影响单圈量子修正——重点是确定哪些鞍点 (deSitter、Hartle-Hawking、隧穿宇宙) 具有良态扰动，并系统处理紫外与红外交散。

Result: 初动量的主导贡献将 ω 限制为半整数，产生一族依赖 c 的共轭动量；固定 ω 时出现四个 c 依赖的主导共轭动量分支：两个对应 deSitter，一个对应 Hartle-Hawking，一个对应隧穿宇宙。所有这些分支的 lapse 鞍点都是相关的，但仅有其中一个 deSitter 鞍点和一个 Hartle-Hawking 鞍点具有良态扰动。边界参数 c 既充当紫外正规化参数，又将单圈行列式的极点移离实 q_f 轴 (对 dS 鞍点)。通过附加抵消项可获得无边界与 dS 的紫外有限波函数；这些波函数的红外行为不仅呈现红外交散，还表现出对初值边界选择的敏感性。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational path integral of $\mathbb{R} \times S^3$ geometries in Lorentzian signature along with the metric fluctuations which live on the $S^3$ hypersurfaces. Each metric configuration is characterised by lapse, scale factor and fluctuations, and is weighted by the exponential of the Einstein-Hilbert action. A particular boundary requirement for the scale-factor is achievable via an infinite family of covariant boundary actions, each restricting the boundary behaviour of the fluctuation field which cannot be arbitrarily chosen. The paper explores the effect of this infinite family of covariant boundary actions on the wavefunction of Universe to one-loop. These boundary actions are characterized by parameters $c$ and $ω$. Dominant contribution of initial momentum in the path-integral restricts $ω$ to be half-integer, leading to an infinite family of $c$-dependent conjugate momenta. In special case for fixed $ω$, four such $c$-dependent dominant branches of conjugate momenta emerge: two lead to deSitter, one leads to Hartle-Hawking and one to the tunnelling Universe. For all these $c$-dependent initial conjugate momenta, the lapse-saddles are relevant, however only, one of deSitter saddle and Hartle Hawking saddle have well-behaved perturbations. The one-loop wavefunction at these well-behaved saddles is computed systematically by isolating the UV-divergences, where the covariant boundary parameter $c$ acts as a UV-regulator and also helps in shifting the poles of one-loop determinant, away from the real $q_f$ axis for dS saddles, where $q_f$ is the final size of the Universe. UV-finite wavefunction for no-boundary and dS is obtained by adding suitable counterterms. The infrared behaviour of these wavefunctions show not only IR-divergences but also sensitivity toward the initial boundary choices.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a new method to construct the tree-level Compton amplitude for gravitational-wave scattering off a Kerr black hole in post-Minkowskian effective field theory. Building on previous computations that required physical consistency of the amplitude, the method uses wave scattering functions from black hole perturbation theory to uniquely determine additional contact terms. We illustrate the iterative procedure by deriving explicit expressions up to sixth order in spin, including previously unknown sectors at the fifth and sixth orders in spin. The amplitude features complex transcendental structures and a leading dissipative contact term at sixth order in spin. We verify our result against known results in the literature and reproduce the one-loop tail result from black hole perturbation theory.

Abstract: We study a supersymmetric Wilson line on the boundary of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory in AdS$_4$, or equivalently on a half-space, as a model for exploring the connection between holographic strings and confining flux tubes. The boundary breaks four-dimensional conformal symmetry and is expected to remove integrability, bringing this system closer to a confining string while preserving enough supersymmetry to constrain its dynamics. Working in the planar limit, we analyze the defect spectrum at weak and strong coupling and compute the superconformal index, thereby relating the perturbative gauge-theory description to the worldsheet excitations of a long holographic string. These complementary perspectives allow us to propose a map between operators in the two regimes, under which the worldsheet axion, in particular, becomes a two-particle state at strong coupling. The resulting picture supports a smooth interpolation from an exponentially growing gauge-theory spectrum to the Cardy-like spectrum of a string worldsheet, providing a non-integrable supersymmetric model for investigating the spectral reorganization expected in confining gauge theories.

Conclusion: 本文建立了6d N=(1,0)超共形场论中半BPS缺陷与量子Seiberg-Witten曲线的系统构造方法，并成功推广到Higgsing树上的多类规范群及带扭转的紧化情形，为研究高维规范理论的量子几何提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study half-BPS defects and quantum Seiberg-Witten curves in 6d $ \mathcal{N}=(1,0) $ SCFTs on a $ -3 $ curve. We compute the partition functions in the presence of codimension-4 and codimension-2 defects on $ \mathbb{R}^4 \times T^2 $ using generalized blowup equations. We then propose quantum Seiberg-Witten curves as elliptic difference equations satisfied by the Nekrasov-Shatashvili limits of the defect partition functions. We apply this construction to the minimal $ \mathfrak{su}(3) $ gauge theory, and to the $ \mathfrak{g}_2 $ and $ \mathfrak{so}(N) $ gauge theories on a Higgsing tree whose endpoint is the minimal $ \mathfrak{su}(3) $ theory. We also investigate defects and the quantum curve of the $ \mathfrak{su}(3) $ gauge theory compactified on a circle with a $ \mathbb{Z}_2 $ twist.

</details>


### [95] [Competition and coexistence of baryonic condensates in AdS hardwall model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40244)
*Akash Singh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究冷致密QCD中双夸克(diquark)与双重子(dibaryon)两类重子数破缺序参量之间的排斥与共存关系。作者采用带完全反作用(full backreaction)的五维AdS硬墙模型，让两个带U(1)_B电荷的标量场共同耦合到同一规范场，发现两者仅在较窄的算符维数Δ₂窗口内同时凝聚，该窗口随电荷q₂增大而变宽、并在两算符重合时关闭；窗口之外只有单一凝聚。该结构在禁闭相与退禁闭相中都存在。在μ_B–T相图中，低温下带两种凝聚的禁闭物质占优，其上方是一窄带只含单一凝聚的退禁闭物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷致密QCD存在多种破缺重子数的序参量（双夸克、双重子凝聚），但尚不清楚它们是相互排斥还是可以共存。作者旨在判断这两类凝聚能否同时存在，以及共存发生的条件。

Method: 采用带完全反作用的五维AdS硬墙(hardwall)全息模型，在体(bulk)中引入两个带电标量场，分别对偶于电荷为q_i、共形维数为Δ_i的重子数破缺算符，并使二者共同耦合到同一个U(1)_B规范场。固定双夸克类算符(q₁=2, Δ₁=3)，系统地改变另一个算符的电荷q₂与维数Δ₂，求解反作用耦合方程组以判断两个标量能否同时获得非零凝聚。

Result: 两个算符仅在Δ₂的一个窄窗口内同时凝聚；该窗口随q₂增大而加宽，并在两算符完全重合时(Δ₂→Δ₁、q₂→q₁)关闭。窗口之外只形成单一凝聚。这一结构在禁闭相和退禁闭相中均保持不变。在μ_B–T平面上，低温下具有双重凝聚的禁闭物质处于热力学优先地位，其上方是一窄带只含单一凝聚的退禁闭物质。

Conclusion: 双夸克与双重子凝聚并非绝对排斥，而是在特定参数窗口（窄的Δ₂区间、随q₂扩展）内可以共存；两算符重合时共存消失。相图中低温禁闭相支持共存，高温退禁闭相仅支持单一凝聚。这表明在冷致密QCD中，不同重子数破缺序参量的共存强烈依赖算符的电荷与共形维数。

Abstract: Cold dense QCD admits several order parameters for baryon-number breaking, among them diquark and dibaryon condensates. We ask whether they exclude one another or coexist, using a five-dimensional AdS hardwall model with full backreaction in which two charged scalars, dual to operators of charges $q_i$ and dimensions $Δ_i$, couple to a common $U(1)_B$ gauge field. With the diquark-like operator fixed at $q_1=2$, $Δ_1=3$, both operators condense only within a narrow window of $Δ_2$, which widens with $q_2$ and closes when the operators coincide. Outside the window, only a single condensate forms. The structure persists in both confined and deconfined phases. In the $μ_B$-$T$ plane, confined matter with both condensates is preferred at low temperature, below a narrow band of deconfined matter with one condensate.

</details>


### [96] [$\mathcal{N}=2$ conformal higher-spin actions in harmonic superspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40274)
*Evgeny Ivanov,Nikita Zaigraev*

TL;DR: 本文在谐波超空间中为 arXiv:2404.19016 引入的超共形高自旋规范多重态构造了线性化的 4D N=2 Weyl 型离壳作用量，并验证了其超共形不变性与线性化规范不变性；同时论证了向任意 N=2 超引力背景的推广仅在 Weyl 平坦超背景下才自洽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为超共形高自旋规范多重态建立一个由 Grassmann 谐波解析性原理决定的几何框架，进而为把这些线性化理论提升到完全非线性水平提供可能的途径，并厘清其作用于一般 N=2 超引力背景时的自洽性条件。

Method: 作者在 4D N=2 谐波超空间中构造 Weyl 型离壳作用量。基本框架是：底层预势（prepotentials）的结构及其规范与超共形变换完全由 Grassmann 谐波解析性原理确定；不变量作用量和广义 N=2 超 Weyl 张量的构造则依赖于 Grassmann 半解析性原理。文中检查了作用量的超共形不变性及线性化规范不变性，并进一步分析向任意 N=2 超引力背景的推广。

Result: 成功构造了线性化 4D N=2 Weyl 型离壳作用量，验证了这些新作用量的超共形不变性与线性化规范不变性；证明在不彻底修改规范变换律和/或谐波零曲率方程的前提下，线性化自旋-s 规范不变性仅与 N=2 Weyl 平坦超背景自洽。

Conclusion: 这项工作不仅为线性化 N=2 超共形规范高自旋理论提供了新的几何框架，还暗示了将其提升到完全非线性层次的可能路径；向任意 N=2 超引力背景推广的结论表明，规范不变性的一致实现只允许 Weyl 平坦的超背景。

Abstract: We construct linearized $4D, \mathcal{N}=2$ Weyl-type off-shell actions in harmonic superspace for the superconformal higher-spin gauge multiplets introduced in arXiv:2404.19016. This work is a followup of our arXiv:2511.16325, where an analogous construction was carried out for conformal $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity. We check the superconformal invariance of the new actions and their linearized gauge invariance. As the characteristic features of our formulation, the structure of the underlying prepotentials and their gauge and superconformal transformations are entirely determined by the fundamental \textit{principle of Grassmann harmonic analyticity}, while the construction of the invariant actions and of the generalized $\mathcal{N}=2$ super-Weyl tensors relies upon the \textit{principle of Grassmann half-analyticity}. This provides not only a new geometric framework for the linearized $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal gauge higher-spin theories, but also suggests possible ways of promoting them to the full nonlinear level. We consider a generalization to an arbitrary $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity background and show that the linearized spin-$\mathbf{s}$ gauge invariance, without radical modifications of the gauge transformation law and/or harmonic zero-curvature equations, is consistent only with $\mathcal{N}=2$ Weyl-flat super-backgrounds.

</details>


### [97] [Bounds on the Diagonal Degree of the Biadjoint Scalar Amplitude Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40355)
*Freddy Cachazo,Rajbir Longia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究双伴随标量理论部分振幅矩阵 $M_n$ 的对角子矩阵的极大秩（对角度 $\Delta_n$），证明了 $\Delta_n$ 相对 CHY 秩 $(n-3)!$ 被指数压制，从而对"是否存在临界 $n_c$ 使所有 $n>n_c$ 时 $M_n$ 存在满秩对角子矩阵"这一问题给出了否定回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHY 构造表明 $M_n$ 的秩为 $(n-3)!$，满秩可逆子矩阵出现在将引力子振幅与胶子振幅联系起来的 KLT 公式中。为寻找项数最少的 KLT 公式，作者之一提出问题：是否存在临界 $n_c$，使得对所有 $n>n_c$，$M_n$ 都存在秩为 $(n-3)!$ 的对角子矩阵。

Method: 作者引入对角度 $\Delta_n$，即 $M_n$ 的最大非奇异对角子矩阵的规模，并通过对 $\Delta_n$ 的上下界估计来回答该问题。具体地，证明了对角度与 CHY 秩之比呈指数衰减：$\Delta_n/(n-3)! = \exp(-\Theta(n))$（当 $n\to\infty$），并建立了显式界 $\exp(-1.5n) \leq \Delta_n/(n-3)! \leq \exp[-0.01n + O(1)]$。

Result: 证明了 $\Delta_n/(n-3)! = \exp(-\Theta(n))$，并给出更精确的界 $\exp(-1.5n) \leq \Delta_n/(n-3)! \leq \exp[-0.01n + O(1)]$。由此回答了原问题：不存在这样的临界 $n_c$，即对所有充分大的 $n$ 都不存在秩为 $(n-3)!$ 的对角子矩阵。

Conclusion: $M_n$ 的对角度（最大非奇异对角子矩阵的秩）相对其 CHY 秩被指数压制，因此文中提出的关于最小 KLT 公式项数的问题得到否定解答：当 $n$ 充分大时，不存在满秩的对角子矩阵。

Abstract: Biadjoint scalar theory partial amplitudes, $m_n(α,β)$, define an $(n-1)!/2\times (n-1)!/2$ matrix, $M_n$, with rows and columns labeled by orderings. The CHY construction of $M_n$ implies that its rank is $(n-3)!$. Invertible submatrices of maximal rank enter in the KLT construction relating graviton amplitudes to gluon amplitudes. Motivated by finding the KLT formula with the smallest number of terms, one of the authors posed the question of whether there is a critical $n_c$ such that for all $n>n_c$ there exists a diagonal submatrix of $M_n$ of rank $(n-3)!$. In this work we obtain bounds on the size of the largest non-singular diagonal submatrix of $M_n$, which we call its diagonal degree $Δ_n$, and we answer the question in the negative. We prove that the diagonal degree is exponentially suppressed relative to the CHY rank: $Δ_n/(n-3)! = \exp(-Θ(n))$ as $n\to \infty$. More explicitly, we establish the bounds $\exp(-1.5n)\leqΔ_n/(n-3)!\leq \exp \left[ -0.01n+O(1)\right]$.

</details>


### [98] [Breaking of integrability in elliptic deformations of AdS3 strings with mixed flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40386)
*Ian Le Meur,Fiona K. Seibold*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了支持混合NSNS与RR通量的IIB型超引力中 $AdS_3 \times S^3 \times T^4$ 超弦的椭圆形变，并通过平坦Lax联络和树级世界面S-矩阵两个角度研究了其可积性；结果表明对一般椭圆参数及NSNS通量会出现非弹性2→2振幅破坏可积性，而仅在Lax分析所确定的可积情形下非弹性振幅消失且满足经典Yang-Baxter方程，尤其是Wess-Zumino项会破坏椭圆形变的可积性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超弦背景的可积形变对于理解AdS/CFT对应及可积系统具有重要意义，而将已知的有理/三角可积形变推广到椭圆情形并考察NSNS通量(含Wess-Zumino项)对可积性的影响是一个开放问题。

Method: 构造AdS3×S3×T4超弦的椭圆形变，NSNS部分包含Wess-Zumino项，并引入耦合形变AdS3与S3因子的B场以保持8个超对称；从两个角度检验可积性：(1)构造平坦Lax联络；(2)计算树级世界面S-矩阵，并检验其是否满足经典Yang-Baxter方程。

Result: 对一般椭圆参数和NSNS通量，发现非弹性2→2振幅，破坏可积性所需的弹性要求；在Lax分析认定的可积情形下，这些非弹性振幅消失，且树级S-矩阵满足经典Yang-Baxter方程；特别地，在有理与三角模型中保持可积性的Wess-Zumino项，会破坏椭圆形变的可积性。

Conclusion: 椭圆形变的可积性比有理和三角情形更受限制：Wess-Zumino项的加入会破坏可积性，仅在Lax分析所界定的特定情形下可积性才得以保持，此时树级S-矩阵满足经典Yang-Baxter方程。

Abstract: We construct elliptic deformations of the $AdS_3 \times S^3 \times T^4$ superstring supported by mixed NSNS and RR fluxes in type IIB supergravity and investigate their integrability. The NSNS sector includes a Wess-Zumino term, and imposing that the theory preserves 8 supersymmetries also requires the presence of a B-field coupling the deformed $AdS_3$ and $S^3$ factors. We explore integrability from two different perspectives: through the construction of a flat Lax connection and the computation of the tree-level worldsheet S-matrix. For generic elliptic parameters and NSNS flux, we find inelastic $2 \rightarrow 2$ amplitudes, violating the elasticity required by integrability. In the integrable cases as identified by the Lax analysis, these inelastic amplitudes vanish and the tree-level S-matrix satisfies the classical Yang-Baxter equation. In particular, the addition of a Wess-Zumino term, which preserves integrability in the rational and trigonometric models, breaks integrability for elliptic deformations.

</details>


### [99] [Holographic duals of JT gravity at finite cutoff: the classical case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40391)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于探究体（bulk）局域性与时空底层结构，理解在有限截断边界及 ETW 膜存在时 JT 引力的相空间自由度，从而推广既有的把 BF 模型动态约化到边界一维系统的构造。

Method: 采用 JT 引力的一阶 BF 表述，引入有限截断边界和世界末日膜；将 BF 模型动态约化为生活在边界上的一维系统；利用该框架推导相空间上全息 Schwarzian 描述的闭合表达式，其自由度刻画有限截断曲线在 AdS₂ 中的嵌入。

Result: 获得了对各个边界所关联的相空间自由度的几何化理解，并推导出有限截断 JT 引力在相空间上的全息 Schwarzian 描述的闭合形式表达式。

Conclusion: 该构造成功将带有限截断边界和 ETW 膜的 JT 引力约化为边界一维系统，明确了边界自由度即有限截断曲线在 AdS₂ 中的嵌入，并为后续伴随论文中的量子化奠定了基础。

Abstract: We formulate JT gravity with finite-cutoff boundaries and end-of-the-world branes in the first-order BF formulation. We dynamically reduce the BF model into a one-dimensional system living at the boundaries, generalizing earlier constructions. We obtain in particular a geometrical understanding of the phase space degrees of freedom associated to each of these boundaries. We leverage these results to probe bulk locality and the underlying structure of spacetime. Within our framework, we are able to derive a closed-form expression for the holographic Schwarzian description of JT gravity at finite-cutoff in phase space. The resulting degrees of freedom describe the embedding of the finite-cutoff curve in AdS$_2$. A quantization of these systems is presented in a companion paper.

</details>


### [100] [A Simplified Born Rule for Cosmological Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40412)
*Fan Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明平直时空中等时 in-in 宇宙学关联函数的所有双边界极点对所有图（含局域导数相互作用）在空间圈动量积分前即相消，从而得到用"嵌入波函数系数"表述的简化玻恩规则；对共形耦合 Tr(φ³) 理论，该规则化为均匀加权的森林切割展开，并通过构造"玻恩多面体"及其规范函数、匹配刻面支撑与物理能量来复现任意图的归一化关联函数，同时给出弦论型公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学关联函数是原初宇宙的自然观测量，经玻恩规则与波函数系数相联系。作者希望理解等时 in-in 关联函数中极点（尤其是双边界极点）的结构与相消机制，并将其几何化，从而以显式保留相互作用依赖的方式刻画哪些物理极点可同时出现。

Method: 针对平直时空等时 in-in 关联函数，证明任意图（含局域导数相互作用）在空间圈动量积分之前所有双边界极点相消；据此定义嵌入波函数系数并给出简化玻恩规则。对共形耦合 Tr(φ³) 理论，把简化玻恩规则表述为均匀加权的森林切割展开；由极点组合关系构造玻恩多面体，以其规范函数（canonical function）复现归一化关联函数，匹配刻面支撑与物理能量，并进一步给出弦论型公式。

Result: 证明任意图的双边界极点相消发生在空间圈动量积分前，得到简化玻恩规则；对共形耦合 Tr(φ³) 理论得到均匀加权森林切割展开；构造的玻恩多面体的规范函数可复现任意图的归一化关联函数，且该几何同时给出弦论型公式。

Conclusion: 双边界极点的普遍相消导致简化玻恩规则，使物理极点的共现结构与相互作用依赖更清晰；通过玻恩多面体这一几何对象，可将共形耦合 Tr(φ³) 理论的关联函数编码为多面体的规范函数，揭示宇宙学关联函数背后的凸几何与组合结构，并建立与弦论型公式的联系。

Abstract: Cosmological correlators are natural observables of the primordial universe and are related to wavefunction coefficients by the Born rule. For equal-time in-in correlators in flat spacetime, we prove that all double-boundary poles cancel for arbitrary graphs, including in theories with local derivative interactions. These cancellations occur before integration over spatial loop momenta and yield a $\textit{simplified Born rule}$ in terms of what we call embedded wavefunction coefficients. The resulting expansion reveals which physical poles can occur together while keeping the interaction dependence explicit. For conformally coupled $\operatorname{Tr}(φ^3)$ theory in flat spacetime, the simplified Born rule takes the form of a uniformly weighted forest-cut expansion. From its pole combinatorics, we construct what we call the $\textit{Born polytope}$, whose canonical function reproduces the normalized correlator for arbitrary graphs after matching its facet supports to the physical energies. The same geometry also yields a stringy formula for correlators.

</details>
