</details>


### [102] [Thermodynamics of Swirling Black Holes and Kerr-Taub-NUT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40529)
*Ernesto Frodden,Diego Hidalgo,Francisco Manzano*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过表面电荷方法研究了带有弦状缺陷（锥形奇异性）的Kerr swirling（KS）和Kerr-Taub-NUT（KTN）黑洞热力学第一定律，发现其熵不再遵循视界面积的四分之一；而可正则化、无弦状缺陷的加速Kerr swirling（AKS）黑洞则满足通常的面积熵定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这些黑洞时空带有沿转轴的弦状缺陷（锥形奇异性）和非平坦渐近区域，导致标准方法（如ADM质量、面积熵）无法直接应用；作者希望在不引入与对称性无关的独立“弦”项的前提下，建立热力学第一定律并理解熵的偏离。

Method: 基于诺特定理将全局对称性与守恒律相联系的思路，采用并改造表面电荷方法，在存在弦状缺陷的背景下准局域地计算有限的质量与角动量积分电荷；对AKS黑洞先用Christodoulou-Ruffini公式确定质量，再与表面电荷法结果相互检验；随后分别建立KS、AKS和KTN的第一定律。

Result: 对于KS和KTN黑洞，得到有限且准局域的积分电荷，并建立不引入无关弦项的第一定律；但黑洞熵偏离视界面积的四分之一，说明标准公式不能简单用于带弦状缺陷的黑洞。相反，AKS黑洞可在奇点外正则化且无弦状缺陷，其电荷满足具有通常面积熵（四分之一）的第一定律，且质量结果与Christodoulou-Ruffini公式一致。

Conclusion: 带弦状缺陷的黑洞具有不同的拓扑结构，需要新的几何框架；在坚持与守恒律相联系的电荷定义下，其熵不再等于面积的四分之一。而无弦状缺陷的AKS黑洞则恢复标准面积熵，表明缺陷结构是导致标准热力学公式失效的关键因素。

Abstract: We establish the first law of black hole thermodynamics for the recently discovered Kerr swirling (KS) and accelerated Kerr swirling (AKS) black hole solutions. We also revisit the first law for the Kerr-Taub-NUT (KTN) spacetime. Guided by Noether's original insight that global symmetries give rise to conservation laws, we employ and adapt the surface charge method for computing the associated charges (mass and angular momentum) in the presence of spacetime string-like defects (conical singularities) along the axes of both KS and KTN black holes. We obtain finite integrated charges in a quasi-local manner, without relying on the non-flat asymptotic regions characteristic of these spacetimes. We then establish a first law without introducing independent stringy terms unrelated to symmetries. For both KS and KTN, the quantities that play the role of the black hole entropy differ from the simple geometrical rule of one-fourth of the horizon area. This mismatch arises because we keep first-law charges associated with conservation laws. It indicates that standard formulas may not be naively used for black holes with string-like defects; they have a different topology and suggest the need for a new geometrical framework. In stark contrast, for the AKS spacetime, which can be regularized outside the singularity and lacks string-like defects, we find that the charges are consistent with a first law having the usual one-fourth of the area entropy. We first determine the mass using the Christodoulou-Ruffini formula and then check its agreement with the result obtained from the surface charge method.

</details>


### [103] [On Geometric Observables in Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40615)
*Manthos Karydas,Murat Kologlu,Allic Sivaramakrishnan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究微扰量子引力中的几何可观测量（距离与时间），通过引力场打扮使其坐标不变，并展示这些有限距离可观测量在取渐近极限时如何化为记忆效应与Shapiro时间延迟算符，同时计算了相关关联函数中的引力子领头阶效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 距离、时间等几何量在经典引力中是基础量，虽在量子引力中广泛出现，但其引力子修正鲜有研究；作者希望建立能够刻画测量（含时空涨落情形）的几何可观测量，并厘清其与渐近端（天球）上实验和操作的关联。

Method: 在微扰量子引力框架下构造距离与时间可观测量以模拟测量过程（含时空涨落），利用引力场打扮（gravitational dressing）保证坐标不变性；随后取渐近极限，将这些有限距离可观测量化为记忆效应算符与Shapiro时间延迟算符，并在此基础上计算关联函数中的引力子领头阶贡献。

Result: 成功定义了一类坐标不变的几何可观测量，证明其渐近极限对应记忆效应与Shapiro时间延迟；并系统计算了这些几何可观测量关联函数中的引力子领头阶效应。

Conclusion: 识别出一类联系天球上实验与操作的具体几何可观测量，为理解量子引力中几何量及其引力子修正提供了新框架与定量结果。

Abstract: Measures of geometry like distance and time are staples of classical gravity. While geometric quantities arise broadly in quantum gravity, their graviton corrections are less explored. In this paper, we study geometric observables in perturbative quantum gravity. We form distance and time observables that model measurements, including in the presence of spacetime fluctuations. Gravitational dressing ensures these constructions are coordinate invariant. Then we show how these finite-distance observables become memory and Shapiro time delay operators upon taking asymptotic limits. We compute leading graviton effects in correlators of geometric observables throughout. Altogether, our results identify a class of geometric observables that link experiments and operations on the celestial sphere.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

### [104] [Meta-Altermagnetism and Enhanced Spin-Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37754)
*Bogeng Wen,Hae-Young Kee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文从理论上提出并研究了二维系统中一类新的量子相变——"元变磁相变"（meta-altermagnetism），即交变磁体中磁序诱导的能带重构使自旋劈裂的范霍夫奇点穿越费米面时发生一阶跳变，并伴随磁八极矩与自旋劈裂电导的显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索交变磁体能否出现不连续跳变（一阶相变），并寻找增强自旋劈裂效应（spin-splitter effect）的物理途径，以服务于自旋电子学应用。

Method: 采用扩展的二维双子晶格正方晶格 $t$-$J$ 模型的平均场理论，引入各向异性跃迁积分，研究磁序诱导的能带重构如何驱动自旋劈裂范霍夫奇点穿过费米面。

Result: 发现一阶元变磁相变产生一个中间交变磁态：交错磁化减弱，但磁八极矩和时间反演奇的自旋劈裂电导显著增强；一阶相变线终止于量子临界端点，随化学势与各向异性跃迁强度变化呈现丰富相图。

Conclusion: 自旋劈裂的范霍夫奇点是增强自旋劈裂效应的关键途径，为基于交变磁体的自旋电子学应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Altermagnets are collinear magnets with zero net magnetization and spin-split electronic bands. Here, we explore whether altermagnetism can exhibit a discontinuous jump, a phenomenon we refer to as meta-altermagnetism. We show that meta-altermagnetism can emerge in 2D systems when magnetic-order-induced band reconstruction drives spin-split van Hove singularities (vHSs) across the Fermi level. Using mean-field theory for an extended $t$-$J$ model on a two-sublattice square lattice including anisotropic hopping integral, we find that a first-order meta-altermagnetic transition produces an intermediate altermagnetic regime with reduced staggered magnetization but strongly enhanced magnetic octupole and time-reversal-odd spin-splitter conductivity. We further show that the first-order transition line terminates at a quantum critical endpoint, giving rise to a rich phase diagram as the chemical potential and anisotropic hopping strength are varied. Our results identify the spin split vHSs as a route to enhancing spin-splitter effect for spintronic applications.

</details>


### [105] [Field theory of competing phases in the interacting Kitaev chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37761)
*Pradip Kattel,Thierry Giamarchi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究一维相互作用拓扑超导体：通过玻色化与DMRG，刻画有限化学势下相互作用Kitaev链的相图，并给出拓扑-电荷序竞争、量子临界性及拓扑相的对偶对称破缺描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对Kitaev链的研究多局限于可精确求解的无相互作用或特殊格点极限，而有限密度下相互作用会非微扰地改变超导配对与公度电荷序之间的竞争。作者希望建立一般有限密度相互作用体系的连续场论描述，理解拓扑与公度性竞争及其引发的量子临界行为。

Method: 结合玻色化(bosonization)与密度矩阵重整化群(DMRG)数值计算。避开特殊可解格点极限，建立连续场论；分析相边界处的临界性(Pokrovsky-Talapov与Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless)；在半经典层次及自由费米子边界理论中分析拓扑相的马约拉纳边界模；并构造对偶连续费米子表示，将拓扑体相改写为常规对称破缺，从而得到SPT-SSB对应的连续版本。

Result: 相图包含：拓扑超导相、公度电荷密度波Mott相、二者之间的中间非公度Luttinger液体，以及大化学势下的平凡能带绝缘区。公度-非公度相边界由Pokrovsky-Talapov临界性支配，非公度-拓扑超导相边界由Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless临界性支配。拓扑相在半经典层次及自由费米子边界理论中支持指数局域的马约拉纳边缘模。对偶费米子表示中拓扑体相对应常规对称破缺，给出SPT-SSB对应的连续场论版本。

Conclusion: 提供了相互作用一维拓扑超导体从拓扑-公度性竞争与量子临界性到拓扑相对偶对称破缺描述的完整解析图像，深化了对一维相互作用拓扑超导体的理解。

Abstract: We study the interacting Kitaev chain with nearest-neighbor interactions and finite chemical potential using a combination of bosonization and density-matrix renormalization-group calculations. Focusing away from special exactly solvable lattice limits, we develop a continuum field-theoretic description of the generic finite-density interacting regime, where doping nonperturbatively modifies the competition between superconducting pairing and commensurate charge ordering. We show that the phase diagram consists of an intermediate incommensurate Luttinger liquid between the topological superconducting and commensurate charge-density-wave Mott phases, as well as a trivial band-insulating regime at large chemical potential. The commensurate--incommensurate and incommensurate--topological-superconducting phase boundaries are governed by Pokrovsky--Talapov and Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless criticality, respectively. We analyze the interacting topological superconducting regime and its boundary excitations. At the semiclassical level, and explicitly in the free-fermion boundary theory, the topological phase supports exponentially localized Majorana edge modes at interfaces between topological and trivial regions. We also construct a dual continuum fermionic representation in which this topological bulk sector is described instead by conventional symmetry breaking. This gives a continuum version of the SPT--SSB correspondence between different fermionic representations. Overall, our results provide an analytical description of one-dimensional interacting topological superconductors, from the competition between topology and commensurability and the resulting quantum criticality to the dual symmetry-breaking description of the topological phase.

</details>


### [106] [Long-Range Toric Code: Finite-Temperature Topological Order in Two Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37765)
*Jacopo Gliozzi,Taylor L. Hughes,Kyung-Su Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出带长程环相互作用的二维环面码模型，保留普通环面码的拓扑有序基态，同时在零温限域任意子；当相互作用足够长程时，限域在有限温度下持续存在，形成有限温度拓扑序，可作为自纠错量子存储器并展现长程纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通二维环面码缺乏有限温度下的稳健性，任意子在有限温度可自由移动从而破坏量子记忆。作者希望引入长程相互作用来限域任意子，从而在二维系统中实现有限温度拓扑序和自纠错量子存储。

Method: 在二维环面码基础上引入额外的长程环相互作用，保持环面码的拓扑有序基态不变，同时在零温限域任意子；通过论证不同相互作用范围下的热力学行为，考察限域在有限温度能否持续。

Result: 对于足够长程的相互作用，限域在有限温度下持续存在，强烈约束任意子移动，产生有限温度拓扑序，构成自纠错量子存储器并具有长程纠缠；对于较短程的相互作用，拓扑序的迹象在有限温度下仅能维持到指数级大的长度尺度，形成部分自纠错的量子存储器。

Conclusion: 通过长程环相互作用可以显著增强二维环面码在有限温度下的拓扑序稳健性，长程情形提供真正的自纠错量子存储，而短程情形仅提供部分自纠错能力。

Abstract: We introduce two-dimensional toric code models with additional long-range loop interactions. Our models preserve the topologically ordered ground states of the ordinary toric code, while confining anyons at zero temperature. For sufficiently long-ranged interactions, we argue that this confinement persists at finite temperatures, strongly constraining anyon mobility and giving rise to finite-temperature topological order. This topological phase acts as a self-correcting quantum memory and exhibits long-range entanglement. For shorter-ranged interactions, signatures of topological order survive only up to an exponentially large length scale at finite temperature, yielding a partially self-correcting quantum memory.

</details>


### [107] [Static Response of the Strongly Coupled Electron Gas from Diffusion Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40093)
*Muhammed H. Güneş,Yubo Yang,Vitaly Gorelov,Miguel A. Morales,Matteo Gatti,Lucia Reining,Shiwei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用扩散蒙特卡罗（DMC）方法，在液态金属密度范围（2≤r_s≤100）内计算了均匀电子气（HEG）的静态密度响应函数 χ(q) 和局域场因子 G(q)，解决了此前零温 G(q) 数据仅局限于金属密度（r_s≤10）的问题，并系统阐述了使这些计算得以实现的方法学要点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域场因子 G(q) 描述了均匀电子气中交换-关联如何修正对外界静态微扰的响应，并进入介电屏蔽、有效电子-电子相互作用以及含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）的交换-关联核。然而，此前精确的零温 G(q) 数据仅覆盖金属密度（r_s≤10），缺乏低密度（大 r_s）液体相的数据，限制了相关理论的发展与验证。

Method: 采用扩散蒙特卡罗（DMC）方法计算静态密度响应函数 χ(q) 与局域场因子 G(q)，覆盖 2≤r_s≤100。方法学上：优化试探波函数中的微扰势，给出其最优值的闭式表达式及线性响应窗口的密度依赖关系；证明回波（backflow）关联对 χ(q) 无影响，且 G(q) 在极低密度下对系统尺寸仅有弱依赖，故 Slater-Jastrow 波函数配合中等系统尺寸即可。此外，提出一个能在整个液相复现数据并可结合对关联函数与静态结构因子进行物理解释的 G(q) 参数化方案。

Result: 在 r_s=2 处与图蒙特卡罗结果一致；在金属密度且 q>k_F 时与以往 DMC 数据吻合，并将数据扩展到 q<k_F。在更低密度下，G(q) 在中等 q 区间出现非单调结构，响应趋近于无相互作用气体。与有限温路径积分蒙特卡罗结果定量一致，表明此前报道的有限温结构源自基态。参数化分析将该非单调结构与对关联函数中的短程有序及静态结构因子联系起来。

Conclusion: 通过优化微扰势并确认回波关联和有限尺寸效应影响微弱，本文确立了利用 Slater-Jastrow 波函数和中等系统尺寸即可精确计算电子气及真实材料响应性质的实用方法。所得 G(q) 在低密度的非单调结构源于基态短程有序，并与有限温结果一致，为电子气响应性质与 TDDFT 交换-关联核提供了可靠的零温参考数据。

Abstract: The local field factor $G(q)$ of the HEG describes how exchange and correlation modify the response to a static perturbation, and enters dielectric screening, effective electron--electron interactions, and the xc kernel of TDDFT. Accurate zero-temperature data for $G(q)$, however, remain limited to metallic densities, $r_s\leq10$. In this work, we present diffusion Monte Carlo calculations of the static density response function $χ(q)$ and of $G(q)$ for $2\leq r_s\leq100$, and describe and discuss the methodological steps that enabled these calculations. We show how optimization of the perturbing potential in the trial wave function has an important effect, as a poor choice can reverse the sign of the response. A closed-form expression is provided for its optimal value, together with the density dependence of the linear-response window. We further show that backflow correlations leave $χ(q)$ unchanged and that $G(q)$ depends only weakly on system size even at very low density, so that Slater--Jastrow wave functions and modest system sizes suffice. Together, these results provide a practical guide for future QMC calculations of response properties of the electron gas and real materials. Our results agree with previous DMC data at metallic densities for $q>k_F$, and extend them below $k_F$. They also agree with diagrammatic QMC at $r_s=2$. At lower densities, our results show that $G(q)$ develops a nonmonotonic structure at intermediate-$q$, where the response approaches that of the noninteracting gas. Quantitative agreement is seen with finite-temperature path integral Monte Carlo, suggesting that the previously reported finite-temperature structure is inherited from the ground state. Using a parametrization of $G(q)$ that reproduces our data throughout the liquid phase, we relate this structure to short-range order in the pair correlation function and the static structure factor.

</details>


### [108] [Spin Space Symmetry Without Spin Space Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37770)
*Jeffrey G. Rau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出直接利用自旋空间群的既约表示（defining representation）来代替构造和识别自旋空间群本身，从而把磁性结构允许的各种响应（磁补偿、压磁、自旋/电荷输运、Edelstein效应、动量空间自旋纹理等）的判据统一归结为空间群表示论问题，并通过表示的外幂（exterior powers）与多极表示的重叠给出简洁、层级化的选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋空间群在非相对论极限下对磁性结构提供完整对称性分类，但实际使用时需要构造并识别具体群，流程繁琐且掩盖了对称性约束的共性。作者希望绕过群的构造，直接在表示层面工作，以组织并简化对允许响应的对称性约束，并揭示不同物理响应之间的内在联系。

Method: 核心方法是用父空间群在自旋空间中的一个实表示（defining representation）来刻画自旋空间群（模掉自旋纯对称）。将各种物理响应允许性的判断转化为空间群表示论问题：考察定义表示的三个外幂与点群多极表示之间的重叠。外幂的波矢内容进一步给出层级化选择定则——均匀响应要求一个、两个或三个序波矢之和为倒格矢，从而在考虑点群对称性之前即可排除大量响应。文中用共线、共面和非共面磁有序材料的广泛实例加以说明。

Result: 不同物理响应的允许条件可简化为定义表示的同一条件，例如 p 波自旋纹理与时间偶 Edelstein 效应在相同条件下允许，压磁与补偿序中的 d 波自旋纹理亦然。外幂的波矢内容提供选择定则的层级：依相关阶次，均匀响应需一、二或三个序波矢求和为倒格矢，这些规则可在考虑点群对称性之前排除许多响应。

Conclusion: 直接在表示层面工作避免了对自旋空间群的构造与识别，同时组织和简化了各种允许响应的对称性约束，使磁补偿、压磁、输运、Edelstein效应及自旋纹理等的判定成为空间群表示论中的普通问题。文章还讨论了将结果推广到磁空间群、与共线序 Landau 理论的联系，以及奇宇称磁体的刻画。

Abstract: Spin space groups provide a complete symmetry classification of magnetic structures in the non-relativistic limit and have become an increasingly useful language for organizing magnetic phases and their physical properties. Each spin space group with a given parent space group is specified, up to spin-only symmetries, by a real representation of that space group acting in spin space. We argue that working directly with this representation not only avoids the construction and identification of the group, but also organizes and simplifies the resulting symmetry constraints on allowed responses. Determining the presence or absence of magnetic compensation, piezomagnetism, spin and charge transport, the Edelstein effect, and momentum-space spin textures becomes an ordinary problem in the representation theory of space groups. The resulting conditions reduce to overlaps between the three exterior powers of the defining representation and multipole representations of the point group. Several physically distinct responses then reduce to the same condition on the defining representation. For example, a $p$-wave spin texture and the time-even Edelstein effect are allowed under the same condition, as are piezomagnetism and a $d$-wave spin texture in compensated order. The wave-vector content of the exterior powers gives a further hierarchy of selection rules: depending on the relevant power, a uniform response requires one, two, or three ordering wave vectors to sum to a reciprocal lattice vector. These rules can exclude many responses before point-group symmetry is considered. We illustrate the approach with examples drawn from a wide variety of materials hosting collinear, coplanar and non-coplanar magnetic order. Finally, we discuss extensions of these results to magnetic space groups, connections to Landau theories of collinear order, and the characterization of odd-parity magnets.

</details>


### [109] [Evidence for a charged vortex lattice in an exciton superfluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37781)
*Ryan L. Lee,Jungwoo Lee,Viliam Vaňo,Bo Zou,Cheng-Li Chiu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Allan MacDonald,Ali Yazdani*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用扫描隧道显微镜在双层石墨烯激子凝聚体中观测到分数电荷涡旋-反涡旋晶格，验证了此前关于弱带电激子超流体在强磁场下稳定涡旋晶格的理论预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转超流体和磁场中的第二类超导体具有量子化涡旋晶格这一标志性特征，但在激子超流体（由电中性电子-空穴对构成）中实现类似涡旋晶格一直十分困难。此前理论预言，在强磁场下弱带电激子超流体可通过分数电荷涡旋核间的相互排斥稳定涡旋-反涡旋晶格，本研究旨在对这一预言进行实验验证。

Method: 在垂直磁场下，于两层石墨烯（由薄六方氮化硼势垒隔开）的朗道能级间形成双层激子凝聚体，并利用扫描隧道显微镜对其带电涡旋进行成像。

Result: 观测到涡旋晶格周期性与总过剩电荷成比例，且对电荷在两层间的分布不敏感，与理论预言定量一致。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了理论预言的涡旋-反涡旋晶格，为激子超流体系统中的相位相干性提供了证据。

Abstract: Lattices of quantized vortices are a defining property of rotating superfluids and of type-II superconductors in magnetic fields. Realizing an analogous vortex lattice in an exciton superfluid-formed from bound electron-hole pairs-has remained challenging because excitons are electrically neutral. Building on previous theoretical studies of the low-energy excitations in an exciton superfluid, recent theory has predicted that in a strong magnetic field, a weakly charged exciton superfluid can stabilize a vortex-antivortex lattice arising from the mutual repulsion of fractionally charged vortex cores. Here we report evidence for this predicted vortex lattice in a bilayer exciton condensate by imaging its charged vortices with the scanning tunnelling microscope. The condensate forms between Landau levels of two graphene layers separated by a thin hexagonal boron nitride barrier in a perpendicular magnetic field. We find that the vortex lattice periodicity scales with the total excess charge and is insensitive to its distribution between the two layers, in quantitative agreement with theoretical predictions. Our observations provide evidence for phase coherence in an exciton superfluid system.

</details>


### [110] [From a Lifshitz Transition to Spin-Density-Wave Order: Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ under Uniaxial Strain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38025)
*Evgeny A. Stepanov,Sophie Beck,Maria Chatzieleftheriou,Antoine Georges*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用先进多体理论框架研究单轴应变对Sr₂RuO₄的影响，发现应变诱导各向异性Lifshitz转变，增强沿拉伸轴方向的电子散射，同时在垂直方向促进相干费米液体行为；该各向异性调控了磁不稳定性，并揭示两个竞争波矢（γ片内及α-β片间嵌套矢量）可稳定磁序，其竞争取决于费米面片的相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来实验发现单轴应变显著提高Sr₂RuO₄的超导临界温度，并伴随涌现磁有序态的实验迹象，但缺乏深入的多体理论理解。作者旨在阐明应变如何影响该材料的光谱与磁学性质，特别是应变诱导磁不稳定性的微观机制。

Method: 采用先进的多体理论框架（advanced many-body theoretical framework），研究应变对Sr₂RuO₄光谱和磁学性质的影响；分析应变诱导的各向异性Lifshitz转变及其对电子散射率与相干费米液体行为的调控，并考察费米面嵌套矢量对磁响应和磁有序的贡献。

Result: 应变诱导各向异性Lifshitz转变，沿拉伸轴方向增强电子散射率，同时在垂直方向促进相干费米液体行为，与实验观测高度一致。该各向异性行为能够解释大应变下有限温度磁有序态的磁不稳定性。磁响应由两个竞争波矢主导：一个位于γ费米面片内，另一个位于α与β费米面片之间；两者均可稳定磁序，其竞争敏感依赖于相应费米面片的相干性。

Conclusion: 单轴应变通过各向异性Lifshitz转变调控Sr₂RuO₄的电子相干性与散射率，并由此驱动磁不稳定。两个竞争嵌套波矢（γ片内与α-β片间）共同决定磁序的稳定性，其优劣取决于费米面片相干性，为理解应变增强超导与磁态的关联提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In recent years, the effects of uniaxial strain on Sr$_2$RuO$_4$ have been extensively investigated, revealing a pronounced enhancement of the superconducting critical temperature accompanied by experimental indications of an emergent magnetically ordered state. In this Letter, we study the impact of strain on the spectral and magnetic properties of this material within an advanced many-body theoretical framework. We find that strain induces an anisotropic Lifshitz transition, which enhances the electron scattering rate along the elongation axis while simultaneously promoting coherent Fermi-liquid behavior in the perpendicular direction, in close agreement with experimental observations. Taking into account this anisotropic behavior allows us to capture the magnetic instability leading to a finite-temperature ordered state at large strain values. Our analysis further reveals that the magnetic response is dominated by two competing wave vectors associated with distinct nesting vectors: one within the $γ$ sheet and the other between the $α$ and $β$ sheets of the Fermi surface. Both wave vectors can stabilize magnetic order, with their competition depending sensitively on the coherence of the respective Fermi surface sheets.

</details>


### [111] [Level ordering in the linear $E \otimes e$ dynamic Jahn-Teller model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38190)
*Naoya Iwahara*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了线性 E⊗e 动态 Jahn-Teller 模型在任意耦合强度下，固定 j 子空间中第 n 个能级 Enj 随 |j| 严格递增，从而在数学上确认基态始终是 j=±1/2 的双重态，并给出了能隙的显式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，线性 E⊗e 动态 Jahn-Teller 模型的基态被认为对应振动角动量 j=±1/2 的双重态，但一直缺乏严格的数学证明。作者旨在以严格的证明取代这一仅凭数值或物理直觉得出的结论，并给出能级排序的定量刻画。

Method: 证明仅借助一个将 j 降低 1 的幺正变换，结合变分原理，从而在任意耦合强度下建立 Enj 随 |j| 的严格单调递增关系；并在此基础上给出基态能隙 E_{0,j+1}-E_{0j} 的显式下界。

Result: 对任意耦合强度及每个 n，证明 E_{n,1/2} < E_{n,3/2} < E_{n,5/2} < ...，即固定 j 子空间中的能级严格随 |j| 递增，故基态恒为 j=±1/2 双重态；同时获得能隙的显式下界。

Conclusion: 通过简洁的幺正变换加变分原理，作者严格确立了线性 E⊗e Jahn-Teller 模型基态为 j=±1/2 双重态这一长期猜测，并给出了能隙下界，为该模型的谱结构提供了严格的理论支撑。

Abstract: The ground level of the linear $E\otimes e$ dynamic Jahn-Teller model has long been believed to be the doublet with vibronic angular momentum $j = \pm\tfrac12$. We prove that, for arbitrary coupling strength, the $n$th level $E_{nj}$ in the subspace of fixed $j$ strictly increases with $|j|$ for every $n$, $E_{n,1/2} < E_{n,3/2} < E_{n,5/2} < \cdots$; in particular, the ground level is always the $j = \pm\tfrac12$ doublet. The proof uses only a unitary transformation that lowers $j$ by one, combined with the variational principle. We also give an explicit lower bound on the gap $E_{0,j+1} - E_{0j}$.

</details>


### [112] [Gravitational anomaly spectroscopy of a Kitaev spin liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38704)
*Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出把手性中心电荷 c 作为二维拓扑相边缘的谱学（光谱学）可观测量，通过调制边缘耦合并利用 Virasoro 代数提取吸收权重，从而无需热输运或纯基态即可测量引力反常，并在 Kitaev 蜂窝自旋液体等模型上以高精度验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性中心电荷 c 度量二维拓扑相的引力反常并决定边缘热流，但传统测量依赖热输运（受声子干扰）或波函数探针（需要纯基态），均存在困难。作者希望找到一种不受这些限制、可在任意热态下、仅通过边缘响应即可提取 c 的谱学方法，并能进一步确定非阿贝尔任意子的共形自旋。

Method: 对边缘耦合施加沿边缘传播的弱调制，其吸收由 Virasoro 代数描述；将吸收权重用同一耦合静态响应对应的平移归一化后，得到 D_n=2n⟨L_0⟩+(c/12)(n^3−n)，从而构造组合量 C≡2D_2−4D_1=c。再通过有无体块 vison 时线权之比 Λ=4h_σ/c 在无需标定的情况下确定非阿贝尔 Ising 任意子的共形自旋 h_σ。文中在 Kitaev 蜂窝自旋液体（7168 自旋）、固定通量扇区内相互作用边缘 Majorana（DMRG）、以及含原生 Floquet 线路的量子处理器协议的精确模拟上进行了验证。

Result: 在 7168 个自旋的 Kitaev 蜂窝自旋液体上得到 C=0.49999、h_σ=0.06250；用测量线自身的位移标定后，96 个自旋即可把 c 测到约 1% 精度，且在 20% 随机耦合误差下仍保持约 1% 精度；固定通量扇区中相互作用的边缘 Majorana（DMRG 处理）在边缘速度变化 ±13% 时 C 仍保持在 1.5% 以内；含原生 Floquet 线路的量子处理器协议的精确模拟在 96–600 个自旋上以 3.5% 以内精度返回 c。

Conclusion: 手性中心电荷 c 可以从边缘谱学中直接提取，即便 c 从激发能隙中抵消，它仍体现在跃迁权重中。该方法无需纯基态、不受声子干扰，并可用作无需标定的非阿贝尔任意子共形自旋探针，为在现实材料与量子处理器上验证拓扑序和引力反常提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The chiral central charge $c$ measures the gravitational anomaly of a two-dimensional topological phase. It fixes the heat current carried along the edge. So far, it has been accessed through thermal transport, where phonons complicate the signal, or through wave-function probes that need a pure ground state. Here we show that $c$ is a spectroscopic quantity of the edge. A weak modulation of the edge couplings that travel along the edge is absorbed according to the Virasoro algebra. Normalized by the response to a static change of the same couplings, the absorbed weights obey $D_n=2n\langle L_0\rangle+\frac{c}{12}(n^3-n)$, so that $\mathcal{C}\equiv2D_2-4D_1=c$ in any state of the ideal edge theory, thermal or not. The central charge thus appears in transition weights, although it cancels from excitation gaps. The ratio of line weights with and without a vison in the bulk, $Λ=4h_σ/c$, needs no calibration and fixes the conformal spin $h_σ$ of the non-Abelian Ising anyon. For the Kitaev honeycomb spin liquid we find $\mathcal{C}=0.49999$ and $h_σ=0.06250$ on 7168 spins. Calibrating with the shift of the measured line itself gives $c$ within about $1\%$ on 96 spins and within $1\%$ under $20\%$ random coupling errors. Interacting edge Majoranas in a fixed flux sector, treated with DMRG, keep $\mathcal{C}$ within $1.5\%$ while the edge velocity changes by $\pm13\%$. An exact simulation of a quantum-processor protocol, including the native Floquet circuit, returns $c$ within $3.5\%$ on 96 to 600 spins.

</details>


### [113] [Mixed-State Topological Transition and Emergent Fractionalized Supersymmetry in the Non-Abelian Kitaev Spin Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38741)
*Zhou-Quan Wan,Hong Yao,Shao-Kai Jian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究由键退相干（bond-dephasing）通道驱动的非阿贝尔 Kitaev 自旋液体中的混合态拓扑相变，通过无符号问题的量子蒙特卡罗模拟揭示了 Choi 态中 Ising⊠Ising 到环面码序的转变，并在时间界面上发现退相干驱动的混合态拓扑相变处涌现 N=1 超对称，临界指数与精确超对称值一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究退相干（开放系统噪声）如何影响非阿贝尔拓扑自旋液体的混合态拓扑序，特别是理解混合态拓扑相变的普适类及其是否能在临界点涌现对称性（如超对称），并为未来的实验观测提供具体可测的物理量。

Method: 采用向量化（Choi-Jamiołkowski）框架，将键退相干通道映射为两套时间反演的分馏 Majorana 费米子系统与带隙 Z2 规范场的耦合，从而消除符号问题，实现无符号问题的量子蒙特卡罗模拟；并在拓扑态与平庸态之间的时间界面上，利用 Chern 数失配引入无能隙 Majorana 模式，研究退相干驱动的混合态拓扑相变。

Result: 增大退相干会凝聚副本间（inter-copy）费米子对，使 Choi 态从 Ising⊠Ising 序转变为环面码（toric-code）序，展开后对应内禀混合态拓扑序；在时间界面上，退相干驱动的混合态拓扑相变处无能隙 Majorana 模式涌现出 N=1 超对称；蒙特卡罗给出临界指数 ν=1.27(3)，反常维数 ηb≃ηf≃0.40，与精确超对称值 ν=5/4、η=2/5 一致；超对称伙伴是分馏化的，物理上表现为不同的规范不变自旋弦。

Conclusion: 退相干可以在非阿贝尔 Kitaev 自旋液体中诱发内禀混合态拓扑相变，并涌现出 N=1 超对称，其临界指数与精确超对称值吻合；分馏化的超对称伙伴以规范不变自旋弦的形式呈现，为未来实验检验提供了具体可观测对象。

Abstract: We study a mixed-state topological transition in the non-Abelian Kitaev spin liquid driven by a bond-dephasing channel. In the vectorized picture, bond dephasing maps onto interactions between two time-reversed fractionalized Majorana systems coupled to gapped $\mathbb Z_2$ gauge fields, enabling sign-problem-free Monte Carlo simulations. We show that increasing dephasing condenses an inter-copy fermion pair, converting ${\rm Ising}\boxtimes\overline{\rm{Ising}}$ to toric-code order in the Choi state and yielding intrinsically mixed-state topological order upon unfolding. More interestingly, at a temporal interface between topological and trivial Kitaev states, a Chern-number mismatch enforces a gapless Majorana mode. Dephasing then drives a mixed-state topological transition at which the gapless mode develops emergent $\mathcal N=1$ supersymmetry. Our Monte Carlo simulations yield $ν=1.27(3)$ and $η_b\simeqη_f\simeq0.40$, consistent with the exact supersymmetric values $5/4$ and $2/5$. The supersymmetric partners are fractionalized and appear physically as distinct gauge-invariant spin strings, providing concrete observables for future experiments.

</details>


### [114] [Magnetism in a homometallic square-planar Cu$({\mathrm{II})}$$_4$Cu$({\mathrm{II})}$ metallacrown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38989)
*Luca Bischof,Ariyan Tavakoli,Sascha Pietruska,Lena Spillecke,Ina Heckelmann,Changhyun Koo,Luca M. Carrella,Eva Rentschler,Hans Christian Schneider,Rüdiger Klingeler*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过对水杨基羟肟酸配体引入大体积取代基，合成了金属冠Cu4Cu，抑制分子间磁相互作用，并结合静态磁化率、EPR谱与基于破缺对称的DFT计算，系统研究了其本征自旋结构、交换耦合常数（J1、J2）及S=1/2基态的磁学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的同金属金属冠Cu(II)[12-MC-4]体系为研究交换耦合Cu(II)团簇磁性提供了理想平台，但分子间磁相互作用会干扰对固有基态自旋结构和交换耦合的精确表征。作者希望通过引入大体积取代基修饰配体，有效抑制分子间相互作用，从而细致探究该体系的内在磁行为。

Method: 合成大体积取代基修饰的金属冠配合物Cu4Cu；通过静态磁化率测量确定反铁磁交换耦合常数；利用X波段和高频/高场（HF-HF）EPR精确测定g因子；采用基于破缺对称性的DFT进行从头算，验证实验得到的交换耦合与g因子；结合磁化数据与EPR激发态信号研究双态-四重态能隙。

Result: 测得中心-外围位点间的反铁磁交换耦合J1=-33.5(8) meV、环上相邻位点间J2=-18.2(5) meV，与DFT计算吻合；低温下呈现S=1/2基态，g因子为gx=2.028、gy=2.056、gz=2.222，近似轴对称且与DFT一致；基态自旋构型以未占据态的dxy特征为标志；磁化数据表明双态-四重态能隙Δ(1/2→3/2)=4.8(4) THz，EPR激发态信号亦印证该跃迁。

Conclusion: 通过引入大体积取代基成功抑制分子间磁相互作用，实现对该金属冠体系本征基态自旋结构、交换耦合及能级跃迁的精确表征，实验与从头算DFT结果相互印证，为交换耦合Cu(II)团簇的磁性研究提供了实验与理论方法相结合的范式。

Abstract: The well-established homometallic metallacrown Cu(II)[12-MC$_\text{Cu(II)N(Shi)}$-4]$^{2-}$, comprising four square-planarly arranged Cu(II) ions surrounding a slightly off-plane central Cu(II) ion, provides an excellent platform for investigating magnetism in exchange-coupled Cu(II) clusters both by experimental and ab initio numerical methods. In the present work, sterically bulky substituents are introduced into the salicylhydroxamate ligand to obtain a modified derivative, Cu$({\mathrm{II})}$[12-MC$_\text{Cu$({\mathrm{II})}$N(L)}$-4]$^{2-}$ (Cu$_4$Cu), thereby effectively suppressing intermolecular magnetic interactions and enabling a detailed investigation of its intrinsic ground-state spin structure and exchange interactions. Static magnetization reveals competing antiferromagnetic exchange interactions of $J_1=-8.1(2)$ THz ($-33.5(8)$ meV) between the central and the peripheral sites and $J_2=-4.40(12)$ THz ($-18.2(5)$ meV) between neighboring sites on the ring of the metallacrown, which are corroborated by ab initio broken symmetry-based density functional theory (DFT). These couplings yield an $S=1/2$ ground state for Cu$_4$Cu at low temperatures, for which X-band and high-frequency/high-field electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) precisely determine the $g$-factors $g_x = 2.028$, $g_y = 2.056$, and $g_z = 2.222$ close to axial anisotropy and in agreement with our DFT results. The ground-state spin-configuration is characterized by $d_{xy}$-character of the unoccupied states. Furthermore, our magnetization data evidence thermal transitions across a doublet-to-quartet gap of $Δ_{1/2\rightarrow3/2} = 4.8(4)$ THz which is also indicated by the emergence of an excited-state EPR signal.

</details>


### [115] [Atomic Charge Chirality from Electric Multipoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39150)
*Satoru Hayami,Rikuto Oiwa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文建立了局部原子电荷分布手性的电多极（$Q_{lm}$）判据，识别出11个晶体学手性点群表征左右手性所需的最低阶多极成分，并指出在无自旋的 s-p-d-f 原子基组内标量电荷密度最高只能到六阶多极，因而无法编码 $C_6$、$D_6$ 和 $O$ 的手性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要从微观层面理解晶体学手性如何编码在局部原子电荷分布中，即建立电荷多极成分与手性之间的定量联系，从而为在原子尺度识别和区分左右手性提供判据。

Method: 作者提出了电多极（$Q_{lm}$）手性判据：对于11个晶体学手性点群逐一确定区分对映手性所需的最低阶多极成分。对轴性群 $C_n$ 与 $D_n$（$n=2,3,4,6$），指出手性来源于两个具有相同 $m=n$、但分别属于相邻秩 $l=n$ 和 $l=n+1$ 的方位角各向异性之间的相对取向，其共存产生随纬度扭转的 n 重电荷分布；并说明极性分量尽管在 $C_n$ 中被允许，但与这一手性机制不同且并非判定手性所必需。对立方群 $T$ 与 $O$ 则识别出不同的实现方式，其中八面体手性首次出现于九阶多极。最后检验了无自旋 s-p-d-f 基组下标量电荷密度所能含有的最高多极阶数。

Result: 对11个晶体学手性点群给出了各自的代表性最低阶多极成分。$C_n$ 和 $D_n$（$n=2,3,4,6$）的手性由 $l=n$ 与 $l=n+1$ 两个相邻秩、同方位角量子数 $m=n$ 的分量相对取向决定；立方群 $T$ 与 $O$ 具有不同实现，且 $O$ 的八面体手性最低出现在九阶多极。在无自旋 s-p-d-f 原子基组内，电荷密度多极最高仅到六阶，故无法编码 $C_6$、$D_6$、$O$ 的手性。

Conclusion: 电多极成分与原子轨道自由度共同决定了晶体学手性在微观层面的编码方式；该判据揭示了哪些点群的手性可由给定原子轨道基组内的标量电荷密度表示，哪些不能，为理解并设计手性电荷分布提供了原则性依据。

Abstract: We establish an electric-multipole ($Q_{lm}$) criterion for chirality of local atomic charge distributions. For the 11 crystallographic chiral point groups, we identify representative lowest-rank multipole contents required to distinguish the opposite handednesses. For the axial groups $C_n$ and $D_n$ with $n=2,3,4,6$, chirality arises from the relative orientation of two azimuthal anisotropies with $m=n$, belonging to consecutive ranks $l=n$ and $n+1$. Their coexistence produces a latitude-dependent twist of the $n$-fold charge distribution. The polar component, although allowed in $C_n$, is distinct from this chiral mechanism and is not required for handedness. The cubic groups $T$ and $O$ exhibit distinct realizations, with octahedral chirality first appearing at rank nine. We further show that, within a spinless $s$-$p$-$d$-$f$ atomic basis, the scalar charge density contains multipoles only up to rank six and therefore cannot encode the handedness of $C_6$, $D_6$, or $O$. These results connect electric-multipole content and atomic orbital degrees of freedom to the microscopic encoding of crystallographic handedness.

</details>


### [116] [A Zoo of Fermionic Relative Rényi Negativities in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39349)
*Linjing Jiang,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一类"相对 Rényi 负性"作为混合态量子纠缠的见证（entanglement witness），并通过将费米子部分时间反演与 size 超算符相联系，建立其路径积分表示。作者在 SYK 模型及其推广（Maldacena-Qi 耦合 SYK 模型）的热态中，用大 N 展开高效计算这一族量，发现一类可作为对数负性的合理代理（proxy），并揭示了对简单观测量不可见的一阶纠缠相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联费米子系统中的量子纠缠刻画在混合态下仍具挑战：成熟诊断量如对数负性（logarithmic negativity）在相互作用系统中计算代价高昂，精确对角化只能处理小尺寸；而现有的 Rényi 代理（如 twisted/untwisted Rényi negativities）即使对可分态也可能取正值，无法可靠地见证纠缠。因此亟需一类既可作为有效纠缠见证、又能在强关联模型和大 N 框架下可计算的新诊断量。

Method: 作者引入一族"相对 Rényi 负性"（relative Rényi negativities）作为纠缠见证。核心手段是将费米子部分时间反演（fermionic partial time reversal）与 size 超算符（size superoperator）联系起来，从而为这些 Rényi 负性建立路径积分表示；再利用大 N 展开，在 SYK 模型及 Maldacena-Qi 耦合 SYK 模型的热态中高效求值。通过比较不同参数下的相对 Rényi 负性，识别出一类可作为对数负性代理的特殊构造，并考察其随温度/耦合参数的相变行为。

Result: 该方法成功在原始 SYK 模型和 Maldacena-Qi 耦合 SYK 模型中计算了相对 Rényi 负性。研究发现一类特殊的相对 Rényi 负性可作为对数负性的合理代理；并揭示出"一阶纠缠相变"（first-order entanglement transitions），这些相变对简单观测量（如能量、常规关联函数等）不可见，换言之只有通过这类 Rényi 负性才能探测到。结果暗示这些负性可以扮演类似自由能的角色。

Conclusion: 相对 Rényi 负性是强关联费米子系统混合态纠缠的有效探针：它们既可作为真正的纠缠见证，又能在 SYK 类模型的大 N 框架下可计算，并成功充当对数负性的代理。所发现的一阶纠缠相变对简单观测量不可见，提示存在一类以这些负性作为自由能的新相变。该工作为强关联费米系统的混合态纠缠刻画提供了新工具和新视角。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is a fundamental resource that gives rise to correlations with no classical counterpart in strongly correlated fermionic systems. However, its characterization in mixed quantum states remains challenging. Established entanglement diagnostics, such as logarithmic negativity, are computationally demanding in interacting systems, and their evaluation by exact diagonalization is restricted to small system sizes. Meanwhile, conventional Rényi proxies, including twisted and untwisted Rényi negativities, can be positive even for separable states. In this work, we introduce a family of \textit{relative Rényi negativities} that serve as entanglement witnesses. By relating fermionic partial time reversal to the size superoperator, we establish a path-integral representation for these Rényi negativities, enabling their evaluation in thermal states of the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model and its generalizations via a large-$N$ expansion. Applying this method, we investigate entanglement in both the original SYK model and the Maldacena-Qi coupled SYK model. We identify a special class of relative Rényi negativities which serve as plausible proxies for logarithmic negativity and uncover first-order entanglement transitions that are invisible to simple observables. These results highlight the utility of relative Rényi negativities as probes of mixed-state entanglement in strongly correlated fermionic systems and suggest the existence of a new class of transitions where these negativities serve as free energies.

</details>


### [117] [High-Field Heat Capacity of Atacamite Cu$_2$Cl(OH)$_3$ for $\mathbf{H} \parallel b$ axis: From Antiferromagnetic Order to Correlated Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39669)
*L. Stödter,A. Demuer,T. Kotte,A. U. B. Wolter,B. Büchner,K. C. Rule,S. Süllow*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文测量了阻挫锯齿链化合物氯铜矿（atacamite, Cu₂Cl(OH)₃）在磁场平行于 b 轴、最高达 35 T 时的比热，观察到随磁场增强从反铁磁长程序转变为场致自旋液体态，并在最高场下出现宽比热异常，符合自旋能隙热激活行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究阻挫锯齿链化合物氯铜矿在强磁场下的低温热力学行为，以验证此前针对 H∥c 轴提出的高场态图像，即锯齿链退耦为基底与顶端两套自旋子系统并形成部分极化态（M≈Msat/2），并进一步理解磁场驱动的量子相变与自旋液体态。

Method: 对氯铜矿单晶在 H∥b 轴、磁场高达 35 T 的条件下测量比热随温度的变化，通过分析转变异常的移动、宽峰异常以及低温比热的指数行为，提取自旋能隙并判定磁有序与自旋液体态之间的转变。

Result: 观察到一个尖锐的转变异常随磁场增大向低温移动，对应反铁磁长程序；磁场继续升高后该异常消失，表明进入场致自旋液体态；在 32 T 以上出现宽比热异常并随磁场向高温移动。高场区低温比热可用指数函数很好描述，表明存在自旋能隙上的热激活。

Conclusion: 实验结果与此前针对 H∥c 轴提出的高场态图像一致，即锯齿链退耦为基底和顶端两类自旋子系统，形成 M≈Msat/2 的部分极化态；宽比热异常及其指数低温行为印证了高场下自旋液体态中存在的自旋能隙。

Abstract: We present the heat capacity of the frustrated sawtooth-chain compound atacamite Cu$_2$Cl(OH)$_3$ in high magnetic fields up to 35 T for $\mathbf{H} \parallel b$ axis. We clearly observe the change from antiferromagnetic long-range order, indicated by a sharp transition anomaly shifted to lower temperatures when increasing field, to a field-induced spin-liquid-like state with the absence of any transition anomaly. Here, in highest magnetic fields above 32 T a broad anomaly is present which is shifted to higher temperatures as the field is increased. The low-temperature heat capacity in the high-field region can be described well by an exponential behavior indicating a thermal activation over a spin gap. The findings are consistent with our scenario for the high-field state in atacamite for $\mathbf{H} \parallel c$ axis, where we proposed the sawtooth chains to decouple into two spin subsystems, basal and apical spin sites, leading to a partially polarized state with $M \sim M_\mathrm{sat}/2$.

</details>


### [118] [Phonon Fluctuations Enhanced Superconductivity in a Three-Band Cuprate Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39710)
*Jiarui Liu,Yao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了三带铜氧化物模型中 B_{1g} 氧屈曲声子对高温超导的微观影响，发现其作用取决于声子处于绝热还是反绝热区：绝热声子抑制 d 波配对，反绝热声子增强配对的相干性与吸引顶点，且存在电子-空穴不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物高温超导的微观起源仍是未解难题，实验与理论均提示声子（尤其是 B_{1g} 氧屈曲模式）可能起关键作用，但声子与电子关联如何协同影响超导配对尚不清楚。

Method: 在三带铜氧化物模型中，针对 B_{1g} 氧屈曲声子，分别考察绝热区（热激发占据）与反绝热区两种情形，通过多个诊断指标分析其对准粒子相干性、d 波吸引配对顶点及超导配对强度的影响。

Result: 发现热激发的绝热声子通过破坏准粒子相干性抑制 d 波配对；反绝热声子则同时增强相干性和 d 波吸引配对顶点，从而在多项诊断中强化超导。这种增强呈现明显的电子-空穴不对称性，源于掺杂载流子不同的轨道特征。

Conclusion: 本文建立了 B_{1g} 声子与电子关联协同、显著增强铜氧化物 d 波超导配对的微观机制。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in cuprates remains an outstanding puzzle due to the interplay of intertwined orders. Experimental observations and theoretical studies have suggested crucial roles of phonons, with the $B_{1g}$ oxygen buckling mode emerging as a promising candidate. Here, we investigate the microscopic influence of this phonon in a three-band cuprate model. We find that the $B_{1g}$ phonon plays opposite roles in superconductivity depending on whether it lies in the adiabatic or antiadiabatic regime at a given temperature. Thermally populated adiabatic phonons suppress $d$-wave pairing through the loss of quasiparticle coherence, whereas antiadiabatic phonons simultaneously enhance coherence and the attractive $d$-wave pairing vertex, strengthening superconductivity across multiple diagnostics. This enhancement also exhibits pronounced electron-hole asymmetry arising from the different orbital characters of doped carriers. Our results therefore establish a microscopic mechanism through which $B_{1g}$ phonons can cooperate with electronic correlations to substantially enhance $d$-wave superconducting pairing in cuprates.

</details>


### [119] [Monte Carlo simulation of mixed spin-orbit ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39943)
*Yu. D. Panov,V. A. Ulitko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用准经典蒙特卡洛方法（热浴算法）研究了磁场对具有最大纠缠的Kugel-Khomskii模型热力学性质的影响，发现外加磁场会同时诱导出非零磁化强度和轨道有序，且二者出现在布里渊区中自旋-轨道关联非零的同一位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究外加磁场如何影响Kugel-Khomskii模型（描述自旋-轨道耦合的强关联电子体系）的热力学性质，特别是自旋、轨道自由度在最大纠缠条件下的响应行为以及磁场对序参量的诱导作用。

Method: 采用准经典蒙特卡洛方法（quasi-classical Monte Carlo），具体使用热浴算法（heat bath algorithm）进行数值模拟，计算不同温度下单格点关联函数（single-site correlators）以刻画模型的热力学行为。

Result: 温度依赖的单格点关联函数表明：施加磁场会导致体系产生非零磁化强度；同时在同一布里渊区位置出现轨道有序，该位置恰为自旋-轨道关联函数（spin-orbit correlator）非零之处。即磁场同时诱导自旋序与轨道序，且二者在动量空间位置上与自旋-轨道关联的峰重合。

Conclusion: 在具有最大纠缠的Kugel-Khomskii模型中，外加磁场可作为同时调控自旋和轨道自由度的有效手段，磁化与轨道有序在自旋-轨道关联主导的布里渊区位置协同出现，揭示了该模型中自旋与轨道自由度的强耦合与纠缠特性。

Abstract: The effect of a magnetic field on the thermodynamic properties of the Kugel-Khomskii model with maximal entanglement using the quasi-classical Monte Carlo method with the heat bath algorithm. The temperature dependencies of single-site correlators show that the application of a magnetic field results in non-zero magnetization and the simultaneous appearance of an orbital ordering at the same point in the Brillouin zone where a non-zero spin-orbit correlator exists.

</details>


### [120] [Electronic liquid crystals and superconductivity from doping a moiré Mott insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39978)
*Yichi Zhang,Yiyu Xia,Zhongdong Han,Jekwan Lee,Brandon Bauer,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Wenjin Zhao,Kin Fai Mak,Jie Shan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在掺杂的莫尔Mott绝缘体（扭曲双层WSe2）中普遍观测到条纹相，并发现从条纹相到超导态的量子相变，且该转变表现出自相似性，揭示两种序之间的紧密联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子液晶（条纹相、向列相）被认为是掺杂Mott绝缘体中短程与长程库仑相互作用折衷的结果，并与高温超导的涌现密切相关（竞争序或交织序）。莫尔半导体为在Mott绝缘体附近探索条纹相与超导的关系提供了可控平台。

Method: 采用电输运测量研究扭曲双层WSe2。通过观测从高温各向同性相到低温各向异性相的热力学相变及伴随的费米面重构来揭示条纹相；并在条纹到超导转变附近捕捉额外的热力学相变（金属性条纹到钉扎绝缘条纹）。通过标度/自相似性分析研究条纹到超导量子相变。

Result: 在掺杂莫尔Mott绝缘体中观察到条纹相的普遍出现；条纹相表现为伴随费米面重构的热力学相变；发现从条纹相到超导的量子相变，其附近还存在金属条纹到钉扎绝缘条纹的额外热力学相变；条纹-超导转变展现出独特的自相似性。

Conclusion: 该工作为在莫尔材料中研究交织序（intwined orders）铺平了道路，表明条纹与超导序之间存在内在紧密联系。

Abstract: Electronic liquid crystals, such as stripe phases and nematics, are self-organized states of matter with spontaneously broken rotational symmetry. They are expected to emerge in a doped Mott insulator as a compromise between short- and long-range Coulomb interactions, and are believed to be intimately connected to the emergence of high temperature superconductivity either as a competing order or an intertwined order. Moiré semiconductors provide a controllable platform to explore the relationship between stripe phases and superconductivity near a Mott insulator. Here we report the generic appearance of stripe phases in a doped moiré Mott insulator and the observation of a stripe to-superconductor quantum phase transition in twisted bilayer WSe2. The stripe phases were revealed by electrical transport studies as a thermodynamic phase transition from a high temperature isotropic phase to a low-temperature anisotropic phase accompanied by a Fermi surface reconstruction. Near the stripe-to-superconductor transition, we also observed an additional thermodynamic phase transition from metallic stripes to pinned insulating stripes. The stripe-to-superconductor transition further exhibits an intriguing self-similarity that highlights the intimate relationship between the two orders. Our results pave the path for studying intertwined orders in moiré materials.

</details>


### [121] [Adiabatic Variational Principle: Born-Oppenheimer Theory for Emergent Electronic-Order Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40117)
*Yang Yang,Ali Rayat,Ho Jang,Yunhao Fan,Sankha Subhra Bakshi,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出绝热变分原理（AVP），从相互作用多体哈密顿量出发，在微观弛豫快于序参量演化的绝热假设下，直接推导出序参量动力学方程，为相场类模拟提供了与具体求解器无关的微观基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的微观多体方法已能高精度描述平衡量子态，但针对涌现序（如电荷密度波、自旋密度波）的大尺度非平衡动力学模拟，通常仍依赖带人为预设自由能泛函的唯象方程，缺乏从多体哈密顿量直接推导的微观基础。

Method: 作者引入绝热变分原理（AVP）：假设微观弛豫远快于序参量演化，使多体态绝热地跟随集体构型（类比玻恩-奥本海默近似）。每个瞬时序参量构型定义一个受约束的准稳态多体态，约束对应的拉格朗日乘子直接给出驱动集体演化的热力学力；并利用非线性 Mori-Zwanzig 投影理论将可逆动力学与集体变量的微观代数联系起来，同时把耗散和涨落归因于被消除的自由度。

Result: 将该框架应用于电荷密度波与自旋密度波动力学，成功恢复了 Allen-Cahn 畴生长行为，能够在解析演化畴网络的同时给出其微观电子结构，并导出了巡游磁性的广义 Landau-Lifshitz 动力学方程。

Conclusion: AVP 为涌现电子序的非平衡动力学相场类模拟提供了一种与具体求解器无关的微观基础，弥合了唯象方程与多体微观理论之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Recent advances in microscopic many-body methods have enabled increasingly accurate descriptions of equilibrium quantum states, yet large-scale simulations of emergent-order dynamics typically rely on phenomenological equations with prescribed free-energy functionals. We introduce the Adiabatic Variational Principle (AVP), a framework for deriving order-parameter dynamics directly from an interacting many-body Hamiltonian. When microscopic relaxation is fast compared with order-parameter evolution, the many-body state follows the collective configuration adiabatically, in analogy with the Born--Oppenheimer separation of electronic and nuclear motion. This assumption makes explicit the adiabatic closure implicit in conventional phase-field descriptions. Each instantaneous order-parameter configuration then defines a constrained quasi-stationary many-body state, and the Lagrange multipliers enforcing these constraints directly provide the thermodynamic forces driving the collective evolution. Nonlinear Mori--Zwanzig projection theory further connects the reversible dynamics to the microscopic algebra of the collective variables and relates dissipation and fluctuations to the eliminated degrees of freedom. Applications to charge- and spin-density-wave dynamics recover Allen--Cahn domain growth, resolve evolving domain networks together with their microscopic electronic structure, and yield generalized Landau--Lifshitz dynamics for itinerant magnetism. The AVP thus provides a solver-independent microscopic foundation for phase-field-like simulations of the nonequilibrium dynamics of emergent electronic order.

</details>


### [122] [Investigation of perovskite related oxides: structural and magnetic properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40139)
*Antara Sarkar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究钙钛矿材料中稀土(R)与过渡金属(TM)离子间相互作用与其结构的关系，重点关注取代、结构畸变和轨道/无序效应对磁性能（居里温度、自旋玻璃转变、自旋重取向、超交换与双交换作用）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿具有多样的结构并表现出结构依赖的磁学性质，在磁电多铁、磁卡效应及自旋电子学器件中具有重要应用前景。因此有必要系统研究不同结构中稀土与过渡金属离子相互作用的本质。

Method: 通过对钙钛矿A位或B位进行稀土(R)与过渡金属(TM)离子的取代，调控离子间相互作用程度与结构性质，观察由此导致的居里温度、自旋玻璃转变和自旋重取向转变等磁性行为的变化，并分析轨道相互作用、无序及超交换/双交换机制在磁转变中的作用。

Result: 不同取代会引起磁相互作用改变并产生结构畸变；观察到自旋玻璃转变、自旋重取向转变以及不同居里温度；确定了过渡金属与稀土的交换作用、磁各向异性、轨道取向及无序诱导相互作用对磁性的影响，并揭示超交换与双交换作用随取代的变化。

Conclusion: 钙钛矿的磁学性质强烈依赖于其结构以及稀土与过渡金属离子间的相互作用；A/B位取代通过引入结构畸变和轨道/无序效应，显著调控超交换与双交换相互作用，从而改变居里温度、自旋玻璃及自旋重取向等磁转变行为。

Abstract: Perovskites exist in varied structures and show structure dependent magnetic properties which is of great interest in the field of multiferroics, magnetocaloric and spintronic devices. Here we intend to study the various rare-earth(R) and transition metal (TM) ion interactions depending on their structures. Different Curie temperature, spinglass transition and spin reorientation transitions were observed depending on the degree of interactions and structural properties. It is interesting to study the role of orbital interactions, disorder in magnetic transitions leading to change in superexchange and double exchange interaction. On substitution at A-site or B-site results in change in magnetic interactions, as well as creates structural distortions. We also obtained results where the spin interaction of the transition metal and rare-earth interaction, magnetic anisotropy, orientations of orbitals, disorder induced interactions were observed.

</details>


### [123] [Topological Quantum Spin Liquids in a Ladder with Majorana fermions and p-wave Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40297)
*Magali Korolev,Fan Yang,Erik S. Sørensen,Karyn Le Hur*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究梯子系统中只具有短程自旋关联的拓扑量子自旋液体，通过将二维Kitaev六角蜂窝模型推广到带状几何的可精确求解模型，发现基态中涌现出静态Z₂规范场（携带0或π通量的Majorana费米子），并在相图中呈现出三个有自旋能隙的自旋液体相和一个Majorana费米子意义上的金属相，同时揭示了有能隙相中边缘受保护零能Majorana费米子的体-边对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在梯子系统中是否能实现整个相图内仅有短程自旋关联的拓扑量子自旋液体，并寻找具有π通量等性质的拓扑量子自旋模型的可行解途径。

Method: 将二维Kitaev六角蜂窝自旋模型推广为带状（ribbon）几何，得到可精确求解的梯子模型；基态中通过Majorana费米子静态地涌现出Z₂规范场（产生0或π通量）；综合运用量子场论视角、数学方法与密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）数值结果，通过遍历整个相图的观测量对该模型进行分类。

Result: 相图包含三个具有自旋能隙的自旋液体相，以及一个Majorana费米子意义上的金属相；提出体-边对应关系，显示在有能隙相中边缘存在受保护的零能Majorana费米子，而在拓扑液体相的体态中存在巡游Majorana费米子；该方法能区分砖墙梯子（产生特殊线）与方块梯子（允许无能隙Majorana液体相）的性质。

Conclusion: 该模型为在正方晶格几何上寻找拓扑量子自旋模型（如π通量情形）的解带来了希望；该方法还能与拓扑超导模型建立对应关系，为研究拓扑量子自旋液体及相关体系提供了新的可解框架。

Abstract: In this article, we address the possibility of topological quantum spin liquids with only short-range (spin) correlations in the whole phase diagram in a ladder system, through an exactly solvable model which represents a generalization of the two-dimensional Kitaev honeycomb spin model to a ribbon geometry. This system gives rise to $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge fields emerging in a static way in the ground state through Majorana fermions which then produce either $0$ or $π$ flux. Through a quantum field theory perspective, mathematical and Density Matrix Renormalization Group results, we present our progress on the geometrical, topological and physical classification of this model through observables navigating on the whole phase diagram presenting three spin-liquid phases with a spin gap and a metallic phase in the sense of Majorana fermions. We propose a bulk-edge correspondence revealing the presence of protected zero-energy Majorana fermions at the edge in the phases with a gap and revealing also the presence of itinerant Majorana fermions in the bulk in the topological liquid phase. The proposed approach allows us to distinguish between properties of the brickwall ladder giving rise to a special line and those of the square ladder allowing for the gapless Majorana liquid phase. This model gives some hope on finding solutions for topological quantum spin models, e.g. with $π$ flux, on a square lattice geometry. This approach also allows for a correspondence onto models of topological superconductivity.

</details>


### [124] [Cage-related crystal-field effects in the paramagnetic range of PrB$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40176)
*Mehdi Amara,Christine Opagiste,Natalya Yu. Shitsevalova*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过比热、热膨胀和磁性测量，揭示了PrB₆在50 K以下顺磁温区的新特性：磁有序温度上方磁熵对应二重态而非三重态，需超过30 K才达到三重简并基态的熵，表明硼笼内晶体场基态发生动力学劈裂，类似CeB₆，并伴有离心Jahn-Teller效应引起的热膨胀反常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前非弹性中子散射认定Pr³⁺的晶体场基态为三重态，但在磁有序温度（TN=6.7 K）上方，磁熵与磁性行为却与该三重态图像不一致。作者旨在厘清PrB₆低温顺磁区微观能级结构的真实面貌，解释磁熵、磁化率与热膨胀之间的表观矛盾。

Method: 作者综合运用比热、热膨胀和磁性测量，准确测定两次连续磁转变的潜热；通过改进晶格贡献的描述，精确导出磁熵随温度的变化；并结合低温顺磁磁化率的分析以及20 K以下热膨胀反常的考察，推断裂缝（splitting）机制。

Result: 在一级磁有序温度TN=6.7 K紧上方，每个Pr³⁺的磁熵接近二重态的数值，而非中子散射认定的三重态晶体场基态；温度需超过30 K才能达到三重简并基态所对应的熵值。这一行为与CeB₆相似，强烈暗示硼笼内晶体场基态发生了动力学劈裂。低温顺磁磁化率呈现类似非磁性基态的特征，进一步支持该劈裂。此外，20 K以下观测到大幅热膨胀反常，与预期的离心Jahn-Teller效应一致。

Conclusion: PrB₆的晶体场基态在硼笼内存在动力学劈裂，导致其磁熵、磁化率及热膨胀在低温顺磁区表现出二重态（甚至近非磁性）特征；只有升至30 K以上才恢复三重简并基态的热力学行为。这一发现与CeB₆类比，深化了对稀土六硼化物中晶体场与晶格耦合动力学的理解。

Abstract: New insights into the paramagnetic range of PrB$_6$ below 50 K are revealed through specific-heat, thermal-expansion and magnetic measurements. The latent heats of the two successive magnetic transitions, together with an improved description of the lattice contribution, enable a detailed determination of the thermal variation of the magnetic entropy. Immediately above the first-order magnetic ordering temperature ($T_N = 6.7$ K), the magnetic entropy per Pr$^{3+}$ is close to that of a doublet, instead of the triplet crystal-field ground state identified by inelastic neutron scattering. Temperatures in excess of 30 K are required to reach the entropy of the threefold-degenerate ground state. This is reminiscent of the CeB$_6$ case and, similarly, strongly suggests a dynamical splitting of the crystal-field ground state within the boron cage. Further evidence of this splitting is provided by the analysis of the low-temperature paramagnetic susceptibility, which resembles that of a non-magnetic ground state. In addition, a large thermal-expansion anomaly is observed below 20 K, consistent with the expected centrifugal Jahn-Teller effect.

</details>


### [125] [Quasi-Phases of Quantum Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40526)
*Nick G. Jones,Paul Fendley,Nathanan Tantivasadakarn,Ruben Verresen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文引入“准相”(quasi-phases)概念，用于描述量子多体相图中连续临界线终止处附近、被能隙隔离但无法通过微扰临界理论的小扰动路径相连接的区域，并给出其物理后果与构造原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统“物质相”的概念不足以刻画相图中连续转变线终止后，其邻近区域是否可视为不同的相（如水与蒸汽）。作者希望回答这一开放问题，即能否在超越通常物质相分类的框架下，对这些区域进行有意义的锐利刻画。

Method: 引入“准相”概念：指临界点附近、在更广相图中相连、但不存在任何可由临界理论小扰动到达的能隙路径的区域。通过中心荷 c=1 的自旋链例子，构造多临界 c=3/2 与 c=9/5 的轨道折叠(orbifold)母理论，描述“商对称保护”与“表示对称保护”拓扑现象；并通过弱化 Lieb-Schultz-Mattis 反常，将准相构造转化为一种建设性原则。

Result: 发现准相具有显著物理后果：同一物质相内的不同区域之间可存在界面模(interface modes)；这些界面模携带更高维的线性表示，而非一维对称保护拓扑相通常标记的射影表示类。支撑不同准相可使一个在紫外尺度上“不必要”的临界点在红外尺度上变得必要（infrared-necessary），这一条件比仅存在余维为一的临界线更强。

Conclusion: 物质相内部存在比传统分类所揭示的更丰富结构：临界线终止附近的能隙区域可被意义明确地区分为不同的“准相”，其刻画需借助更大多临界母理论及对反常条件的弱化，从而拓展了对物质相与拓扑现象的理解。

Abstract: Within quantum many-body phase diagrams, continuous transition lines can appear and then terminate. However, it has remained unclear whether the neighboring regimes can be meaningfully described as distinct phases, akin to water and vapour, since any sharp characterization would have to go beyond the usual notion of phases of matter. We answer this in the affirmative by introducing quasi-phases: gapped regimes near a critical point that are connected in the broader phase diagram, but not by any gapped path accessible from small perturbations of the critical theory. The physical consequences include interface modes between regimes in the same phase of matter. More striking still, describing both the extended critical line and the gapped path connecting the regimes requires a bigger multicritical parent theory. In spin-chain examples with continuous terminating lines with central charge $c=1$, we find multicritical $c=3/2$ and $c=9/5$ orbifolds describing so-called 'quotient symmetry protected' and 'representation symmetry protected' topological phenomena. While one-dimensional symmetry-protected topological phases are labeled by projective representation classes, these quasi-phases can have interface modes carrying higher-dimensional linear representations. We turn this into a constructive principle through a weakening of the Lieb-Schultz-Mattis anomaly. Supporting distinct quasi-phases makes an unnecessary critical point infrared-necessary, even if it is unnecessary at the ultraviolet scale. This condition is more restrictive than simply having a codimension-one critical line, revealing richer structure within phases of matter than their conventional classification suggests.

</details>


### [126] [Algebraic theory of abelian fractons and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40596)
*Wilbur Shirley,Evan Wickenden,Bowen Yang,Agnès Beaudry,Michael Hermele*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种用于描述阿贝尔带隙分形子序和拓扑相中融合、迁移率与统计的代数理论，将阿贝尔任意子的代数描述推广到受限迁移率与空间延展激发系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阿贝尔任意子理论仅适用于完全可移动的点状激发，无法刻画分形子序中受限迁移率和空间延展激发；需要一套统一的代数框架来编码融合、迁移率与统计，并理解这类非平凡带隙相的激发结构与相不变量。

Method: 引入"编织融合复形"（BFC）：以链复形编码融合与迁移率，并配备"编织链映射"编码统计，其等价类定义"编织融合理论"（BFT）。该框架涵盖任意平移不变泡利码及二维所有阿贝尔任意子理论（含手征理论），并在此基础上推导点状融合与迁移率约束、提取互统计理论、形式化"激发-探测器原理"。

Result: 框架覆盖任意平移不变泡利码和二维所有阿贝尔任意子理论；给出证据支持"每个非退化BFT可实现为某带隙相的激发理论，且描述该相的BFT是相不变量"这一猜想；证明非退化BFT中X-cube与各向异性模型中X或Z扇区均不能单独出现；对仅含点状激发的非退化理论，提取出由融合模及其到统计对偶的映射构成的更简互统计理论，且在奇融合阶下与完整编织融合理论等价；将远程探测解释为分形子序中互统计的推广，并证明在唯一无穷空间基态假设下"激发-探测器原理"等价于非退化性。

Conclusion: 所提BFC/BFT代数框架成功统一并推广了阿贝尔任意子理论至带隙分形子序，揭示了融合、迁移率与统计之间的深层约束，并通过非退化性与激发-探测器原理的等价性建立了理论完备性的判据。

Abstract: We develop an algebraic theory of fusion, mobility, and statistics in abelian gapped fracton orders and topological phases. The framework generalizes the algebraic description of abelian anyons to systems with restricted-mobility and spatially extended excitations. We consider translation-invariant bosonic systems whose pointlike and extended excitations have finite fusion order. The basic objects are braided fusion complexes (BFCs): chain complexes encoding fusion and mobility, equipped with a braiding chain map encoding statistics. Their equivalence classes define braided fusion theories (BFTs). Our framework encompasses arbitrary translation-invariant Pauli codes, as well as all abelian anyon theories in two spatial dimensions, including chiral theories. We give evidence for the conjecture that every nondegenerate BFT can be realized as the excitation theory of a gapped phase and that the BFT describing such a phase is a phase invariant. We derive constraints on pointlike fusion and mobility, showing, for example, that neither the Pauli X nor Z sectors of the X-cube and anisotropic models can occur alone in a nondegenerate BFT. For nondegenerate theories with only pointlike excitations, we extract a simpler theory of mutual statistics, consisting of a fusion module and a map to its statistical dual. This theory of mutual statistics is an invariant of the BFT and, at odd fusion order, gives an equivalent description of the full braided fusion theory. We interpret remote detection as the generalization of mutual statistics to fracton orders. In the BFC setting, we give a precise formulation of the excitation-detector principle: every nontrivial excitation can be detected by an operator supported at spatial infinity, and every nontrivial detector detects some excitation. Assuming a unique infinite-space ground state, we prove that this principle is equivalent to nondegeneracy.

</details>


### [127] [Variational wavefunctions for ideal anyon gas: superfluid and skyrmion Hall crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40657)
*Ya-Hui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文针对统计角θ∈(0,π)的任意子理想气体基态这一长期未解问题，构造了结合精确任意子统计与神经网络关联的变分波函数，系统研究了自旋、味对称性以及莫尔材料中的候选基态，提出SU(3)味任意子可能处于"色超导体"或"斯格明子霍尔晶体"态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子（统计角介于玻色子与费米子之间）理想气体的基态是一个长期悬而未决的问题。近期分数量子反常霍尔绝缘体的实验观测重新激发了对该问题的兴趣。作者希望超越传统复合玻色子、复合费米子、部分子等波函数框架，利用神经网络变分方法精确处理任意子统计，在球面上系统判定不同统计角、自旋和味对称性下的基态相，并为扭转MoTe₂中观测到的重入整数量子反常霍尔相和超导现象提供微观波函数解释。

Method: 作者在球面上构造精确满足任意子交换统计的变分波函数，将复合玻色子、复合费米子与部分子波函数同神经网络关联（neural-network correlations）结合，并通过变分蒙特卡洛优化能量。针对自旋less及SU(2)半子（semion）情况验证了配对任意子超流体优于晶体态；针对θ=π/3的SU(3)味任意子，构造了斯格明子霍尔晶体波函数（任意子在自旋纹理产生的有效磁场中形成整数量子霍尔态）与手征色超导体波函数（六任意子成簇的色-味锁定配对，对应于具有SU(3)色规范与SU(3)味对称性的全子金属的后代，波函数为超行列式而非部分子行列式的乘积），并在变分框架内比较了其与晶体态及U(1)²、Z₃规范群色-味锁定态的能量。

Result: 对自旋less及SU(2)半子，确认了配对任意子超流体，其能量显著低于最优晶体态。对θ=π/3自旋less情形，液体与晶体能量较为接近。对θ=π/3的SU(3)味任意子发现两个候选基态：斯格明子霍尔晶体与手征色超导体；在所选变分ansatz内，色超导体能量优于晶体态以及U(1)²和Z₃规范群的色-味锁定相。据此推测，扭转MoTe₂中的重入整数量子反常霍尔相是掺杂任意子的斯格明子霍尔晶体，而观测到的超导可能为色超导体，后者可经历向f+if BCS超导体的相变，伴随手征中心荷跳跃3。

Conclusion: 通过理论引导的复合粒子/部分子波函数与神经网络关联的结合，本文为任意子理想气体基态问题提供了有力的变分证据，揭示了从配对超流、晶体到新奇拓扑色超导的丰富相图。SU(3)味任意子的色超导体态及其f+if BCS过渡预言为扭转MoTe₂的实验现象（重入整数量子反常霍尔相与超导）提供了统一的微观解释，并提示可能存在手征中心荷改变3的拓扑相变。

Abstract: The ground state of an ideal gas of anyons with statistical angle $θ\in(0,π)$ is a long-standing open problem, with renewed interest from the recent observations of fractional Chern insulators. In this work we construct variational wavefunctions that build in the exact anyon statistics and combine theory-guided composite-boson, composite-fermion and parton wavefunctions with neural-network correlations on the sphere. For spinless and SU(2) semions we confirm the paired anyon superfluid, well below the best crystal states. At $θ=π/3$ liquids and crystals are closer in energy for the spinless case. For $θ=π/3$ anyons with SU(3) flavor, we find two candidates. One is a skyrmion Hall crystal, in which the anyons form an integer quantum Hall state in the effective magnetic field from the spin texture. The other is a chiral color superconductor of six-anyon clusters, the color-flavor-locked pairing descendant of a holon metal with SU(3) color gauge symmetry and SU(3) flavor symmetry; its wavefunction is a hyperdeterminant rather than a product of parton determinants. Within our variational ansatz the color superconductor has better energy than both crystals and the color-flavor locked phases with U(1)$^2$ and Z$_3$ gauge groups. For twisted MoTe$_2$ this suggests that the reentrant integer quantum anomalous Hall phase is the skyrmion Hall crystal of the doped anyons, and that the observed superconductivity may be the color superconductor, which can undergo a transition to an $f+if$ BCS superconductor with a jump of the chiral central charge by 3.

</details>
