### [128] [Exact Solution of the $(2+1)$-Dimensional Dunkl-Klein-Gordon Oscillator with a Uniform Magnetic Field in Non-Commutative Phase Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37820)
*R. D. Mota,D. Ojeda-Guillén*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在推广了 Dunkl 导数的非对易相空间中求解 Klein-Gordon 振子（有无均匀磁场两种情况），给出精确的波函数与能谱，并分析了能谱对宇称的依赖及非相对论极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Dunkl 变形、非对易相空间几何与磁场三者结合对 Klein-Gordon 振子系统能级的影响，并考察宇称反射对称性与非相对论极限。

Method: 先用 Dunkl 导数推广标准对易相空间并评估反射宇称对称性；再利用 Seiberg-Witten 映射将系统扩展到非对易相空间；对有无均匀磁场两种情况分别精确求解本征函数与能谱（有磁场时对应广义 Landau 能级），并计算非相对论极限与作图分析。

Result: Dunkl 形变参数、非对易相空间几何与磁场的组合产生了依赖宇称的能级修正；当外磁场或非对易参数趋于零时，精确解还原为此前文献中的对易 Dunkl 模型。

Conclusion: Dunkl 形变、非对易几何与磁场的联合作用使能级出现宇称依赖的修正，且解在退化极限下与已有结果一致，验证了方法的正确性。

Abstract: We solve the Klein-Gordon oscillator in a non-commutative phase space, generalized using Dunkl derivatives, both with and without interaction with a uniform magnetic field. For each of these cases, we begin by introducing the Dunkl derivative into the standard commutative phase space to evaluate the reflection parity symmetries. Then, using the Seiberg-Witten map, we extend the generalized system to a non-commutative phase space. In both cases, we determine the eigenfunctions and the energy spectrum exactly (which correspond to generalized Landau levels when a magnetic field is present). Furthermore, we compute the non-relativistic limit of the system and graphically analyze the behavior of the energy spectra. Our results demonstrate that the combination of the Dunkl deformation parameters, the non-commutative phase space geometry, and the magnetic field produces a parity-dependent modification of the energy levels. Finally, we verify that when the external magnetic field or the non-commutative parameters vanish, our exact solutions correctly reduce to the commutative Dunkl models established in previous works.

</details>


### [129] [Algebraic Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37904)
*Kasia Rejzner*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述代数量子场论（AQFT），从量子力学出发构造自由标量场，利用因果性与正能量约束局域测量，并通过 Reeh–Schlieder 定理、局域 von Neumann 代数结构与分裂性质揭示连续统 QFT 与有限自由度量子力学的本质差异，进而讨论超选择理论、局域协变性、微扰与同调方法以及测量理论和量子信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于：AQFT 将可观测量及其时空局域化与态的选择分离开来，从而能够在不必假设共享同一希尔伯特空间的条件下比较真空态、热态与荷电表示，为理解 QFT 的局域结构与量子力学有限自由度模型的根本区别提供严格框架。

Method: 方法包括：从熟悉量子力学出发发展公理化框架；构造自由标量场；运用因果性与正能量条件约束局域测量；利用 Reeh–Schlieder 定理、局域 von Neumann 代数结构与分裂性质分析局域代数；通过超选择理论联系可观测量荷与统计及内对称性；并将局域协变性、微扰方法与同调方法推广到弯曲时空、相互作用与规范理论。

Result: 结果表明：AQFT 框架能够区分连续统 QFT 与有限自由度量子力学——局域 von Neumann 代数结构、分裂性质及 Reeh–Schlieder 定理揭示了二者关键差异；超选择理论建立了可观测量荷、统计与内对称性之间的联系；相关方法可进一步延伸至弯曲时空、相互作用、规范理论以及测量理论和量子信息领域。

Conclusion: 代数量子场论提供了一个以可观测量代数、时空局域化和态为核心的精炼框架，可统观地比较真空、热与荷电表示，并严格刻画 QFT 独有的局域结构；该框架及其延拓（局域协变性、微扰与同调方法）为弯曲时空、相互作用、规范理论、测量理论与量子信息提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Algebraic quantum field theory describes a quantum system through its observables, their localisation in spacetime, and the states that assign them expectation values. The separation of the specification of observables from the choice of a state makes it possible to compare vacuum, thermal and charged representations without assuming that they share a preferred Hilbert space. We develop the framework from familiar quantum mechanics, construct the free scalar field, and explain how causality and positive energy constrain local measurements. The Reeh--Schlieder theorem, the structure of local von Neumann algebras and the split property reveal key differences between quantum theory in the continuum (i.e. QFT) and ordinary quantum mechanics with finitely many degrees of freedom. Superselection theory relates observable charges to statistics and internal symmetries. Finally, local covariance and perturbative and homological methods extend the discussion to curved spacetime, interactions and gauge theories. We also discuss measurement theory and quantum information aspects of AQFT.

</details>


### [130] [From gauge-covariant Noether invariants to electromagnetic velocity-dependent mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38128)
*A. S. de Castro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从标准 Noether–Bessel-Hagen 构造出发，明确给出从相对论规范不变世界线荷到非相对论速度依赖力学广义守恒量的约化过程，全程保留准不变边界项，并建立三种既有描述之间的显式约化映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电粒子的时空对称性只需将电磁势保持到规范变换（准不变性），但现有文献中相对论世界线描述、非相对论坐标时拉氏量与场-粒子闭系统三者的守恒量常被混淆。作者希望厘清这一约化链条，严格展示规范不变性边界项如何贯穿始终。

Method: 采用约定 S_p=-mc∫ds+q∫A，取正则世界线一形式 P=-m u^♭+qA；对满足 L_X A=dχ_X 的 Killing 矢量构造荷 C_X=P(X)-qχ_X。对其取低速极限，得到坐标时拉氏量的 Noether–Bessel-Hagen 荷，并逐生成元导出广义线动量、角动量与能量。进一步以均匀磁场给出依赖规范的代表元与规范不变的平移荷，用 Darwin 相互作用在消去场后给出多粒子的保守 O(c^{-2}) 实现，并用总应力能量 Killing 荷描述闭粒子-场系统。

Result: 得到一条显式约化映射，将相对论规范不变世界线荷、非相对论速度依赖力学广义常量、以及粒子-场闭系统的总应力能量 Killing 荷三种既有描述联系起来，同时明确三者对应不同的守恒量而非同一对象。均匀磁场情形提供了初等的规范依赖代表元与规范不变的平移对称荷。

Conclusion: 通过保留准不变边界项，能够严格追踪规范自由度，从而显式贯通三条规范相关的约化路径；这澄清了三类守恒量的区别与联系，表明它们虽描述同一物理系统却不可简单等同。

Abstract: For a charged particle, a spacetime symmetry need preserve the electromagnetic potential only up to a gauge transformation. Starting from the standard Noether--Bessel-Hagen construction, we make explicit the reduction from the relativistic gauge-invariant worldline charge to the generalized constants of nonrelativistic velocity-dependent mechanics, retaining the quasi-invariance boundary term throughout. With the convention $S_p=-mc\int ds+q\int A$, the canonical worldline one-form is $P=-m u^\flat+qA$, and a Killing vector satisfying $\mathcal L_XA=dχ_X$ generates $C_X=P(X)-qχ_X$. Its low-velocity limit is the Noether--Bessel-Hagen charge of the coordinate-time Lagrangian, from which generalized linear momentum, angular momentum and energy follow generator by generator. A uniform magnetic field gives an elementary gauge-dependent representative but gauge-invariant translational charge. The Darwin interaction then supplies the conservative $O(c^{-2})$ many-particle realization after field elimination, while a closed particle--field system is governed by the total stress-energy Killing charge. The result is an explicit reduction map linking three established descriptions without identifying their distinct conserved quantities.

</details>


### [131] [Homogenization theory of topological Anderson insulators: genuine gaps and pseudo-gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38150)
*Guillaume Bal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为拓扑安德森绝缘体发展了一套均匀化理论，研究常数系数椭圆系统在尺度ε快速振荡涨落扰动下的极限行为，证明算子依范数-预解式收敛到均匀化极限，并刻画了界面与随机情形下的拓扑不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解拓扑安德森绝缘体中无序如何诱导非平凡拓扑相，特别是通常被视为拓扑平凡扰动的零均值电场势与磁场如何通过二阶Born修正改变拓扑相位，以及随机涨落对谱隙和拓扑指数定义的影响。

Method: 对常数系数椭圆系统施加尺度为ε的振荡涨落，利用二阶Born公式得到阶一修正M，建立范数-预解式收敛的均匀化理论；对半平面局限的涨落构造携带体差不变量的界面；对随机涨落，通过沿规避稀有区域的路径变形定义指标，并证明其几乎必然等于Born近似均匀化算子的体差不变量。

Result: 周期涨落不平均为零，产生由二阶Born公式给出的阶一修正M，算子收敛到均匀化极限；零均值电场势与磁场可改变拓扑相位；半平面涨落形成的界面在ε充分小时携带体差不变量且对局部缺陷稳定；随机涨落下均匀化谱隙变为赝隙，通过沿变形路径定义的指标几乎必然等于Born近似下均匀化算子的体差不变量（适用于低阶矩消失的一阶合金及含BHZ模型在内的一类临界标量无序二阶系统）。

Conclusion: 无序诱导的拓扑安德森绝缘体可通过均匀化理论严格刻画：Born修正M决定拓扑相，界面拓扑稳定，随机情形的拓扑指标沿规避稀有区域的变形路径定义并几乎必然等于均匀化算子的体差不变量。

Abstract: A topological Anderson insulator is an insulator whose non-trivial topological phase is induced by disorder. We develop a homogenization theory of this phenomenon for constant-coefficient elliptic systems perturbed by fluctuations oscillating at scale $\varepsilon$. Periodic fluctuations of every order below that of the operator, with appropriate amplitudes, do not average out: they produce an order-one correction $M$ given by a second-order (Born) formula, and the operator converges in the norm-resolvent sense to its homogenized limit. In particular, electric potentials and magnetic fields of zero mean, usually regarded as topologically trivial perturbations, generate corrections $M$ that change the topological phase. Fluctuations confined to a half-plane create an interface that carries the bulk-difference invariant of the homogenized operator for all $\varepsilon$ sufficiently small, a property that is stable under local defects. For random fluctuations, rare regions typically fill the homogenized gap, which becomes a pseudo-gap, and the standard interface index need no longer be defined. We define an index along cuts deformed so as to avoid the rare regions, and prove that it equals almost surely the bulk-difference invariant of the operator homogenized in the Born approximation, for first-order alloys with vanishing low moments and for a class of second-order systems with scalar disorder at criticality that includes the BHZ model.

</details>


### [132] [Bulk gap stability and spectral flow for fermions with weak long-range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40299)
*Stefan Teufel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：弱长程多体相互作用不会破坏自由费米子体系的体态能隙，且存在一个准局域谱流把自由基态输运到相互作用体系的基态。该结果允许多项式衰减，并给出二维以上离散点集上的充分衰减条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多体相互作用对自由费米子系统体态能隙稳定性的影响，以及能否用准局域变换连接自由基态与相互作用基态，从而为具长程相互作用体系的基态结构与能隙鲁棒性提供严格数学基础。

Method: 设 Φ_h 为自由费米相互作用（可含配对项），其无限体积 Majorana 系数算子 h 在 (-g,g) 无谱；设 V 为偶自伴多体相互作用。对充分小的 |λ|，构造相互作用体态系统 Φ_λ=Φ_h+λV 的纯基态 ω_λ 及准局域自同构 β_{0,λ}，使得 ω_λ=ω_0∘β_{0,λ}。证明通过构造谱流并结合激励数比较（excitation-number comparison）建立能隙，同时借助配套论文中的多项式传播界控制演化交互作用。

Result: 每个基态 ω_λ 具有 GNS 哈密顿量，其能隙至少为 g-C_*|λ|‖V‖_*。对均匀离散点集 Γ⊂R^d 上的双格点相互作用，当跳迁、配对系数及相互作用项均以 (1+|x-y|)^{-ϖ}（ϖ>2d）衰减时结论成立；谱流生成元保持多项式的空间指数，其自同构满足该指数下的局域性界；若 h 与 V 为拉伸指数衰减，谱流生成元亦然。

Conclusion: 弱长程多体相互作用保持自由费米子体系的体态能隙，谱流可把自由基态准局域地输运到相互作用基态，且结果对多项式乃至拉伸指数衰减的相互作用均成立。

Abstract: We prove that weak long-range many-body interactions preserve the bulk gap of a free fermion system and that a quasi-local spectral flow transports the free ground state to ground states of the interacting system. Let $Φ_h$ be a free fermionic interaction, possibly including pairing terms, whose infinite-volume Majorana coefficient operator $h$ has no spectrum in $(-g,g)$, and let $\mathcal V$ be an even self-adjoint many-body interaction. For sufficiently small $|λ|$, we construct pure ground states $ω_λ$ of the interacting bulk system $Φ_λ=Φ_h+λ\mathcal V$ and quasi-local automorphisms $β_{0,λ}$ such that $ω_λ=ω_0\circβ_{0,λ}$, where $ω_0$ is the free ground state of $Φ_h$. Each $ω_λ$ has a GNS Hamiltonian with gap at least $g-C_*|λ| \|\mathcal V\|_*$, where $\|\cdot\|_*$ is the relevant interaction norm.
  Our result allows polynomial decay of both the free coefficients and the many-body interaction. For two-site interactions on a uniformly discrete point set $Γ\subset\mathbb{R}^d$, a sufficient condition is decay as $(1+|x-y|)^{-\varpi}$ with $\varpi>2d$, for both the hopping and pairing coefficients and the interaction terms. The spectral-flow generator preserves the spatial exponent of the polynomial interaction norm, and its automorphisms satisfy locality bounds at that exponent. If $h$ and $\mathcal V$ have stretched-exponential decay, so does the spectral-flow generator.
  The proof constructs the spectral flow and establishes the gap through an excitation-number comparison, using polynomial propagation bounds for evolved interactions proved in a companion paper.

</details>


### [133] [Nonequilibrium Thermodynamics of Semilinear Stochastic Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38166)
*Zelin Liu,Hao Ge*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为半线性随机偏微分方程建立严格的非平衡热力学数学框架，以关联 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程的不变高斯测度为参考测度，定义热力学量、推导熵平衡方程，给出熵产生率的显式表达式，并证明其消失等价于时间可逆性；同时建立样本路径熵产生与涨落定理，并应用于多类模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机偏微分方程（SPDE）系统的非平衡热力学缺乏统一严格的数学框架。传统非平衡热力学多针对有限维系统或特定模型，而 SPDE 的无穷维性质、不可逆不变测度以及样本路径层面的涨落定理需要系统化的数学处理，以刻画热力学量与时间可逆性、相对熵之间的关系。

Method: 以半线性 SPDE 关联的 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程的不变高斯测度（不必时间可逆）作为参考测度；用概率密度定义热力学量并推导熵平衡方程；给出熵产生率的显式表达；将样本路径熵产生等同于前向路径测度关于时间反演路径测度的 Radon-Nikodym 导数之对数；对非平稳系统将样本路径熵产生分解为 housekeeping heat 与自由能耗散两部分；证明各满足积分涨落定理与非负期望。

Result: 证明平稳态下熵产生率消失当且仅当过程时间可逆；样本路径熵产生满足涨落定理；非平稳下 housekeeping heat 与自由能耗散各自满足积分涨落定理且期望非负；平均自由能耗散等于相对平稳分布的相对熵之减少；理论成功应用于带空间关联噪声的半线性随机热方程、随机聚合物模型、散度自由漂移的非线性模型，以及两个与不同温度热浴接触的耦合随机热方程。

Conclusion: 本文为半线性 SPDE 的非平衡热力学建立了严格、统一的数学框架，建立了熵产生、时间可逆性与涨落定理之间的定量联系，并通过若干典型模型验证了框架的普遍适用性。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous mathematical framework for the nonequilibrium thermodynamics of semilinear stochastic partial differential equations. The invariant Gaussian measure of the associated Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process, which need not be time-reversible, serves as the reference measure. We define thermodynamic quantities in terms of probability densities and derive the entropy balance equation. We obtain explicit expressions for the entropy production rate and prove that its vanishing in the stationary state is equivalent to time reversibility. Sample-path entropy production is identified with the logarithm of the Radon-Nikodym derivative of the forward path measure with respect to the time-reversed path measure and is shown to satisfy fluctuation theorems. For nonstationary systems, sample-path entropy production decomposes into housekeeping heat and free-energy dissipation, each satisfying an integral fluctuation theorem. Both have nonnegative expectations, and the mean free-energy dissipation equals the decrease in relative entropy with respect to the stationary distribution. The theory is applied to semilinear stochastic heat equations with spatially correlated noise, a random polymer model, a nonlinear model with a divergence-free drift, and two coupled stochastic heat equations in contact with heat baths at different temperatures.

</details>


### [134] [Local determination of low-energy states in gapped fermion systems with weak long-range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40337)
*Stefan Teufel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对体能隙费米子系统，证明在弱偶相互作用下，低能态具有定量局部确定性：区域内部深处局域期望值不依赖外界相互作用与所选低能态，误差随到区域补集的距离衰减。由此推出代数基态唯一性、局部扰动局域化(LPPL)、有限样本低能激发边界局域化及关联衰减等强结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究体能隙费米子系统中低能态的局域性质：希望证明在加入弱的多体相互作用后，系统低能态的局部可观测量可由块体内相互作用唯一确定，且不受远处扰动或边界项的影响，从而为理解基态唯一性、缺陷局部性与边界效应提供定量基础。

Method: 将自由基态到相互作用块体基态的准绝热输运(quasi-adiabatic transport)与一个新条件——局部激发可探测性(LED, local excitation detectability)相结合。配套论文建立弱相互作用下的能隙稳定性并构造输运，本文则在足够弱耦合下证明LED成立；抽象定理是非微扰的，只要输运与LED假设满足即适用。

Result: 证明了低能态的定量局部确定性：块体相互作用在区域U内唯一决定低能局部期望，误差随到U补集距离指数(多项空间衰减下)衰减。并得出四条推论：(i)块体相互作用具有唯一代数基态；(ii)强LPPL原理成立，远处缺陷仅带来空间衰减的局域期望变化；(iii)有限样本低能激发任意时间局域于边界附近，仅剩空间衰减尾巴，且对任意偶边界项一致成立；(iv)U内部深处到外部的关联很小，给出以空间间隔和到U^c距离表示的定量界。

Conclusion: 在弱偶相互作用且允许多项式空间衰减的体能隙费米子系统中，低能态具有定量局部确定性；该抽象定理非微扰，依赖于准绝热输运与LED两个假设，并推出基态唯一性、LPPL、边界局域化与关联衰减等一系列强局域化结论。

Abstract: We consider bulk-gapped fermion systems whose infinite-volume bulk is obtained by adding sufficiently weak even many-body interactions to a free Hamiltonian with spectral gap $g>0$, allowing polynomial spatial decay of both parts. We prove quantitative local determination of low-energy states: whenever another interaction agrees with the bulk interaction inside a finite or infinite region $U$, with bulk terms crossing its boundary unchanged or removed, all its states with excitation energies in $[0,3g/4]$ have local expectations close to those of the bulk ground state deep inside $U$. The error decays with the distance to the complement of $U$, independently of the interaction terms supported outside $U$ and of the chosen low-energy state.
  Quantitative local determination has strong consequences. (i) The bulk interaction has a unique algebraic ground state. (ii) A strong "local perturbations perturb locally" (LPPL) principle holds: arbitrary distant defects change local ground-state expectations only by a spatially decaying error. (iii) Low-energy excitations in finite samples remain localized near the boundary for all times, up to spatially decaying tails, uniformly over arbitrary even boundary terms. (iv) Correlations from deep inside $U$ to outside $U$ are small, with quantitative bounds in terms of spatial separation and distance to $U^c$.
  To prove local determination of low-energy states, we combine quasi-adiabatic transport of the free ground state to the interacting bulk ground state with a new condition, local excitation detectability (LED). A companion paper establishes gap stability and constructs the transport for weak interactions, while we prove LED at sufficiently weak coupling. The abstract theorem is nonperturbative and applies whenever the transport and LED hypotheses hold.

</details>


### [135] [Finite-volume and thermodynamic-limit lower bounds for the coherently driven Bose--Hubbard model via the Lieb--Yamazaki method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38464)
*Maciej Janowicz,Joanna Kaleta,Filip Krzyżewski,Andrzej Zembrzuski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Lieb--Yamazaki正算子分解，为相干驱动的Bose--Hubbard模型导出基态能量的严格下界，并通过位移与Bogoliubov变换对角化所得二次边界哈密顿量，进而给出任意维度下的稳定性条件、热力学极限能密度、激发谱及高斯态可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干驱动的Bose--Hubbard模型缺乏精确可解性，需要为其基态能量建立严格的变分下界，以判定系统稳定性并提供可定量验证的物理约束。

Method: 采用Lieb--Yamazaki正算子分解构造二次边界哈密顿量，利用位移变换和Bogoliubov变换将其对角化，导出任意维度下的显式稳定性条件与有限体积下界，并计算高斯态下的填充数、粒子数涨落、二阶关联等可观测量，辅以数值评估下界紧致性。

Result: 得到显式稳定性条件与有限体积能量下界，确立热力学极限能密度并刻画边界哈密顿量的激发谱；推导出高斯态填充数、粒子数涨落及二阶关联函数，识别出反聚束与亚泊松统计的参数区域；数值计算表明该下界在代表性参数区间具有良好紧致性。

Conclusion: 通过正算子分解构造的二次边界哈密顿量，为相干驱动Bose--Hubbard模型的基态能量提供了严格且可对角化的下界框架，并能解析地刻画稳定性、激发谱与量子统计性质，为理解该类非平衡开放耗散/驱动晶格体系提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We derive rigorous lower bounds on the ground-state energy of the coherently driven Bose--Hubbard model using a Lieb--Yamazaki positive-operator decomposition. The resulting quadratic bounding Hamiltonian is diagonalized by displacement and Bogoliubov transformations, yielding explicit stability conditions and finite-volume bounds in arbitrary dimension. We establish the thermodynamic-limit energy density and characterize the excitation spectrum of the bounding Hamiltonian. We also derive its Gaussian-state observables, including filling, number fluctuations, and second-order correlations, and identify regimes of antibunching and sub-Poissonian statistics. Numerical calculations assess the tightness of the bounds across representative parameter regimes.

</details>


### [136] [A Primer on Instanton Physics - A Short Course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38717)
*Pervez Hoodbhoy*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 这是一套面向高年级本科生和初级研究生的六讲入门讲义，介绍量子力学与量子场论中的瞬子（instanton）概念，前四讲专注于粒子力学铺垫，后两讲延伸到半经典场论的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高年级本科生和初级研究生提供一条系统、循序渐进的路径，帮助他们入门理解量子力学与量子场论中瞬子的概念及其在半经典场论中的应用。

Method: 采用六讲讲座的形式（2026年10月6日至21日于新不伦瑞克大学讲授），先以四讲篇幅专门讨论粒子力学以建立必要的基础知识，再用最后两讲过渡到半经典场论中的具体应用。

Result: 形成了一套完整的讲义结构：第一至四讲聚焦于讲解瞬子所需的量子力学基础与相关技巧，第五、六讲将这些背景应用于半经典场论。

Conclusion: 该讲义通过先粒子力学后场论的递进式安排，为初学者搭建了理解量子力学和量子场论中瞬子的入门框架，强调扎实的基础铺垫是掌握半经典场论应用的前提。

Abstract: These six lectures, delivered at the University of New Brunswick 6-21 October 2026, are an introduction to instantons in quantum mechanics and quantum field theory for senior undergraduate and beginning graduate students. The first four lectures deal solely with particle mechanics, building the necessary background for the last two that cover applications in semi-classical field theory.

</details>


### [137] [From Entanglement Bootstrap to DHR Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38821)
*Kohtaro Kato,Yoshiko Ogata*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维量子自旋系统中建立了纠缠引导（entanglement bootstrap）与 Doplicher–Haag–Roberts（DHR）超选择扇区理论之间的直接联系，证明了 Haag 对偶性，并建立了简单纠缠引导任意子类型与不可约超选择扇区之间的一一对应，同时为每个融合通道构造了从纠缠引导融合空间上的密度矩阵到算子代数融合通道中归一化正缠结算子（intertwiner）的仿射双射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子的描述长期存在两种并行但缺乏直接关联的框架：基于熵的纠缠引导方法和基于算子代数的 DHR 超选择扇区理论。此前二者的联系往往需要借助可精确求解的格点模型作为中介。本文旨在越过这种中介，直接从基本假设出发建立熵描述与算子代数描述之间的内在对应，从而统一两种进路。

Method: 作者以二维量子自旋系统为对象，在纠缠引导假设下工作。首先证明 Haag 对偶性（Haag duality），这是 DHR 理论中局域可观测代数与其实子代数之间的关键对偶关系；随后利用该对偶性证明简单纠缠引导任意子类型与不可约 DHR 超选择扇区一一对应；最后针对每个融合通道，构造一个从纠缠引导融合空间上的密度矩阵到相应算子代数融合通道中归一化正缠结算子的仿射双射。

Result: 在纠缠引导假设下证明了 Haag 对偶性成立；建立了简单纠缠引导任意子类型与不可约超选择扇区之间的一一对应；并对每个融合通道构造了密度矩阵空间与归一化正缠结算子之间的仿射双射。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在无需借助可精确求解格点模型的情况下，直接连通了任意子的熵描述（纠缠引导）与算子代数描述（DHR 理论），为拓扑序中任意子理论提供了统一的数学基础，并有望推动两类方法在更一般量子多体系统中的交叉应用。

Abstract: We establish a direct connection between the entanglement bootstrap and the Doplicher--Haag--Roberts theory of superselection sectors for two-dimensional quantum spin systems. Under the entanglement-bootstrap assumptions, we prove Haag duality and establish a one-to-one correspondence between simple entanglement-bootstrap anyon types and irreducible superselection sectors. For each fusion channel, we further construct an affine bijection from density matrices on the entanglement-bootstrap fusion space to normalized positive intertwiners in the corresponding operator-algebraic fusion channel. These results connect the entropic and operator-algebraic descriptions of anyons directly, without passing through exactly solvable lattice models.

</details>


### [138] [The geometry of Floquet states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38845)
*Vladimir Y. Cherynak,John R. Klein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究"好"周期哈密顿量（即弗洛凯谱非简并的周期哈密顿量）的模空间，构造其几何模型，并完全确定该空间的有理上同调环；同时提出两个关于绝热驱动下渐近量子演化的弗洛凯绝热定理猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期（含时）哈密顿量在量子力学中普遍存在，而"好"哈密顿量（弗洛凯谱非简并）是其中性态良好的一类。理解这类哈密顿量空间的整体几何与拓扑结构，对弗洛凯理论与绝热演化问题具有基础意义。

Method: 利用弗洛凯态（Floquet states）构造模空间的几何模型，该模型借助构型空间、旗流形和主丛进行表述；并由此推导出有理上同调环的完整描述。在绝热部分，考虑一条单参数"好"周期哈密顿量族，并对其渐近量子演化提出两个弗洛凯版的绝热定理猜想。

Result: 主定理的推论是完全确定了"好"周期哈密顿量空间的有理上同调环。文中还给出了两个关于绝热驱动下渐近量子演化的弗洛凯绝热定理猜想（尚未证明）。

Conclusion: 通过弗洛凯态建立的几何模型揭示了"好"周期哈密顿量模空间的拓扑结构，并完整刻画了其有理上同调环；绝热部分提出的两个猜想为弗洛凯框架下的绝热定理提供了研究方向。

Abstract: This paper studies the moduli space of "good" periodic Hamiltonians, i.e., periodic Hamiltonians with nondegenerate Floquet spectrum. The corresponding Floquet states determine a geometric model for the moduli space of such Hamiltonians. The model is expressed in terms of configuration spaces, flag manifolds, and principal bundles. A corollary of the main result is the complete determination of the rational cohomology ring of the space of good periodic Hamiltonians.
  The paper also considers the adiabatic driving of a 1-parameter family of good periodic Hamiltonians. We formulate two conjectures describing the resulting asymptotic quantum evolution. These are Floquet analogues of the adiabatic theorem in quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [139] [A First-Order Phase Transition in Spin-Imbalanced BCS Theory of Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38961)
*Marius Lemm,Barbara Roos*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了自旋不平衡BCS泛函中存在一阶相变：在足够小的自旋不平衡和足够低的温度下，存在一个严格低于正常态线性稳定性阈值的共存耦合常数，使Cooper对密度发生非零跳变、极小能量出现拐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Caldas与Mota的数值工作和Freiji、Hainzl、Seiringer的解析工作提示：在自旋依赖化学势（μ_+≠μ_-）的自旋不平衡BCS模型中存在一种不寻常的一阶相变——正常态整体失稳却仍保持局部稳定。本文旨在为该现象提供严格的数学证明，填补理论与数值结论之间的严格性空白。

Method: 研究平移不变的BCS超导泛函，系统为自旋1/2费米子且自旋化学势不同。核心方法是将BCS能量极小化分解为两部分：在固定配对（Cooper对密度）下先对单体密度矩阵作"内层"极小化，该内层问题可通过Legendre–Fenchel变换精确求解，从而得到一个强制性（coercivity）估计，用于控制能量泛函并证明一阶相变的结构。

Result: 证明了对每个足够小的固定自旋不平衡及足够低的温度，存在一个共存耦合常数λ_fo，它严格小于正常态的线性稳定性阈值λ_lin；在该λ_fo处，Cooper对密度表现出非零跳变，且极小化能量出现拐点（kink），即确为一阶相变。

Conclusion: 本文从数学上严格确立了自旋不平衡BCS泛函中一阶相变的存在性，并给出了判定这一非线性（非微扰）相变的关键技术——通过Legendre–Fenchel变换精确处理内层最小化而得到的强制性估计。这为理解该类模型中"正常态整体失稳但局部稳定"的非常规相变提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: We study a translation-invariant BCS functional of superconductivity for a system of spin-$1/2$ fermions with spin-dependent chemical potentials, $μ_+\neqμ_-$. Numerical work of Caldas and Mota and analytical work of Freiji, Hainzl, and Seiringer has suggested an unusual first-order phase transition in spin-imbalanced BCS models, in which the normal state becomes globally unstable while remaining locally stable. Here, we prove that a first-order phase transition occurs in such spin-imbalanced BCS functionals. For each sufficiently small fixed spin imbalance and sufficiently low temperature, there is a coexistence coupling $λ_{\mathrm{fo}}$ strictly below the linear-stability threshold $λ_{\mathrm{lin}}$ of the normal state at which the Cooper pair density exhibits a nonzero jump and the minimized energy has a kink. The methodological insight is a coercivity estimate obtained by decomposing the BCS energy minimization into two parts, where the ``inner'' problem minimizes over the one-particle density matrices at fixed pairing and can be exactly solved by a Legendre--Fenchel transform.

</details>


### [140] [Minimally-coupled Quasilinear Symmetric Hyperbolic Systems, Naturally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39107)
*Eleanor March,James Owen Weatherall*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在自然方程（喷流丛的闭子对象）框架下研究对称双曲性与适定性，提出非自然方程的"自然化"方法，并利用"最小耦合"从单流形上的方程构造出（同构意义下唯一）的自然方程；进而定义对称化子、双曲化与约束，恢复标准的存在唯一性结果，并证明最小耦合与自然性蕴含因果锥与度量光锥重合、以及相应二阶张量关于 Levi-Civita 导数无散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论在讨论偏微分方程的对称双曲性与适定性时，常常依赖非几何的、坐标相关的构造；作者希望将这些问题放到协变的"自然方程"框架中，从而以不变的方式理解对称双曲性，并澄清最小耦合在构造几何方程时的作用与唯一性。

Method: 作者采用范畴化的自然丛与喷流丛语言，把自然方程定义为自然丛上喷流丛的闭子对象；给出任意（非自然）方程自然化的方法；利用最小耦合从单流形上定义的方程诱导出自然方程，并证明其同构意义下的唯一性。在此基础上定义对称化子、双曲化与约束，借助任意选定的背景结构（如度量）建立能量估计并恢复存在唯一性定理，同时对一阶拟线性最小耦合的自然对称双曲方程推导游因果锥与度规光锥重合、以及双曲化对应的二阶张量的无散度性质。

Result: 证明最小耦合给出构造自然方程的唯一（同构意义下）方法；对任意背景结构（如度量）可恢复标准存在唯一性结果；并得到两个关键结构结论：一阶拟线性最小耦合自然对称双曲自然方程的因果锥必与度量光锥一致，且与之关联的某个二阶张量关于 Levi-Civita 导数算子必无散度。

Conclusion: 自然方程框架与最小耦合为对称双曲性和适定性提供了协变、不依赖坐标选择的刻画；自然性与最小耦合二者结合不仅约束了方程结构（因果锥等于光锥），还自动保证某些守恒律（二阶张量无散度），从而将几何自然的假设与物理上因果性和守恒性联系起来。

Abstract: We consider symmetric hyperbolicity and well-posedness in the context of natural equations, which are closed subobjects of jet bundles over natural bundles. We show how non-natural equations can be "naturalized", and we discuss how a precise version of "minimal coupling" offers a (provably unique, up to isomorphism) method for constructing natural equations from certain equations defined on a single manifold. We discuss how to define symmetrizers, hyperbolizations, and constraints for minimally coupled natural equations, and we show how these can be used to recover standard existence and uniqueness results, relative to any choice of background structure (a metric, for minimally coupled systems). Additionally, we show that minimal coupling and naturality together imply that the causal cones associated with a (first order, quasilinear, minimally coupled) naturally symmetric hyperbolic natural equation must always coincide with the metric lightcones, and that a certain rank-2 tensor, associates with a natural hyperbolization, must always be divergence free relative to the associated Levi-Civita derivative operator.

</details>


### [141] [Higher genus polylogarithms associated with the D'Hoker-Hidding-Schlotterer connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39382)
*Takashi Ichikawa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究任意亏格带标记点黎曼面族上由 DHS 联络定义的高亏格多对数，并证明在分离退化下，DHS 联络可由椭圆曲线上的 BL 联络与带标记点黎曼球上的 KZ 联络胶合而成，从而将高亏格多对数显式表示为以多重 zeta 值与椭圆多重 zeta 值为系数的形变参数及其对数的幂级数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亏格多对数是多重 zeta 值与椭圆多重 zeta 值的自然高亏格推广，但其显式结构长期缺乏清晰的几何描述。本文旨在通过退化（degeneration）方法，将高亏格情形归结为低亏格（亏格 0 与 1）的已知对象，从而给出高亏格多对数的显式展开。

Method: 作者考虑亏格 g、带 n 个标记点的黎曼面族上的 D'Hoker–Hidding–Schlotterer（DHS）联络。在黎曼面的分离退化（separating degeneration，即退化曲线将曲面分裂为若干低亏格分量）下，证明 DHS 联络可以经由胶合构造得到：即由椭圆曲线（亏格 1）上的 Brown–Levin（BL）联络与带标记点黎曼球（亏格 0）上的 Knizhnik–Zamolodchikov（KZ）联络拼接而成。基于这一胶合分解，进一步将关联的高亏格多对数按形变参数展开。

Result: 在分离退化下，DHS 联络可被构造为 BL 联络与 KZ 联络的胶合；相应地，高亏格多对数可显式描述为形变参数及其对数的幂级数，其系数由多重 zeta 值（MZVs）与椭圆多重 zeta 值（eMZVs）表达。

Conclusion: 通过分离退化将高亏格问题化归为亏格 0 与亏格 1 的已知联络理论，揭示了 DHS 联络的胶合结构，并据此给出了高亏格多对数的显式幂级数描述，系数为多重 zeta 值与椭圆多重 zeta 值。

Abstract: We study higher genus polylogarithms associated with the D'Hoker-Hidding-Schlotterer (DHS) connection defined on families of Riemann surfaces of any genus with marked points. Under separating degenerations of Riemann surfaces, we show that the DHS connection can be constructed by gluing the Brown-Levin (BL) connections on elliptic curves and the Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov (KZ) connections on pointed Riemann spheres. Therefore, the associated higher genus polylogarithms can be described explicitly as power series in deformation parameters and their logarithms for the families of Riemann surfaces whose coefficients are expressed by multiple zeta values and elliptic multiple zeta values.

</details>


### [142] [Generalized Frobenius Structures on Hurwitz Spaces, Their Open Extensions and Associated Principal Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39411)
*Shilin Ma*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在任意亏格的 Hurwitz 空间上，借助扩展原微分（extended primary differentials）构造广义 Frobenius 结构，并通过含 Cauchy 核的围道积分得到其主层级与几乎对偶层级的显式无弥散 Lax 表示；同时构造了原结构与几乎对偶结构的开 WDVV 方程解及其秩一扩张的主层级，并指出这些构造容许 Shramchenko 形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望在 Hurwitz 空间的广义 Frobenius 结构、其主层级（principal hierarchy）及相关对偶、开（open）结构的框架下，给出显式且统一的构造，以推广并显式化已有的 Frobenius 流形与可积层级理论。

Method: 1) 利用扩展原微分（extended primary differentials）在任意亏格 Hurwitz 空间上构造广义 Frobenius 结构；2) 通过含 Cauchy 核的围道积分导出这些结构及其几乎对偶（almost-dual）结构的无弥散（dispersionless）Lax 表示，从而显式给出主层级；3) 对原结构与几乎对偶结构分别构造开 WDVV 方程的解，并导出所得秩一扩张的主层级；4) 证明这些构造可进行 Shramchenko 形变。

Result: 得到了任意亏格 Hurwitz 空间上广义 Frobenius 结构及其几乎对偶结构的显式无弥散 Lax 表示与主层级；构造了原结构和几乎对偶结构的开 WDVV 方程解，并给出了相应秩一扩张的显式主层级；同时证明了上述构造均可实现 Shramchenko 形变。

Conclusion: 该工作系统地把广义 Frobenius 结构、主层级的 Lax 表示、开 WDVV 结构及其秩一扩张统一纳入同一框架，并表明整套构造具备 Shramchenko 形变这一灵活性，为 Hurwitz 空间上的可积结构研究提供了显式而一般的工具。

Abstract: We construct generalized Frobenius structures on Hurwitz spaces of arbitrary genus using extended primary differentials. Through contour integrals involving the Cauchy kernel, we derive explicit dispersionless Lax representations for the principal hierarchies of these structures and their almost-dual counterparts. We also construct solutions to the open WDVV equations for both the original and almost-dual structures, and derive explicit principal hierarchies for the resulting rank-one extensions. Finally, we show that these constructions admit Shramchenko deformations.

</details>


### [143] [Arithmetic localization for quasiperiodic long-range operators with monotone potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39448)
*Hongyi Cao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一类具有α-Hölder单调势与长程跳跃（按指数衰减）的一维准周期算子，在ε满足显式小性条件下，证明当ρ>αβ时算子具有均匀Anderson局域化，其中β为频率共振的指数强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Jitomirskaya与Kachkovskiy针对单调准周期Schrödinger算子的算术局域化策略推广到长程跳跃情形，且不依赖Lyapunov指数，从而覆盖跳跃衰减较慢、无法用传统Lyapunov指数方法处理的情形。

Method: 证明采用算术局域化策略：利用频率共振的指数强度β刻画丢番图性质，结合α-Hölder单调势的单调性控制谱的扰动，并在显式小性条件|T(x,y)|≤ε e^{-ρ|x-y|}下，借助多尺度分析或共振隔离思想，证明在ρ>αβ时特征函数呈指数衰减。

Result: 在显式的ε小性条件下，当ρ>αβ时，所研究的长程准周期算子具有均匀Anderson局域化；该结果无需Lyapunov指数即可成立。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将算术局域化方法推广到长程跳跃的单调准周期算子，在跳跃衰减速率与频率共振强度满足ρ>αβ时，免于Lyapunov指数地建立了均匀Anderson局域化，拓展了此类方法的适用范围。

Abstract: We study one-dimensional quasiperiodic operators with $α$-Hölder monotone potentials and long-range hopping satisfying $|T(x,y)|\leq \varepsilon e^{-ρ|x-y|}$. Under an explicit smallness condition on $\varepsilon$, we establish uniform Anderson localization for such operators in the regime $ρ>αβ$, where $β$ is the exponential strength of frequency resonances. Our proof extends the arithmetic localization strategy for monotone quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators by Jitomirskaya and Kachkovskiy to the long-range setting, without relying on the Lyapunov exponent.

</details>


### [144] [A Combinatorial Formula and a Hilbert Scheme Realization of a Rank-One Virasoro Irregular Conformal Block](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39786)
*Hajime Nagoya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对一般中心荷，给出了秩一 Virasoro 非正则共形块 (0,0,1) 型在非正则奇点处形式展开的组合公式与几何实现；在 c=1 时证明作者猜想，并用 skew-tableau 数与 Littlewood-Richardson 系数表达参数无关系数，证明其非负性与整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究秩一 Virasoro 非正则共形块在非正则奇点处的形式展开，特别是其参数无关系数的组合结构、非负性与整性，并在 c=1 情形下证明作者此前提出（未证实）的猜想。

Method: 将归一化共形块实现为与 C^2 上点 Hilbert 概型相关的局部等变 Hilbert-Fock 空间上的系数意义下矩阵元；在 c=1 时结合 screening 积分的扇形渐近与 Laguerre-Jack 求值；利用升次微分算子的流表达 screening 核，并通过单盒 Pieri 规则与 Okounkov-Olshanski 的移位 Jack 系数公式计算其指数的 Jack 系数。

Result: 对一般中心荷给出了 (0,0,1) 型非正则共形块形式展开的组合公式与几何实现；在 c=1 时证明了作者猜想，将参数无关系数用 skew-tableau 数与 Littlewood-Richardson 系数显式表达，并确立了这些系数的非负性与整性。

Conclusion: 论文通过几何与组合方法系统刻画了秩一 Virasoro 非正则共形块 (0,0,1) 型的形式展开，在 c=1 情形下完全解决了作者猜想，揭示了系数与对称函数（Jack 多项式）、表示论（Littlewood-Richardson）及组合（skew-tableau）之间的深刻联系，并证明了系数的正性与整性。

Abstract: For general central charge, we give a combinatorial formula and a geometric realization of the formal expansion of the rank-one Virasoro irregular conformal block of type $(0,0,1)$ at its irregular singular point. We realize the normalized block as a coefficientwise matrix element on the localized equivariant Hilbert--Fock space associated with Hilbert schemes of points on $\mathbb C^2$. At $c=1$, we prove the author's conjecture and express the parameter-independent coefficients in terms of skew-tableau numbers and Littlewood--Richardson coefficients, establishing their nonnegativity and integrality. The proof combines sectorial asymptotics of screening integrals with Laguerre--Jack evaluations. We express the screening kernel using the flow of a degree-raising differential operator and compute the Jack coefficients of its exponential from the one-box Pieri rule and the shifted Jack coefficient formula of Okounkov and Olshanski.

</details>


### [145] [An Egorov-type Theorem for Analytic Wick Observables in Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39806)
*Julien Malartre*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出解析 Wick 观测量在半经典参数 ε 下传播的精确 Egorov 型公式，该公式为超指数衰减系数的收敛展开，适用于含时二次哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Breteaux 针对多项式符号所建立的 Egorov 型结果推广到更一般的解析 Wick 观测量（超指数衰减系数），以覆盖如空间截断的 P(φ)₂ 模型和 Höegh-Krohn 模型变体等更广泛的量子场论模型。

Method: 在半经典参数 ε>0 下，对具有超指数衰减系数的解析 Wick 观测量，在含时二次哈密顿量作用下，构造精确的 Egorov 型公式，并将其表示为一个以 ε 为参数的收敛展开。

Result: 得到了精确的 Egorov 型公式，其形式为在 ε>0 下的收敛展开；成功将该公式推广适用于空间截断的 P(φ)₂ 模型，以及 Höegh-Krohn 模型的一种变体。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了 Breteaux 关于多项式符号的结果，证明了对更广泛的一类解析 Wick 观测量仍可获得收敛的精确 Egorov 型传播公式，并具体应用到两个量子场论模型中。

Abstract: We provide an exact Egorov-type formula, given by a convergent expansion in the semi-classical parameter $\varepsilon > 0$, for the propagation of a certain class of analytic Wick observables with super-exponentially decaying coefficients, under the action of a time-dependent quadratic Hamiltonian. This work generalizes a result established by S. Breteaux for polynomial symbols, and includes the spatially cutoff $P(\varphi)_2$ model, as well as a variant of the Höegh-Krohn model.

</details>


### [146] [Three-dimensional stress-gradient non-locality problem: Displacement field for an arbitrary and a conservative force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39888)
*Slađan Jelić,Dušan Zorica*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在无界域上解析求解了三维应力梯度非局部介质的静力学问题，考虑标量型和张量型两类Eringen应力梯度模型，将位移场分解为局部型与非局部型叠加，并针对任意体力及保守体力（重力）情形给出并图示了各模型的分析位移表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于获得三维应力梯度非局部介质在任意体力（特别是保守体力）作用下的位移场解析解，以明确不同标量型和张量型Eringen非局部模型对位移场的影响与差异。

Method: 在无界域上，建立由平衡方程、无限小应变以及标量型与张量型应力梯度Eringen三维模型构成的线性方程组。由于方程线性，将每个模型的解表示为各模型共有的局部型位移场与各模型非局部算子特有的非局部型位移场的叠加，并对三类标量型和五类张量型模型逐一求解。

Result: 针对任意体力，得到六种不同的解析位移场表达式：一种为某标量型模型特有，三种为个别张量型模型特有，其余两种表达式分别由一个标量型与一个张量型模型共有。在重力体力情形下，位移矢量场的图示呈现五种不同模型，因为两个张量型模型在体力保守时退化为相同结果。

Conclusion: 论文成功解析求解了三维应力梯度非局部静力学问题，揭示了不同Eringen模型在任意体力和重力情形下位移场的异同，为应力梯度非局部弹性理论提供了明确的分析解与模型分类依据。

Abstract: In order to obtain the displacement field, both generally in the case of an arbitrary body force and specifically in the case of a conservative body force, the mechanical problem of statics of three-dimensional stress-gradient non-local medium is analytically solved on unbounded domain by considering a system of equations consisting of an equilibrium equation, infinitesimal strain, and both scalar- and tensor-type stress-gradient Eringen three-dimensional models. Since the system of equations is linear, for each model the solution is obtained in the form of a superposition of the local-type displacement field, common to each model, and the non-local-type displacement field, specific to the non-locality operator of the model. Among the three scalar-type and five tensor-type considered models, six distinct analytical displacement field expressions are obtained for an arbitrary body force: one expression specific to a scalar-type model, three specific to individual tensor-type models, while each of the remaining two expressions are common to one scalar- and one tensor-type model. The displacement vector fields are graphically represented in the case of gravitational body force, yielding five different models, since two tensor-type models become the same when the force is conservative.

</details>


### [147] [Optimization Inverse Spectral Problems for Sturm--Liouville Operators with Potentials in $L^p(0,1)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40231)
*Nur Valeev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维 Sturm–Liouville 算子的优化逆谱问题(OISP)：在给定有限特征值序列下，寻找与先验势 $V_0$ 在 $L^p$ 距离意义下最接近的势 $V$。论文证明了等谱流形的正则性、OISP 解的存在性及最优势的显式解析表示，并讨论了唯一性、数值方法与实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆谱问题的传统研究多关注给定谱数据的可行势是否存在及如何重构，但现实中常需要在一族等谱势中选出与已知参考势最接近者（如噪声数据拟合、最优设计），因此需要在优化视角下研究逆谱问题，并将其纳入正则性、存在性、唯一性与可计算性的理论框架。

Method: 作者将问题建模为 $L^p$ 空间中的约束优化：在固定前 $m$ 个特征值 $\lambda_1^*<\dots<\lambda_m^*$ 的等谱流形上最小化 $\|V-V_0\|_{L^p}$。核心工具包括：(1) 等谱流形的正则性刻画；(2) 利用幂映射作用于平方特征函数的线性组合给出最优势的解析表示；(3) 统一的微分-代数方法处理平方特征函数，证明线性相关时势可表为它们的解析函数；(4) 对 $p=2$ 利用二阶条件证明非退化驻点的孤立性与全局极小点的有限性；对一般 $p\in(1,\infty)$ 用度量/泛函分析与 Baire 纲定理建立第一特征值的唯一性及一般情形的通用唯一性；(5) 构造对称势垒反例、冻结特征函数迭代与带谱数据延拓的 Newton–KKT 方法。

Result: 论文给出了等谱流形正则性定理、OISP 解的存在性及最优势的显式解析公式（幂映射作用于平方特征函数线性组合）。证明了平方特征函数线性相关时势为其解析函数；$p=2$ 时非退化驻点孤立、全局极小点有限；对 $1<p<\infty$，当 $\lambda_1^*\ge\lambda_1(V_0)$ 时第一特征值唯一，并在 $L^p(0,1)$ 中按 Baire 纲意义成立通用全局唯一性。并给出 $m=1,2$ 时存在至少两个全局极小点的对称势垒反例，及迭代法与 Newton–KKT 法的收敛保证边界。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Sturm–Liouville 优化逆谱问题建立了较完整的理论（正则性、存在性、显式解、唯一性）与数值算法框架，并指出其可应用于非线性边值问题以及声学/电磁谐振器的诊断与最优谱设计等实际问题。

Abstract: We study an optimization inverse spectral problem (OISP) for a one-dimensional Sturm--Liouville operator on a finite interval. The problem is to find a potential $V \in L^p(0,1)$ that realizes a prescribed finite set $λ_1^* < λ_2^* < \dots < λ_m^*$ and has the smallest $L^p(0,1)$-distance from an a priori given potential $V_0 \in L^p(0,1)$.
  We formulate and prove results on the regularity of the isospectral manifold, the existence of an OISP solution, and an explicit analytic representation of the optimal potential by means of a power map applied to a linear combination of squared eigenfunctions.
  Using a unified differential-algebraic approach to squared eigenfunctions, we show that whenever these squares are linearly dependent, the corresponding potential can be expressed in terms of them and is an analytic function.
  For $p=2$, we prove that nondegenerate stationary points are isolated and that the number of global minima is finite provided that each of them is nondegenerate. For every $1<p<\infty$, we establish uniqueness for the first eigenvalue when $λ_1^*\geλ_1(V_0)$ and generic global uniqueness in the sense of Baire category in $L^p(0,1)$.
  A separate section provides examples with at least two global minima for $m=1,2$ in the presence of a sufficiently high finite symmetric barrier. We describe an iteration with frozen eigenfunctions and a local Newton--KKT method with continuation in the spectral data, and state explicitly the limits of their guarantees.
  The final part discusses applications of OISP theory to nonlinear boundary value problems and to applied problems of diagnostics and optimal spectral design of acoustic and electromagnetic resonators.

</details>


### [148] [Three-dimensional QCA that are not stably circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40235)
*Bowen Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明三维手性 Z_p Clifford 量子元胞自动机（QCA）对所有奇素数 p 都不是有限深度量子电路，即使张量上任意辅助自由度并与平移复合后亦然。由此推出三维 QCA 模掉稳定化电路和平移的群不是有限生成的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前这类结论仅在 Clifford 范畴内成立；作者希望将其扩展到完整的量子电路范畴，并借助一个新的不变量的概念来处理可逆子代数，从而证明非有限生成性。

Method: 关键工具是引入可逆子代数的一个新不变量，并猜想它等于所承载任意子理论的总量子维度的平方（模去有理数的平方）。作者利用该不变量证明三维手性 Z_p Clifford QCA 即使并入辅助自由度、复合平移，仍非有限深度量子电路。

Result: 对每个奇素数 p，Haah 及 Sun–Yang–Wang–Tantivasadakarn–Chen 构造的三维手性 Z_p Clifford QCA 都不是有限深度量子电路（即便允许张量任意辅助自由度并与平移复合）。因此，三维 QCA 模掉稳定化电路和平移后的群不是有限生成的。

Conclusion: 通过新的可逆子代数不变量，作者将非有限深度性质的证明推广到 Clifford 范畴之外，并确立三维 QCA 商群的无限生成性，为该不变量与任意子理论总量子维度的关系提出猜想。

Abstract: We prove that the three-dimensional chiral $\Z_p$ Clifford quantum cellular automata of Haah and of Sun--Yang--Wang--Tantivasadakarn--Chen are, for every odd prime $p$, not finite-depth quantum circuits, even after tensoring with arbitrary ancillary degrees of freedom and composing with translations. Previously such statements were known only inside the Clifford category. As a consequence, the group of three-dimensional QCA modulo stabilized circuits and translations is not finitely generated. The key ingredient is a new invariant of invertible subalgebras, which we conjecture to be the square of the total quantum dimension of the hosted anyon theory, modulo rational squares.

</details>


### [149] [Symmetries and exact solutions of stochastic differential equations: the CIR model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40316)
*Miguel A. Rodríguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用随机微分方程的对称性，解释了经典Cox-Ingersoll-Ross利率过程中著名的临界参数关系，并通过对称性求出模型解，同时复现了近期一项关于随机微分方程对称性与其前向Kolmogorov方程关系研究的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cox-Ingersoll-Ross (CIR) 过程中存在一个广为人知的临界参数关系，但该关系的深层原因尚未得到充分阐明。作者希望通过随机微分方程的对称性视角来解释这一临界关系的来源。

Method: 研究CIR过程所满足的随机微分方程的对称性（李对称），利用该对称性求解模型；并将随机微分方程的对称性与其对应的前向Kolmogorov（Fokker-Planck）方程联系起来进行分析。

Result: 成功用对称性解释了CIR过程的临界参数关系，并通过对称性方法求得模型解；同时复现并印证了近期广义框架下关于随机微分方程对称性与前向Kolmogorov方程之间关系的结论。

Conclusion: CIR过程中的临界参数关系可由随机微分方程的对称性自然导出，对称性方法为理解该类利率模型的参数约束与求解提供了统一且有效的途径。

Abstract: It is well known that in the celebrated Cox-Ingersoll-Ross process for interest rates there exists a critical relation among the model parameters. In the present work, we explain this critical relation by studying the symmetries of the stochastic differential equation and compute the solution of the model using this symmetry. In this way we also recover results of a recent paper where this property has been studied within a broader context by analyzing the relationships among the symmetries of stochastic differential equations and their corresponding forward Kolmogorov equations.

</details>


### [150] [Twinor Theory: Field Equations from $(A)dS_4^{\mathbb C}$ Spin-Tractors: A Twin to Twistor Theory Modelled on $(A)dS_4^{\mathbb C}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40435)
*Chen-Hsu Chien*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Twinor理论，作为扭子理论的时空对应物，通过复化(A)dS_4上基于全纯约化Cartan几何来构建，给出统一的twinor场方程，并据此复现有质量Dirac–Fierz–Pauli系统与零质量方程，进而延伸到非线性引力与物质相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为扭子理论提供一个基于时空的对应框架，利用全纯约化Cartan几何统一处理质量、自旋、手征性与引力，在无需作用量的条件下刻画Einstein–Cartan动力学及物质耦合，并厘清相互作用高自旋场的一致性要求。

Method: 以复化(A)dS_4上的全纯约化Cartan几何为模型；由基本旋-张量丛定义Dirac twinors，其对称幂给出阶梯twinors，极端Lorentz分量定义手征twinors。引入一般twinor场方程 D Y = J；其相邻收缩与混合散度受源关系约束，对完全对称投影不加额外方程。在平坦无挠Lorentz背景下，施加质量归一化源及消失的附加收缩源与混合散度，得到有质量Dirac–Fierz–Pauli系统；无质量手征实现给出零静止质量方程。通过完全伴随曲率表述非线性引力，用能量-动量与自旋流指定Einstein–Cartan动力学；用伴随Bianchi恒等式约束源相容性，BGG型序列组织无挠无穷小形变；物质相互作用经耦合联络与收缩源关系进入，曲率相容由 D J = F_D Y 记录。

Result: 在平坦无挠背景下复现了有质量Dirac–Fierz–Pauli系统与零静止质量方程；在固定质量归一化内，给出正实数质量平方的现实条件为粒子尺度上的anti-de Sitter现实条件，且与宇宙学尺度和背景曲率无关；质量耦合相位因此在量子化前即存在于经典联络中。

Conclusion: Twinor理论为扭子理论提供了时空对应框架，统一产生有/无质量场方程及引力动力学；质量相位源于经典联络的anti-de Sitter现实条件；非线性引力与物质相互作用可通过联络与曲率约束一致地组织，两种手征性均被保留，并把高自旋相互作用的额外一致性要求与底层丛构造区分开来。

Abstract: Twinor theory is formulated as a spacetime-based counterpart to twistor theory using holomorphic reductive Cartan geometry modelled on complexified $(A)dS_4$. The fundamental spin-tractor bundle defines Dirac twinors, its symmetric powers define ladder twinors, and their extremal Lorentz components define chiral twinors. The general \textit{twinor field equation} is $D\mathbf Y=\mathbf J$. Its adjacent contractions and mixed divergence are constrained by source relations; no additional equation is imposed on its completely symmetric projection. On a flat torsion-free Lorentzian background, the adjacent equations with the mass-normalization source and vanishing additional contracted sources, together with vanishing mixed divergence, reproduce the massive \textit{Dirac--Fierz--Pauli system}; the massless chiral realization gives the zero-rest-mass equations. Within the fixed mass normalization, the reality condition yielding a positive real mass squared is the \textit{anti-de Sitter reality condition} on the particle scale, independently of the cosmological scale and background curvature. The mass-coupling phase is therefore present in the classical connection before quantization. Nonlinear gravity is formulated through the full adjoint curvature, with energy--momentum and spin currents specifying Einstein--Cartan dynamics without requiring an action. The adjoint Bianchi identity constrains source compatibility, while a BGG-type sequence organizes torsion-free infinitesimal deformations. Matter interactions enter through the coupled connection and contracted source relations, with curvature compatibility recorded by $D\mathbf J=\mathcal F_D\mathbf Y$. Both chiralities are retained, and the additional consistency requirements for interacting higher-spin fields are distinguished from the underlying bundle construction.

</details>


### [151] [Entropic Fluctuations in Quantum Statistical Mechanics: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40463)
*Tristan Benoist,Laurent Bruneau,Vojkan Jakšić,Aannalisa Panati,Claude-Alain Pillet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述并比较了将经典Evans–Searles（瞬态）与Gallavotti–Cohen（稳态）涨落定理推广到量子系统的多种途径，特别补全了此前缺失的、在量子动力系统代数框架下二次测量熵产生的Gallavotti–Cohen（稳态）涨落定理，并借助模理论、量子传递算符的谱共振等工具，以自旋-费米子模型为例阐明量子统计力学中熵涨落的本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Evans–Searles与Gallavotti–Cohen涨落定理刻画了系统在瞬态/稳态下熵产生率的普适统计特征，但将其推广到量子系统仍存在多种方案且存在空缺（尤其是代数框架下稳态Gallavotti–Cohen版本的缺失）。作者希望系统梳理、比较这些推广路径，并统一其数学结构，以深化对量子统计力学中熵涨落性质的理解。

Method: 本文以综述方式回顾并比较量子涨落定理的几种推广途径：朴素（naive）方法、二次测量（two-time measurement）、相空间收缩（phase space contraction）以及辅助态层析（ancilla state tomography）。重点讨论小系统与多个无限延展热库耦合的开放量子系统，并引入模理论（modular theory）将经典概念自然延拓到量子域。文中还讨论了正则熵涨落原理（principle of regular entropic fluctuations）的量子版本，将各类熵产生的统计性质与量子传递算符（quantum transfer operators）的谱共振联系起来，并以自旋-费米子模型这一非平衡统计力学范式模型进行应用验证。

Result: 作者补全了此前缺失的、在量子动力系统代数框架下基于二次测量熵产生的Gallavotti–Cohen（稳态）涨落定理推广；建立了模理论与量子涨落定理的关联，阐明了经典概念向量子域的延拓方式；给出了正则熵涨落原理的量子版本；证明了各种熵产生定义的统计性质可由量子传递算符的谱共振刻画，并在自旋-费米子模型上成功应用。

Conclusion: 这些结果为量子统计力学中熵涨落的本质提供了新的洞见：通过模理论可自然地将经典涨落定理推广到量子域，不同熵产生方案之间存在统一的结构联系，且其统计特征由量子传递算符的谱共振决定，从而为理解开放量子系统的非平衡涨落提供了坚实而统一的数学框架。

Abstract: The celebrated Evans--Searles, respectively Gallavotti--Cohen, fluctuation theorem concerns certain universal statistical features of the entropy production rate of a classical system in a transient, respectively steady, state. In this paper, we review and compare several possible extensions of these fluctuation theorems to quantum systems. We present, in an introductory way, the approach proposed in a recent series of works [Lett. Math. Phys. 114, 32 (2024): doi:10.1007/s11005-024-01777-0], [Rev. Math. Phys 38, 2461006 (2026): doi:10.1142/S0129055X24610063], [Commun. Math. Phys. 406, 201 (2025): 10.1007/s00220-025-05360-z] and [J. Math. Phys. 67, 022101 (2026): doi:10.1063/5.0311182] including a previously missing extension of the Gallavotti--Cohen (steady state) version of the FT to two-time measurement entropy production in the algebraic setting of quantum dynamical systems. We focus on the open quantum systems, where a small system interacts with several (infinitely extended) reservoirs, and discuss the following approaches: naive, two-time measurement, phase space contraction and ancilla state tomography. We review the connection with modular theory. The latter provides a way to extend the classical notions to the quantum domain. We discuss the quantum version of the principle of regular entropic fluctuations, introduced in the classical context in [Nonlinearity 24, 699 (2011): doi:10.1088/0951-7715/24/3/003]. Finally, we relate the statistical properties of these various notions of entropy production to spectral resonances of quantum transfer operators and apply this approach to a paradigmatic model of non-equilibrium statistical mechanics: the spin-fermion model. The results presented here shed a new light on the nature of entropic fluctuations in quantum statistical mechanics.

</details>


### [152] [Universal Critical Jump for Inverse-Square Ising Ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36131)
*Dyuman Bhattacharya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类平移不变铁磁伊辛链在临界点的严格关系 2β_c B m_c^2=1，该结论不依赖有限核或尾部收敛速率的限制，并将结果推广到连续时间的正斯蒂尔杰斯欧姆自旋-玻色子模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于研究具有长程、缓慢衰减相互作用（满足 n^2J_n→B>0）的铁磁伊辛链的临界行为，特别是刻画临界点处磁化强度的不连续跳变（jump）与临界乘积 2β_c B m_c^2 之间的严格关系，并突破以往结论对有限核结构及尾部收敛速率的限制。

Method: 作者通过对宏观界面能量的估计（bounding the energy of macroscopic interfaces）来排除临界乘积超过一的情况，从而证明 2β_c B m_c^2=1；同时证明尽管存在非零跳变，连通磁化率在临界点及其邻近有序区间发散，并进一步论证在临界耦合下，当外场趋于零时超额磁化强度比场的任意正幂衰减得更慢。最后将跳变关系推广到连续时间、正斯蒂尔杰斯类欧姆自旋-玻色子模型。

Result: 证明了 2β_c B m_c^2=1 在无有限核或尾部收敛速率限制下严格成立；连通磁化率在临界点及邻近有序区间均为无穷大；临界耦合处超额磁化强度的衰减速率慢于外场的任意正幂；并在连续时间正斯蒂尔杰斯欧姆自旋-玻色子模型中也证明了相应跳变关系。

Conclusion: 作者在极一般条件下严格确立了铁磁伊辛链临界点处跳变与临界乘积的普适关系，揭示了临界磁化率发散和超额磁化强度非幂律衰减等临界现象，并将其推广至连续时间量子模型，深化了对一类长程相互作用系统临界行为的理解。

Abstract: For every translation-invariant ferromagnetic Ising chain with $n^2J_n\to B>0$, we prove $2β_cBm_c^2=1$, without restrictions on the finite core or the tail convergence rate. The proof bounds the energy of macroscopic interfaces and rules them out if the critical product exceeds one. Despite the nonzero jump, connected susceptibility is infinite both at criticality and in a neighboring ordered interval. At the critical coupling, the excess magnetization decays more slowly than any positive power of the field as the field tends to zero. We also prove the jump relation in continuous time, for a positive-Stieltjes class of Ohmic spin-boson models.

</details>


### [153] [Generalized Dirac Equation in Metric-Affine Spacetimes: Consistency, Effective Mass, and Spinor Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23609)
*Muzaffer Adak,Ali Bagci,Caglar Pala,Ozcan Sert*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在含曲率、挠率和非度规性的四维度量-仿射时空中构造广义 Dirac 方程，旋量联络在 Clifford 代数完整基上展开含 8 个复耦合常数，并通过協变化与变分的一致性、厄米性与对标量/赝标量质量项的讨论，最终得到由耦合常数决定的有效质量与旋量曲率之间的代数联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义度量-仿射时空中 Dirac 场如何与曲率、挠率和非度规性耦合，厘清旋量联络中众多耦合常数之间的约束关系，并探讨非度规性/标架依赖项是否能在质量参数为零的情况下产生有效质量。

Method: 在 cl(1,3) Clifford 代数完整基上展开旋量联络（含 8 个复耦合常数），通过要求 Dirac 方程直接协变化与对应对称化 Lagrangian 变分结果一致，导出 8 个实一致性条件；在静态各向同性对称远平行（teleparallel）扇区中检验 Hamiltonian 对弯曲空间 Dirac 内积的厄米性；进一步推导广义旋量曲率的 Clifford 分解，并分析质量为零时有效质量与旋量曲率的关系。

Result: 16 个独立实参数被减至 8 个；单一自由 Dirac 场在常系数下得到一个有效质量 m_eff = sqrt((m-4b_3)^2 + 16b_4^2) 及一个可通过常手征场重定义移除的手征相位；即使 m=0 也仍可有非零有效质量。广义旋量曲率的 Clifford 分解表明，当仿射曲率、挠率、非度规性全消失时旋量曲率仍可非零；当 m=0 时，b_3^2+b_4^2 同时决定有效质量平方与残存旋量曲率。

Conclusion: 度量-仿射时空中标架依赖的耦合项引入标量与赝标量质量贡献，使得零裸质量下仍可产生有效质量；一致性条件将独立参数大幅缩减；有效质量平方与残存旋量曲率由同一组合 b_3^2+b_4^2 决定，建立了质量生成与旋量曲率之间的代数关联。

Abstract: We formulate a generalized Dirac equation in four-dimensional metric-affine spacetimes with curvature, torsion, and non-metricity. The spinor connection is expanded over the complete basis of the Clifford algebra $cl(1,3)$ and contains eight complex coupling constants. Requiring agreement between direct covariantization of the Dirac equation and variation of the corresponding symmetrized Lagrangian imposes eight real consistency conditions, reducing the number of independent real parameters from sixteen to eight. In the static isotropic symmetric teleparallel sector, formal Hermiticity of the Hamiltonian with respect to the curved-space Dirac inner product reproduces the corresponding restrictions on the non-metricity and coframe couplings. The coframe-dependent terms introduce scalar and pseudoscalar mass contributions that persist in Minkowski spacetime. For a single free Dirac field with constant coefficients, they yield one effective mass, $m_{\mathrm{eff}}=\sqrt{(m-4b_3)^2+16b_4^2}$, and a chiral phase removable by a constant chiral field redefinition. A nonzero effective mass is therefore possible even when the original mass parameter vanishes. We also derive the Clifford decomposition of the generalized spinor curvature and show that it can remain nonzero when affine curvature, torsion, and non-metricity all vanish. When $m=0$, the combination $b_3^2+b_4^2$ determines both the effective mass squared and the surviving spinor curvature, establishing an algebraic link between these contributions.

</details>

### [154] [High-Performance Nuclear Demagnetization Refrigerator Reaching 50 $μ$K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38622)
*Zhengchao Liu,Junyang Song,Bingbing Tong,Hiroki Ikegami*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文报告了一套基于标准湿式稀释制冷机和中型铜核级设计的核去磁制冷系统，可将温度降至50 μK，并能在100 μK下维持超过十天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期量子技术和量子材料进展迅速，深亚毫开尔文（约0.1 mK以下）温区可能为凝聚态物理带来新机遇，因此需要高效、实用的低温实验平台。

Method: 采用标准湿式稀释制冷机搭配中型铜核级构建核去磁系统，通过Pt NMR温度计测温，并进行数值分析评估铜核自旋系统的实际冷却温度。

Result: Pt NMR温度计测得的温度达到50 μK；制冷机可在100 μK下维持超过十天；即使仅预冷12小时，也能进入深亚毫开尔文温区并在100 μK下维持超过三天。数值分析表明铜核自旋系统被冷却至约18 μK，而温度计处的局部热漏将测得的温度限制在约50 μK。

Conclusion: 该系统具备高效、实用的深亚毫开尔文温区实验能力，可作为该温区凝聚态物理实验的实用平台。

Abstract: Research in the deep sub-mK regime around 0.1 mK may open new opportunities in condensed matter physics in light of recent rapid progress in quantum technologies and quantum materials. Here, we report the design and performance of a nuclear demagnetization system that provides efficient access to the deep sub-mK regime for a wide range of experiments, using a standard wet dilution refrigerator with a medium-sized copper nuclear stage. We show that the temperature measured by a Pt NMR thermometer reaches 50 $μ$K and that the refrigerator can maintain 100 $μ$K for more than ten days. Remarkably, even with only 12 hours of precooling, the refrigerator cools into the deep sub-mK regime and can maintain 100 $μ$K for more than three days, providing sufficient time for sample measurements. Numerical analyses indicate that the copper nuclear-spin system is cooled to about 18 $μ$K, whereas a local heat leak to the thermometer limits the thermometer temperature to about 50 $μ$K. These features establish the system as a practical platform for efficient experiments in the deep sub-mK regime.

</details>


### [155] [Quantum Hamiltonian time crystals from covariant quantum Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39886)
*Antti J. Niemi*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文通过施加固定谱与固定电荷期望的约束，在密度矩阵上最小化能量，构造了经典哈密顿时间晶体的量子对应物，并用对称协变的量子 Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert 方程将其选定为周期吸引子；以 Cu₃ 分子磁体为灵感的 chiral 自旋三聚体在无塞曼项条件下可实现该运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典哈密顿时间晶体是沿对称轨道以拉格朗日乘子设定的频率运动的受限能量极小值；作者希望将其概念推广到量子体系，构造满足固定谱与守恒荷约束的时间晶体态，并给出能自然收敛到这些态的耗散动力学。

Method: 在固定谱（本征值）与固定电荷期望值的约束下对密度矩阵最小化能量，得到量子时间晶体态；提出对称协变的量子 Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert（qLLG）方程作为动力学，其以这些态为周期吸引子，同时保持谱不变并由方程动力学地确定拉格朗日乘子；模型为受 Cu₃ 分子磁体启发的 chiral 自旋三聚体。

Result: 该构造产生以拉格朗日乘子确定的频率做周期运动的量子时间晶体态；qLLG 方程将它们选为周期吸引子，并在演化中保持密度矩阵谱不变、动力学地固定乘子；在无塞曼项、处于预先设定的磁化强度下，chiral 自旋三聚体能够实现这种时间晶体运动。

Conclusion: 通过能量最小化加固定谱与电荷期望约束，可将经典哈密顿时间晶体推广为量子版本；对称协变的 qLLG 耗散动力学能以周期吸引子的方式稳定这些非平衡时间晶体态，并在实际分子磁体系统（如 Cu₃ 三聚体）中可观测实现。

Abstract: Classical Hamiltonian time crystals are constrained energy minima that move along symmetry orbits at frequencies set by Lagrange multipliers. We construct quantum counterparts by minimizing the energy over density matrices with fixed spectrum and charge expectations. A symmetry-covariant quantum Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert equation selects them as periodic attractors, preserving the spectrum and fixing the multipliers dynamically. A chiral spin trimer motivated by Cu$_3$ molecular magnets realizes this motion at a prepared magnetization, without a Zeeman term.

</details>


### [156] [Quantum Metric Scaling and Its Exceptions in Classical Flat Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40066)
*Kin Hung Fung,K. T. Law,C. T. Chan*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文探讨经典局域共振质量-弹簧系统中的平带局域化，指出局域长度在平带附近遵循量子度量长度，但在精确平带频率处偏离该标度，且选择性质量无序可产生发散局域长度或极短拖尾，表明无序可选择超出量子度量预测的局域尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子度量长度被提出作为一维平带中局域长度尺度，但该标度关系在经典系统中是否成立及其例外情况尚不清楚。作者旨在研究经典平带（局域共振质量-弹簧系统）中量子度量标度及其失效情形，以阐明无序对局域尺度的额外选择作用。

Method: 在一维局域共振质量-弹簧系统构成的经典平带中，通过引入无序（尤其是选择性质量无序），考察局域长度随频率的变化，并将其与量子度量长度进行比较，重点检验平带附近与精确平带频率处的行为差异。

Result: 发现局域长度在平带附近遵循量子度量长度，但在精确平带频率处偏离该比例关系；选择性质量无序在不改变干净系统度量的情况下，可在平带频率附近产生发散局域长度或任意短的拖尾。

Conclusion: 经典平带系统的局域化在一般情形遵循量子度量标度，但存在例外；无序能够选择超出量子度量单独预测范围的局域尺度，说明仅凭量子度量不足以完整描述局域化行为。

Abstract: The quantum metric length has been proposed as the localization length scale in one-dimensional flat bands. Here, we study this quantum metric scaling and the exceptions to it in a classical flat band formed by a locally resonant mass--spring system. We find that the localization length follows the quantum metric length near the flat band but departs from this proportionality at the exact flat-band frequency. Selective mass disorder also produces divergent localization lengths or arbitrarily short tails near the flat-band frequency without changing the clean metric. These exceptions show that disorder can select localization scales beyond those predicted by the quantum metric alone.

</details>

### [157] [Tuning La0.7Sr0.3MnO3 magnetic anisotropy via interfacial coupling in homometallic trilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37736)
*Nunzia Coppola,Giovanni Vinai,Alksandr Yu Petrov,Chiara Sacco,Hafiz Sami Ur Rehman,Sofia Scarnicci,Lucia Capogna,Cinzia Di Giorgio,Carmela Aruta,Luigi Maritato,Alice Galdi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过磁光克尔效应（MOKE）与磁电阻测量，探讨了SrMnO3/La0.7Sr0.3MnO3/SrMnO3三明治结构中由界面结构重建、轨道重建以及铁磁/反铁磁竞争所引发的复杂磁性行为，证明可通过调控中间铁磁层厚度来调节界面作用，从而实现对磁性质的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索异质结构界面处结构重建、轨道重建及铁磁/反铁磁竞争的相互作用，以验证利用异质结构作为有效策略来调控和控制复杂磁性行为（如自旋重取向、交换偏置、各向异性磁电阻）的可行性。

Method: 制备嵌入两层同金属反铁磁SrMnO3（SMO）之间的铁磁La0.7Sr0.3MnO3（LSMO）三明治结构，选取两种不同LSMO厚度以改变界面作用；结合磁光克尔效应（MOKE）与磁电阻（MR）测量，系统表征其磁各向异性、矫顽场、交换偏置及各向异性磁电阻随温度的变化。

Result: 当温度低于125 K时，界面交换作用开始主导衬底诱导的磁各向异性，导致LSMO易磁化轴相对室温方向发生自旋重取向；界面处超交换与直接交换作用竞争形成高度受挫的界面自旋玻璃态，使矫顽场和交换偏置增强约20倍；界面效应所引发的磁晶各向异性改变导致各向异性磁电阻响应发生变化，并主导低温行为。

Conclusion: 通过改变LSMO层厚度来调控界面作用，可有效调节三明治异质结构的磁性质；界面交换作用与磁各向异性的竞争以及受挫界面自旋玻璃态是产生自旋重取向、交换偏置增强和各向异性磁电阻变化的关键机制，证实同金属锰氧化物异质结构是调控磁性行为的有效平台。

Abstract: In this work we explore the complex magnetic behaviour of SrMnO3/La0.7Sr0.3MnO3/SrMnO3 trilayers combining magneto-optic Kerr effect (MOKE) and magneto-resistance measurements. The interplay of structural and orbital reconstruction and the competition of ferromagnetism/antiferromagnetism at the interfaces is responsible of a variety of complex magnetic properties, highlighting the use of heterostructures as an effective strategy to tune and control magnetic behaviour. We demonstrate the possibility to tune the magnetic properties of a ferromagnetic La0.7Sr0.3MnO3 (LSMO) layer, embedded in two homometallic antiferromagnetic layers (SrMnO3, SMO), by properly varying the role of interfaces using two different LSMO thickness values. Spin reorientation of the LSMO magnetic easy axis away from its room temperature orientation was observed below 125 K, due to the predominance of exchange interaction at the interface over the substrate-induced magnetic anisotropy, in agreement with established models for homometallic manganite heterostructures. The competition of super-exchange and exchange interactions at the interface results in a highly frustrated interfacial spin glass state, resulting in 20-fold enhancement of the coercive field and exchange bias. The modification of the magnetocrystalline anisotropy driven by the interfacial effects leads to a change in the anisotropic magneto-resistance response of the system, which dominates the low temperature behaviour.

</details>


### [158] [MoireKP: An Automated Workflow for Symmetry-Constrained Moiré Continuum Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37760)
*Yan Zhang,Hanqi Pi,Wangqian Miao,Zhong Fang,Hongming Weng,Xi Dai,B. Andrei Bernevig,Quansheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: MoireKP 是一个自动化工作流，可从局域轨道的从头算哈密顿量出发构建紧致的连续模型，用于高效研究摩尔材料，并在三种代表性摩尔体系中以小于 3.0 meV 的误差复现目标能带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理哈密顿量包含研究摩尔材料所需的全部电子信息，但其规模庞大，导致后续计算成本高昂，因此需要一种自动化的方法将其压缩为紧致且保留波函数信息的连续模型。

Method: 作者提出 MoireKP 自动化工作流：首先构造按谷分辨的截断原子平面波（TAPW）哈密顿量，选取低能基组，并把晶体对称性操作投影到该基组中；由这些表示确定对称性允许的哈密顿量项，再通过将低能有效哈密顿量投影到相应算符基上求得各项系数。模型维度、局域化参考态和谐波截断既可自动选取，也可由用户指定。

Result: 将该工作流应用于 AA 堆叠的扭转双层 MoTe₂、AB 堆叠的扭转双层 ZrS₂ 以及 A–AB 扭转三层 MoTe₂，覆盖 K、M 和 Γ 谷。所有模型在目标能量窗口内、沿高对称 k 路径均能以小于 3.0 meV 的误差复现 TAPW 能带。

Conclusion: MoireKP 提供了一种基于哈密顿量的紧致化方案，在保留所选低能子空间内波函数信息的同时，能以高精度和低计算成本支撑摩尔材料的后续研究。

Abstract: First-principles Hamiltonians contain the electronic information needed to study moiré materials, but their size makes subsequent calculations costly. We present MoireKP, an automated workflow for constructing compact continuum models from localized-orbital ab initio Hamiltonians. The workflow constructs a valley-resolved truncated atomic-plane-wave (TAPW) Hamiltonian, selects a low-energy basis, and projects the crystal-symmetry operations into this basis. These representations determine the symmetry-allowed Hamiltonian terms, whose coefficients are obtained by projecting the low-energy effective Hamiltonian onto the corresponding operator basis. The model dimension, localized reference states, and harmonic cutoff can be selected automatically or specified by the user. This Hamiltonian-based construction retains wave-function information within the selected low-energy subspace. We apply the workflow to AA-stacked twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$, AB-stacked twisted bilayer ZrS$_2$, and A--AB twisted trilayer MoTe$_2$, covering K, M, and $Γ$ valleys. All models reproduce TAPW band energies within 3.0 meV in the target windows along high-symmetry $k$ paths.

</details>


### [159] [Planar energetics do not determine twin persistence in nanocrystalline aluminium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37925)
*Yue Wu,Yuan Tian,Haoyu Wan,Deng Pan,Mingwei Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文证明平面能量不足以决定面心立方金属中形变孪晶是否持续存在；两个机器学习势在相同加载下分别预测 64% 与 14% 的孪晶保留率，差异源于畸变分位错芯内沿拖拽平移方向的作用力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面能量（planar energetics）是面心立方金属孪生能力的标准描述量，也是原子间势的常规验证目标；但文章质疑它们能否真正决定孪晶能否持续存在，旨在揭示决定孪晶保留与否的真正机制。

Method: 采用两个机器学习原子间势，它们再现相同的零载荷滑移曲线和紧密相近的分层能量面；从共同的分位错前驱体出发，在相同加载条件下预测孪晶覆盖保留率；并通过分析畸变分位错芯内的原子力来解释分歧。

Result: 两个势分别预测 64% 和 14% 的保留孪晶覆盖率；分歧可追溯至畸变分位错芯内沿拖拽平移方向、会移除外层堆垛层错的作用力。平面能量设定了容限：外层堆垛层错使中间面剪切的增量势垒降低超过一半，但孪晶完成与层错移除间仅相差 10–21 mJ m⁻²。

Conclusion: 孪晶是否持续存在并非由平面能量决定，而是由局部牵引力相对于全滑移平分线的取向，以及来自晶界网络的分位错滑移前端的到达顺序所决定。

Abstract: Planar energetics are the standard descriptors of twinnability in face-centred cubic metals and the usual validation targets for interatomic potentials. Here we show that they do not determine whether deformation twins persist in nanocrystalline aluminium. Two machine-learned potentials reproducing the same zero-load slip curves and closely spaced layered energy surfaces predict, from a common separated-fault precursor under identical loading, retained twin coverage of 64\% and 14\%. The divergence traces to forces within distorted partial cores acting along the trailing translation that would remove an outer fault. Planar energetics do set the margin: an outer fault more than halves the incremental barrier for middle-plane shear, yet twin completion and fault removal remain separated by only 10--21 mJ m\textsuperscript{-2}. Whether an assembled twin persists is decided instead by the orientation of local traction relative to the full-slip bisector and by the arrival order of partial-slip fronts from the grain-boundary network.

</details>


### [160] [Analytical framework for interpreting thermoreflectance measurements of nanoline gratings under finite spot illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37957)
*Abhishek Pathak,Seungwon Park,Shubham Deshmukh,Alan J. H. McGaughey,Jonathan A. Malen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于频域热反射（FDTR）的技术，用于测量表面刻有金属纳米线光栅的材料堆叠的有效热学性质，并通过解析模型与实验对比完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前FDTR方法难以准确考虑有限激光光斑与纳米线光栅结构对热输运的影响，限制了其用于测量随加热器尺寸变化的有效热导率，进而无法有效探测声子平均自由程（MFP）相关的热输运贡献。

Method: 作者建立了考虑泵浦/探测激光高斯强度分布下的有限光斑半径以及纳米线光栅尺寸的解析热扩散模型，用于预测FDTR相位响应；并在a-SiO₂和c-蓝宝石基底上制备宽度100–1000 nm的Au纳米线光栅进行测量验证。

Result: 模型与测量在不同激光光斑半径和纳米线宽度下均表现出极好的一致性；获得的c-蓝宝石体热导率以及Au-Cr/a-SiO₂、Au-Cr/c-蓝宝石界面的热边界导与作者先前测量及文献值相当。由于这两种基底的声子MFP较短，在全部纳米线宽度范围内热输运仍处于扩散区。

Conclusion: 该模型经过验证，可作为将此方法推广到测量随加热器尺寸变化的有效热导率的稳健基线，尤其适用于非扩散热输运区可用以探测声子MFP相关热导率贡献的材料。

Abstract: A frequency-domain thermoreflectance (FDTR)-based technique is presented to measure the effective thermal properties of a material stack whose surface is patterned with metallic nanoline gratings that are heated and probed by lasers. An analytical heat diffusion model is derived for predicting the FDTR phase response that incorporates a finite laser spot radius with a Gaussian intensity profile for the pump and probe lasers, as well as the dimensions of the nanoline grating. The model is validated by comparing with measurements of Au nanoline gratings, with widths ranging from 100 to 1000 nm, fabricated on a-SiO$_2$ and $c$-sapphire substrates. Due to the short phonon mean free paths (MFPs) in these substrates, thermal transport remains in the diffusive regime for the whole range of nanoline widths. The model and the measurement show excellent agreement across different laser spot radii and nanoline widths, with a bulk thermal conductivity value of $c$-sapphire and the thermal boundary conductances at the Au-Cr/a-SiO$_2$ and Au-Cr/$c$-sapphire interfaces comparable to our prior measurements and the literature. The validated model will serve as a robust baseline for extending this method to measure effective thermal conductivity as a function of the heater size. This approach is particularly relevant for materials where non-diffusive thermal transport regimes enable probing phonon MFP dependent contributions to the thermal conductivity.

</details>


### [161] [Electronic-morphological decoupling in mixed-dimensional halide perovskite heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37964)
*Mahmoud Samadpour,Alireza Fathi Beiraghvandi,Kazem Meraji,Tristan Sachsenweger Ballantyne,Fuxiang Ji,Mahdi Mohammadi,Nasim Kabir,Wolfgang Tress*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了低维Ruddlesden-Popper覆盖层对3D钙钛矿表面形貌与光电性质的影响，发现尽管覆盖层增加表面粗糙度，却不会增加电子不均匀性，反而提升发光、抑制非辐射复合，并在老化过程中保持光电均匀性，证明形貌不规则与电子均匀性可以解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观念认为表面形貌是薄膜质量的指标，更光滑的薄膜通常具有更均匀的光电性质。作者旨在挑战这一假设，探究表面形貌演化是否必然导致电子异质性增加，为开发耐用高性能钙钛矿光伏器件提供新设计原则。

Method: 作者在3D钙钛矿上引入由空间位阻型5-叔丁基菲啶鎓阳离子衍生的低维Ruddlesden-Popper覆盖层，结合统计原子力显微镜（AFM）分析与共聚焦、空间分辨光致发光（PL）光谱，将表面形貌与微尺度光电性质相关联；并通过加速老化实验对比原始薄膜与覆盖异质结构的光电时空均匀性。

Result: 尽管覆盖层增加了表面粗糙度和高度不对称性，但并未增加电子不均匀性；相反，异质结构表现出增强的PL、降低的非辐射复合，以及相比原始3D薄膜更均匀的辐射准费米能级分裂（QFLS）空间分布。在加速老化下，原始薄膜出现显著的发射与QFLS空间波动，而覆盖异质结构基本保持光电均匀性。

Conclusion: 低维覆盖层抑制了老化诱导的光电无序，表明尽管存在形貌不规则，仍可维持电子均匀性。这种形貌与电子性质之间的解耦为开发耐用、高性能的钙钛矿光伏器件提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Surface morphology is widely considered an indicator of thin-film quality, with smoother films generally expected to exhibit more uniform optoelectronic properties. Here, we demonstrate that substantial surface evolution does not necessarily lead to increased electronic heterogeneity in mixed-dimensional perovskite heterostructures. We investigate how low-dimensional Ruddlesden-Popper capping layers derived from the sterically hindered 5-tert-butyl-phenanthridinium cation affect the spatial distributions of photoluminescence (PL) characteristics and quasi-Fermi level splitting (QFLS) in 3D perovskites. By combining statistical atomic force microscopy (AFM) analysis with confocal, spatially resolved PL spectroscopy, we correlate surface topography with microscale optoelectronic properties.
  Although capping increases surface roughness and height asymmetry, it does not increase electronic inhomogeneity. Instead, the heterostructures exhibit enhanced PL, reduced non-radiative recombination, and improved spatial uniformity of radiative QFLS compared with pristine 3D films. Under accelerated aging, this contrast becomes more pronounced: pristine films develop substantial spatial fluctuations in emission and QFLS, whereas capped heterostructures largely preserve their optoelectronic homogeneity. These findings indicate that the low-dimensional capping layer suppresses aging-induced optoelectronic disorder, demonstrating that electronic uniformity can be maintained despite morphological irregularity. This decoupling provides a design principle for developing durable, high-performance perovskite photovoltaics.

</details>


### [162] [Thermal laser evaporation of superconducting Nb films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38070)
*Yifei Yan,David S. Catherall,Finley B. Donachie,Dorsa Najafipanah,Austin J. Minnich*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用热激光蒸发（TLE）技术在蓝宝石衬底上沉积铌薄膜，所得薄膜的电学和超导性能接近块体材料，表明TLE是一种适用于难熔元素的高质量物理气相沉积方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLE作为一种新兴物理气相沉积技术，能以连续波激光蒸发最难熔的元素，但其沉积薄膜的质量（尤其是超导难熔元素）尚需系统评估。作者旨在通过沉积并表征铌薄膜，验证TLE在难熔材料制备中的可行性与质量水平。

Method: 使用自建的TLE系统，配备1070 nm连续波光纤激光器，以533 W激光功率（聚焦到约500 μm直径）蒸发铌靶，在c面蓝宝石上以0.19 Å/s的速率沉积铌薄膜，并对薄膜的厚度、粗糙度、室温电阻率、剩余电阻率比（RRR）及超导转变温度进行表征。

Result: 所得薄膜厚度为22.47±0.05 nm，均方根粗糙度为0.23±0.02 nm，室温电阻率为21.60±0.05 μΩ·cm，RRR为5.71。超导转变温度Tc为9.24 K，仅比块体值低10 mK，转变宽度29 mK。在不加热衬底的条件下，这些性能达到或超过了其他技术沉积的薄膜。

Conclusion: 该工作证实TLE是一种适用于难熔元素及化合物的多功能物理气相沉积方法，并支持了TLE在该领域日益形成的共识。

Abstract: Thermal laser evaporation (TLE) is an emerging physical vapor deposition (PVD) technique that uses continuous-wave lasers to evaporate even the most refractory elements. The deposition and characterization of superconducting refractory elements are of interest to assess the quality of films deposited by TLE. Here, we report the deposition of niobium (Nb) on c-plane sapphire using a home-built TLE system equipped with a 1070 nm continuous-wave fiber laser. A deposition rate of 0.19 Å/s was achieved with 533 W of laser power focused to a diameter of approximately 500 $μ$m on a Nb target. A film with a thickness of $22.47 \pm 0.05$ nm and a root-mean-square (RMS) roughness of $0.23 \pm 0.02$ nm exhibited a room-temperature resistivity of $21.60 \pm 0.05$ $μΩ\mathrm{cm}$ and a residual-resistivity ratio (RRR) of 5.71. The superconducting transition temperature, $T_\mathrm{c}$, of the film was measured as 9.24 K, only 10 mK below the bulk value, with a transition width of 29 mK. Among films deposited without substrate heating, the electrical and superconducting properties of the present films meet or exceed those deposited by other techniques. This work contributes to the emerging consensus regarding TLE as a versatile PVD method for refractory elements and compounds.

</details>


### [163] [Emergent higher-order optical nonlinearity in self-assembled polar supertextures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38138)
*Daniel A. Bustamante Lopez,Deepankar Sri Gyan,Xiaojiang Li,Vladimir A. Stoica,Sujit Das,Peter Meisenheimer,Akash Surampalli,Mohit Tanwani,Xianghan Xu,Aiden Ross,Long-Qing Chen,Sang-Wook Cheong,Lane W. Martin,Ramamoorthy Ramesh,Venkatraman Gopalan,Wanzheng Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了在自组装极性斯格明子中、中红外激发下观测到巨幅且超快的二次谐波产生（SHG），其瞬态强度呈现源自高阶非线性的独特功率依赖，并揭示了极性超晶格中普遍存在的强高阶光学非线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巨光学非线性能够实现先进材料调控，并将奇异光-物质现象桥接至下一代光子学，但表现出超越二阶的强高阶光学非线性的材料通常十分罕见，因此亟需寻找这类材料体系。

Method: 作者在自组装极性斯格明子中利用中红外激发测量瞬态SHG，分析其幂律依赖；并采用相场模拟展示平衡态下各极性织构内部强且周期性的局域介电常数变化，确认中红外激发下极性斯格明子中的快速极化振荡。

Result: 实验观测到巨幅、超快的SHG，瞬态SHG强度呈现源自高阶非线性的独特功率依赖；相场模拟证实局域介电常数的强周期性调制可满足epsilon-near-zero（近零介电常数）条件，从而无需光子工程即可解锁显著增强的非线性光学响应。

Conclusion: 自组装极性拓扑结构具有强本征光学非线性，可作为量子信息处理与高速光通信的平台。

Abstract: Giant optical nonlinearities enable advanced materials control and bridge exotic light-matter phenomena to next-generation photonics. However, materials exhibiting strong higher-order optical nonlinearity beyond the second order are typically rare. Here, we report the discovery of a giant and ultrafast second-harmonic generation (SHG) in self-assembled polar skyrmions under mid-infrared excitations. The transient SHG intensity exhibits a distinctive power dependence originating from higher-order nonlinearity. More generally, we show that large higher-order nonlinearity emerges across diverse domain structures in polar superlattices. Phase-field simulations demonstrate a strong and periodic local permittivity variation within each polar texture at equilibrium, and further confirm the fast polarization oscillation in polar skyrmions with mid-infrared excitations. This intense local permittivity modulation can satisfy epsilon-near-zero conditions, unlocking dramatically enhanced nonlinear optical responses without photonic engineering. Consequently, the self-assembled polar topologies with strong inherent optical nonlinearity could establish a platform for quantum information processing and high-speed optical communications.

</details>


### [164] [Crystoria: A Large-Scale Crystal Dataset Linking Thermodynamic and Dynamical Stability for Materials AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38195)
*Ke-Xin Pang,Ze-Feng Gao,Yan Gao,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出Crystoria，一个包含264万晶体结构的大型数据集，首次将动力学稳定性评估扩展到百万级规模，其中67.68%的结构被判定为动力学稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模晶体数据库极大推动了材料发现，但动力学稳定性评估因计算成本远高于常规结构-能量计算，导致其在规模上严重滞后，难以与快速扩张的晶体结构空间相匹配，形成关键数据缺口，制约了材料研究与机器学习的发展。

Method: 系统地筛选低能与亚稳态无机晶体，标准化其结构身份，并将机器学习加速的结构弛豫与基于声子的动力学筛选相结合，构建大规模动力学稳定性数据集。

Result: 构建了包含2,649,640个晶体结构的数据集，覆盖89种元素、230个空间群中的219个、含1-48个原子的原胞；其中1,793,353个（67.68%）被判定为动力学稳定，且动力学稳定性在化学与晶体学空间中呈现显著差异。

Conclusion: Crystoria将动力学稳定性提升到百万级晶体结构规模，为材料AI提供了物理可靠的基础，并支持对动力学晶体景观的系统性探索。

Abstract: Large-scale crystal databases have transformed materials discovery by making millions of structures and thermodynamic properties accessible to materials informatics. Yet dynamical stability remains sparsely characterized at a comparable scale because its evaluation is substantially more computationally demanding than conventional structure and energy calculations. This mismatch creates a critical gap between the rapidly expanding crystal-structure space and the physical information available for materials research and machine learning. Here we introduce Crystoria, a large-scale crystal dataset designed to bring dynamical stability to the scale of modern materials data. We construct Crystoria by systematically curating low-energy and metastable inorganic crystals, standardizing their structural identities, and combining machine-learning-accelerated structural relaxation with phonon-based dynamical screening. The resulting dataset contains 2,649,640 crystal structures, spanning 89 elements, 219 of the 230 space groups, and primitive cells containing 1-48 atoms. Among them, 1,793,353 structures (67.68%) are classified as dynamically stable, revealing substantial variation in dynamical stability across chemical and crystallographic space. By bringing dynamical stability to the scale of millions of crystal structures, Crystoria provides a physically grounded foundation for materials artificial intelligence (AI) and enables systematic exploration of the dynamical crystal landscape.

</details>


### [165] [Finite-Temperature Magnetism and Longitudinal Spin Transport in Compensated Half-Metal Ferrimagnets with Near-Zero Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38302)
*Jiahao Shentu,Jie Li,Jianting Dong,Meng Zhu,Chen Wang,Qing Zhang,Yizhuo Song,Wenqi Sun,Jia Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Heusler型全补偿半金属亚铁磁体Mn3Al的电子结构、有限温度磁性及自旋输运性质，发现其为零温下100%自旋极化的半金属全补偿亚铁磁体，在300 K仍保持近补偿亚铁磁序，且Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al磁隧道结在300 K仍具有高达232%的巨隧穿磁电阻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全补偿亚铁磁体（FCF）因零温磁矩近乎消失、电子结构完全自旋极化及超快自旋动力学，是下一代自旋电子器件的极具前景的材料平台，可减少杂散磁场并降低开关电流，实现高速低功耗器件。然而其有限温度磁性与自旋输运性质仍需系统研究，以验证其在室温下的实用性。

Method: 作者采用密度泛函理论（DFT）结合蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）模拟，系统研究Heusler型全补偿半金属亚铁磁体Mn3Al的电子结构、有限温度磁性与自旋依赖输运性质；并以Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al磁隧道结（MTJ）为对象计算隧穿磁电阻。

Result: 研究表明Mn3Al为半金属全补偿亚铁磁体，零温下具有100%自旋极化与完全补偿的磁化强度；其在300 K时净磁化强度低于11.6 emu/cc，证实室温下近补偿亚铁磁序的稳健性；Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al磁隧道结在0 K呈现巨隧穿磁电阻，且在300 K仍高达232%。

Conclusion: Mn3Al及其相关半金属全补偿亚铁磁体是面向磁电阻应用的自旋电子器件的优秀候选材料，为高速、低功耗自旋电子器件的发展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Fully compensated ferrimagnets, characterized by nearly vanishing magnetic moments at zero temperature, perfect spin-polarized electronic structures, and ultrafast spin dynamics, represent a highly promising materials platform for next-generation spintronic devices. By minimizing stray magnetic fields and reducing switching currents, FCFs enable a viable path toward high-speed, low-power spintronic devices. In this work, we present a systematic investigation of the electronic structure, finite-temperature magnetism, and spin-dependent transport properties of the Heusler-type fully compensated half-metallic ferrimagnet Mn3Al in magnetic tunnel junctions. We demonstrate that Mn3Al is a half-metallic FCF with 100% spin polarization and fully compensated magnetization at 0 K. Combining density functional theory with Monte Carlo simulations, we characterize its finite-temperature magnetic properties and show that Mn3Al exhibits a net magnetization below 11.6 emu/cc at 300 K, confirming the robustness of its nearly compensated ferrimagnetic order at room temperature. Furthermore, we predict that the Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al MTJ yields a giant tunneling magnetoresistance effect at 0 K, which remains as high as 232% at 300 K. Our results establish Mn3Al and related half-metallic FCFs as excellent candidates for magnetoresistance-based spintronic applications.

</details>


### [166] [First-Principles Identification of Higher-Order Configurational Energy Terms and Their Magnetic-State Dependence in Lithium-Rich Oxyfluorides: From QUBO to HUBO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38321)
*Joonho Bae*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用自旋极化DFT识别出锂富氧氟化物中QUBO模型无法表示的三阶非加和构型能贡献，并在两种无序岩盐正极材料上测试了QUBO到HUBO的扩展，发现三次HUBO显著降低拟合误差，同时揭示了磁态依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QUBO模型只能表示二阶（两体）加和能量，无法捕捉无序岩盐材料构型能中实际存在的三阶非加和贡献；为更准确建模构型能量，需要验证向高阶（HUBO）扩展的必要性与有效性。

Method: 采用自旋极化密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，构建保持成分的DFT能量图；对比QUBO与三次HUBO模型的拟合效果；在Li₂MnO₂F（LMOF）和Li₂Mn₀.₅Ti₀.₅O₂F（LMTiOF）两种无序岩盐正极材料上测试；通过直接DFT能量差验证三阶非加和性，并比较不同磁性初始化以考察其对电子态收敛的依赖。

Result: 三次HUBO能够捕捉QUBO缺失的非加和贡献，并在两种材料上大幅降低拟合误差；直接DFT能量差证实了三阶非加和性；LMTiOF中的该项贡献强烈依赖于收敛后的电子态（磁态）；提取的项描述的是保持成分的交换变量间的有效耦合，而非唯一确定的微观三原子相互作用。

Conclusion: 在对构型能量建模时应包含高阶项（HUBO），并在构建DFT能量图时需考虑磁态依赖性；所提取的高阶项应理解为有效耦合而非唯一识别的微观多体相互作用。

Abstract: In this work, we use spin-polarized density functional theory (DFT) to identify substantial third-order, nonadditive configurational energy contributions in lithium-rich oxyfluorides that quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) models cannot represent. We test the extension from QUBO to higher-order unconstrained binary optimization (HUBO) in two disordered-rocksalt cathode materials, Li$_2$MnO$_2$F (LMOF) and Li$_2$Mn$_{0.5}$Ti$_{0.5}$O$_2$F (LMTiOF), using composition-preserving DFT energy maps. Using fixed-geometry energy maps, we show that cubic HUBO captures nonadditive contributions absent from QUBO models and substantially reduces fitting errors in both materials. Direct DFT energy differences confirm third-order nonadditivity, while comparisons of magnetic initializations reveal a strong dependence of the LMTiOF contribution on the converged electronic state. These results support the inclusion of higher-order terms when modeling configurational energetics and highlight the need to account for magnetic-state dependence in constructing DFT energy maps. The extracted terms describe effective couplings among composition-preserving exchange variables, rather than uniquely identified microscopic three-atom interactions.

</details>


### [167] [Odd Elasticity in Strain-Gradient Continua: Anisotropy Classification and Non-Hermitian Elastodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40581)
*Payam Poorsolhjouy*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文将奇弹性(odd elasticity)框架推广至三维应变梯度形式，基于点群对称性系统推导出四、五、六阶奇弹性刚度张量，并通过动态虚功原理建立平衡方程与广义声学张量，揭示非互易模量的存在条件与非厄米弹性动力学的各向异性要求。
